## Act I / 第一幕

===Original===
**Aubrey Tanqueray’s** Chambers in the Albany﻿—a richly and tastefully decorated room, elegantly and luxuriously furnished: on the right a large pair of doors opening into another room, on the left at the further end of the room a small door leading to a bedchamber. A circular table is laid for a dinner for four persons which has now reached the stage of dessert and coffee. Everything in the apartment suggests wealth and refinement. The fire is burning brightly.
***Aubrey Tanqueray**, **Misquith**, and **Jayne** are seated at the dinner table. **Aubrey** is forty-two, handsome, winning in manner, his speech and bearing retaining some of the qualities of young-manhood. **Misquith** is about forty-seven, genial and portly. **Jayne** is a year or two **Misquith’s** senior; soft-speaking and precise﻿—in appearance a type of the prosperous town physician. **Morse**, **Aubrey’s** servant, places a little cabinet of cigars and the spirit-lamp on the table beside **Aubrey**, and goes out.*
Misquith
Aubrey, it is a pleasant yet dreadful fact to contemplate, but it’s nearly fifteen years since I first dined with you. You lodged in Piccadilly in those days, over a hat-shop. Jayne, I met you at that dinner, and Cayley Drummle.

===Chinese===
**奥布里·坦奎雷（Aubrey Tanqueray）**在奥尔巴尼（the Albany）的寓所——一间装饰华丽而雅致的房间，陈设精美考究：右侧一双大门通往另一个房间，左侧房间深处有一扇小门通向卧室。一张圆桌上摆着四人晚宴的餐具，此时已进行到上甜点与咖啡的阶段。寓所中的一切无不显示出富足与品位。炉火正旺。
***奥布里·坦奎雷**、**米斯基斯（Misquith）**与**杰恩（Jayne）**围坐在餐桌旁。**奥布里**四十二岁，相貌英俊，风度翩翩，言谈举止间仍保留着几分青年人的气质。**米斯基斯**四十七岁上下，和蔼富态。**杰恩**比**米斯基斯**年长一两岁，说话轻声细语、字斟句酌——外表正是那种事业兴旺的城市医生的典型。**奥布里**的仆人**莫尔斯（Morse）**把一只小雪茄匣和酒精灯放到**奥布里**手边的桌上，随即退出。*
米斯基斯
奥布里，细想起来这既叫人愉快又叫人害怕：距我第一次同你共进晚餐，已经快十五年了。你那时住在皮卡迪利（Piccadilly），一家帽子铺的楼上。杰恩，我就是在那次晚宴上认识你的，还有凯利·德鲁姆尔（Cayley Drummle）。

===Original===
Jayne
Yes, yes. What a pity it is that Cayley isn’t here tonight.
Aubrey
Confound the old gossip! His empty chair has been staring us in the face all through dinner. I ought to have told Morse to take it away.

===Chinese===
杰恩
是啊，是啊。可惜凯利今晚不在。
奥布里
这个老碎嘴子真该死！他那把空椅子整顿饭都瞪着我们。我早该叫莫尔斯把它搬走的。

===Original===
Misquith
Odd, his sending no excuse.
Aubrey
I’ll walk round to his lodgings later on and ask after him.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
奇怪，他连个缘由都没派人来说一声。
奥布里
待会儿我散步去他住处看看他。

===Original===
Misquith
I’ll go with you.
Jayne
So will I.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
我跟你一起去。
杰恩
我也去。

===Original===
Aubrey
*Opening the cigar-cabinet.* Doctor, it’s useless to tempt you, I know. Frank﻿—***Misquith** and **Aubrey** smoke.* I particularly wished Cayley Drummle to be one of us tonight. You two fellows and Cayley are my closest, my best friends﻿—
Misquith
My dear Aubrey!

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（打开雪茄匣。）*医生，我知道，馋您是白搭。弗兰克（Frank）——***米斯基斯**与**奥布里**抽起雪茄。*我特别希望凯利·德鲁姆尔今晚也在我们中间。你们两位和凯利是我最亲近、最好的朋友——
米斯基斯
我亲爱的奥布里！

===Original===
Jayne
I rejoice to hear you say so.
Aubrey
And I wanted to see the three of you round this table. You can’t guess the reason.

===Chinese===
杰恩
听你这么说我很高兴。
奥布里
我还想让你们三位围坐在这张桌旁。你们猜不到缘故吧。

===Original===
Misquith
You desired to give us a most excellent dinner.
Jayne
Obviously.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
你想请我们吃一顿极其丰盛的晚宴。
杰恩
这还用说。

===Original===
Aubrey
*Hesitatingly.* Well﻿—I﻿—*Glancing at the clock*﻿—Cayley won’t turn up now.
Jayne
H’m, hardly.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（迟疑地。）*呃——我——*（瞟了一眼时钟）*——凯利这会儿是不会来了。
杰恩
唔，来不了了。

===Original===
Aubrey
Then you two shall hear it. Doctor, Frank, this is the last time we are to meet in these rooms.
Jayne
The last time?

===Chinese===
奥布里
那就讲给你们两位听吧。医生，弗兰克，这是我们在这些房间里最后一次相聚了。
杰恩
最后一次？

===Original===
Misquith
You’re going to leave the Albany?
Aubrey
Yes. You’ve heard me speak of a house I built in the country years ago, haven’t you?

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
你要搬出奥尔巴尼了？
奥布里
是的。我多年前在乡下盖了一幢房子，你们听我说起过吧？

===Original===
Misquith
In Surrey.
Aubrey
Well, when my wife died I cleared out of that house and let it. I think of trying the place again.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
在萨里（Surrey）的那幢。
奥布里
唉，我妻子去世后，我就把那房子腾空了，租了出去。如今我想再回去住住看。

===Original===
Misquith
But you’ll go raving mad if ever you find yourself down there alone.
Aubrey
Ah, but I shan’t be alone, and that’s what I wanted to tell you. I’m going to be married.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
可你一个人待在那儿，会发疯的。
奥布里
啊，我不会是一个人，这正是我要告诉你们的。我要结婚了。

===Original===
Jayne
Going to be married?
Misquith
Married?

===Chinese===
杰恩
要结婚了？
米斯基斯
结婚？

===Original===
Aubrey
Yes﻿—tomorrow.
Jayne
Tomorrow?

===Chinese===
奥布里
是的——就在明天。
杰恩
明天？

===Original===
Misquith
You take my breath away! My dear fellow, I﻿—I﻿—of course, I congratulate you.
Jayne
And﻿—and so do I﻿—heartily.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
你把我惊得说不出话了！亲爱的伙计，我——我——当然，恭喜你。
杰恩
我——我也恭喜——衷心地恭喜。

===Original===
Aubrey
Thanks﻿—thanks.
*There is a moment or two of embarrassment.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
谢谢——多谢。
*（众人都尴尬了一两分钟。）*

===Original===
Misquith
Er﻿—ah﻿—this is an excellent cigar.
Jayne
Ah﻿—um﻿—your coffee is remarkable.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
呃——啊——这雪茄真不错。
杰恩
啊——唔——你这咖啡好极了。

===Original===
Aubrey
Look here; I daresay you two old friends think this treatment very strange, very unkind. So I want you to understand me. You know a marriage often cools friendships. What’s the usual course of things? A man’s engagement is given out, he is congratulated, complimented upon his choice; the church is filled with troops of friends, and he goes away happily to a chorus of good wishes. He comes back, sets up house in town or country, and thinks to resume the old associations, the old companionships. My dear Frank, my dear good doctor, it’s very seldom that it can be done. Generally, a worm has begun to eat its way into those hearty, unreserved, prenuptial friendships; a damnable constraint sets in and acts like a wasting disease; and so, believe me, in nine cases out of ten a man’s marriage severs for him more close ties than it forms.
Misquith
Well, my dear Aubrey, I earnestly hope﻿—

===Chinese===
奥布里
听我说；你们两位老朋友八成觉得我这样行事很古怪、很无情。所以我要你们理解我。你们知道，婚姻常常使友谊降温。通常的情形是怎样的？一个男人订了婚，人人道贺，夸他挑得好；教堂里挤满了成群的朋友，他在一片祝福声中欢欢喜喜地离开。他回来了，在城里或乡下安了家，想着恢复旧日的交往、旧日的情谊。我亲爱的弗兰克，我亲爱的好医生，这很少能办到。通常，一条蛀虫已经开始啃噬那些婚前的热诚、不设防的友谊；一种可憎的拘谨悄然而至，像痼疾一样侵蚀一切；所以，相信我，十有八九，一个人的婚姻为他切断的亲密纽带，比它缔结的还要多。
米斯基斯
唔，亲爱的奥布里，我衷心希望——

===Original===
Aubrey
I know what you’re going to say, Frank. I hope so, too. In the meantime let’s face dangers. I’ve reminded you of the *usual* course of things, but my marriage isn’t even the conventional sort of marriage likely to satisfy society. Now, Cayley’s a bachelor, but you two men have wives. By the by, my love to Mrs. Misquith and to Mrs. Jayne when you get home﻿—don’t forget that. Well, your wives may not﻿—like﻿—the lady I’m going to marry.
Jayne
Aubrey, forgive me for suggesting that the lady you are going to marry may not like our wives﻿—mine at least; I beg your pardon, Frank.

===Chinese===
奥布里
我知道你要说什么，弗兰克。我也如此希望。眼下咱们还是直面危险吧。我提醒了你们*通常*的情形，可我这门婚事，甚至连那种能让社会满意的常规婚姻都不是。凯利是单身汉，可你们两位都有太太。对了，回家后代我向米斯基斯夫人和杰恩夫人问好——可别忘了。呃，你们的太太未必——喜欢——我要娶的这位女士。
杰恩
奥布里，恕我多嘴，你要娶的那位女士也未必喜欢我们的太太——至少未必喜欢我太太；请你原谅，弗兰克。

===Original===
Aubrey
Quite so; then I must go the way my wife goes.
Misquith
Come, come, pray don’t let us anticipate that either side will be called upon to make such a sacrifice.

===Chinese===
奥布里
正是如此；那么我必须随我太太走的方向走。
米斯基斯
得了，得了，咱们可别预先就认定哪一方得作出这样的牺牲。

===Original===
Aubrey
Yes, yes, let us anticipate it. And let us make up our minds to have no slow bleeding-to-death of our friendship. We’ll end a pleasant chapter here tonight, and after tonight start afresh. When my wife and I settle down at Willowmere it’s possible that we shall all come together. But if this isn’t to be, for Heaven’s sake let us recognise that it is simply because it can’t be, and not wear hypocritical faces and suffer and be wretched. Doctor, Frank﻿—*Holding out his hands, one to **Misquith**, the other to **Jayne***﻿—good luck to all of us!
Misquith
But﻿—but﻿—do I understand we are to ask nothing? Not even the lady’s name, Aubrey?

===Chinese===
奥布里
不，不，咱们就这么预先认定了吧。也让我们下定决心，别让我们的友谊慢慢失血而死。今晚我们在此了结愉快的一章，过了今晚重新开始。等我太太和我在威洛米尔（Willowmere）安顿下来，我们大家或许还会重聚。倘若不能，看在老天的份上，就让我们承认这仅仅是因为不可能，而不去戴假面具、受罪、痛苦。医生，弗兰克——*（伸出双手，一只给**米斯基斯**，一只给**杰恩**）*——祝我们大家好运！
米斯基斯
可是——可是——我理解得对吗，我们什么都不问？连那位女士的姓名也不问，奥布里？

===Original===
Aubrey
The lady, my dear Frank, belongs to the next chapter, and in that her name is Mrs. Aubrey Tanqueray.
Jayne
*Raising his coffee cup.* Then, in an old-fashioned way, I propose a toast. Aubrey, Frank, I give you “The Next Chapter!”
*They drink the toast, saying, “The Next Chapter!”*

===Chinese===
奥布里
那位女士，我亲爱的弗兰克，属于下一章，而在那一章里，她的名字是奥布里·坦奎雷夫人。
杰恩
*（举起咖啡杯。）*那么，按老派规矩，我提议干一杯。奥布里，弗兰克，我为“下一章”干杯！
*（众人举杯同饮，齐道：“下一章！”）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Doctor, find a comfortable chair; Frank, you too. As we’re going to turn out by-and-by, let me scribble a couple of notes now while I think of them.
**Misquith** and **Jayne**
Certainly﻿—yes, yes.

===Chinese===
奥布里
医生，找把舒服的椅子坐；弗兰克，你也是。反正待会儿我们要出门，趁现在想着，让我草草写几张便条。
**米斯基斯**与**杰恩**
当然——好，好。

===Original===
Aubrey
It might slip my memory when I get back.
***Aubrey** sits at a writing-table at the other end of the room, and writes.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
回来时我可能就忘了。
***奥布里**坐到房间另一头的写字台前写起来。*

===Original===
Jayne
*To **Misquith**, in a whisper.* Frank﻿—***Misquith** quietly leaves his chair and sits nearer to **Jayne**.* What is all this? Simply a morbid crank of Aubrey’s with regard to ante-nuptial acquaintances?
Misquith
H’m! Did you notice *one* expression he used?

===Chinese===
杰恩
*（对**米斯基斯**耳语。）*弗兰克——***米斯基斯**悄悄离开座位，坐到**杰恩**近旁。*这到底是怎么回事？不过是奥布里对婚前故交的一种病态怪念头？
米斯基斯
唔！你注意到他用过的*一个*说法吗？

===Original===
Jayne
Let me think﻿—
Misquith
“My marriage is not even the conventional sort of marriage likely to satisfy society.”

===Chinese===
杰恩
让我想想——
米斯基斯
“我这门婚事，甚至连那种能让社会满意的常规婚姻都不是。”

===Original===
Jayne
Bless me, yes! What does that suggest?
Misquith
That he has a particular rather than a general reason for anticipating estrangement from his friends, I’m afraid.

===Chinese===
杰恩
天哪，正是！这说明什么？
米斯基斯
恐怕说明，他预料与朋友们疏远，是有特殊缘故的，不是泛泛之论。

===Original===
Jayne
A horrible *mésalliance*! A dairymaid who has given him a glass of milk during a day’s hunting, or a little anemic shopgirl! Frank, I’m utterly wretched!
Misquith
My dear Jayne, speaking in absolute confidence, I have never been more profoundly depressed in my life.
***Morse** enters.*

===Chinese===
杰恩
一桩可怕的*门不当户不对的婚姻*！一个打猎时递过他一杯牛奶的挤奶女工，或者一个面色苍白的小店员！弗兰克，我难受极了！
米斯基斯
亲爱的杰恩，绝对私下里说，我这辈子从没这么沮丧过。
***莫尔斯**上。*

===Original===
Morse
*Announcing.* Mr. Drummle.
***Cayley Drummle** enters briskly. He is a neat little man of about five-and-forty, in manner bright, airy, debonair, but with an undercurrent of seriousness.*
***Morse** retires.*

===Chinese===
莫尔斯
*（通报。）*德鲁姆尔先生到。
***凯利·德鲁姆尔**轻快地上。他是个约莫四十五岁的整洁小个子男人，举止明快洒脱、温文和悦，骨子里却透着一股严肃。*
***莫尔斯**退下。*

===Original===
Drummle
I’m in disgrace; nobody realises that more thoroughly than I do. Where’s my host?
Aubrey
*Who has risen.* Cayley.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
我丢尽脸了；这一点谁也不会比我认识得更透彻。我的主人在哪儿？
奥布里
*（已起身。）*凯利。

===Original===
Drummle
*Shaking hands with him.* Don’t speak to me till I have tendered my explanation. A harsh word from anybody would unman me.
***Misquith** and **Jayne** shake hands with **Drummle**.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（与他握手。）*在我作出解释之前，谁都别跟我说话。随便谁一句重话都会叫我垮掉的。
***米斯基斯**和**杰恩**与**德鲁姆尔**握手。*

===Original===
Aubrey
Have you dined?
Drummle
No﻿—unless you call a bit of fish, a cutlet, and a pancake dining.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你用过晚餐了吗？
德鲁姆尔
没有——除非一点鱼、一片肉排、一张薄煎饼也算晚餐。

===Original===
Aubrey
Cayley, this is disgraceful.
Jayne
Fish, a cutlet, and a pancake will require a great deal of explanation.

===Chinese===
奥布里
凯利，这可太不像话了。
杰恩
鱼、肉排加薄煎饼，可需要好好解释一番了。

===Original===
Misquith
Especially the pancake. My dear friend, your case looks miserably weak.
Drummle
Hear me! hear me!

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
尤其是那张薄煎饼。亲爱的朋友，你这情形看来糟糕得很哪。
德鲁姆尔
听我说！听我说！

===Original===
Jayne
Now then!
Misquith
Come!

===Chinese===
杰恩
说吧！
米斯基斯
快说！

===Original===
Aubrey
Well!
Drummle
It so happens that tonight I was exceptionally early in dressing for dinner.

===Chinese===
奥布里
好啦！
德鲁姆尔
碰巧今晚我换晚餐礼服换得出奇地早。

===Original===
Misquith
For which dinner﻿—the fish and cutlet?
Drummle
For *this* dinner, of course﻿—really, Frank! At a quarter to eight, in fact, I found myself trimming my nails, with ten minutes to spare. Just then enter my man with a note﻿—would I hasten, as fast as cab could carry me, to old Lady Orreyed in Bruton Street?﻿—“sad trouble.” Now, recollect, please, I had ten minutes on my hands, old Lady Orreyed was a very dear friend of my mother’s, and was in some distress.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
为哪顿晚餐——鱼和肉排那顿？
德鲁姆尔
当然是为*这顿*晚餐——真是的，弗兰克！八点差一刻的时候，我发现自己已经在修指甲，还富余十分钟。恰在此时我的仆人拿着一张便条进来——问我能否以马车所能及的速度，火速赶往布鲁顿街（Bruton Street）的奥雷耶德老夫人（old Lady Orreyed）处？——“有伤心事。”你们想想，我手头正闲着十分钟，奥雷耶德老夫人是我母亲的一位至交，又正伤心难过。

===Original===
Aubrey
Cayley, come to the fish and cutlet?
**Misquith** and **Jayne**
Yes, yes, and the pancake!

===Chinese===
奥布里
凯利，说回鱼和肉排吧？
**米斯基斯**与**杰恩**
对，对，还有薄煎饼！

===Original===
Drummle
Upon my word! Well, the scene in Bruton Street beggars description; the women servants looked scared, the men drunk; and there was poor old Lady Orreyed on the floor of her boudoir like Queen Bess among her pillows.
Aubrey
What’s the matter?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
我以名誉担保！唉，布鲁顿街那场面简直没法形容；女仆们满面惊惶，男仆们醉醺醺的；可怜的奥雷耶德老夫人倒在闺房的地上，活像拥着枕头的一代女王贝丝（Queen Bess）。
奥布里
出了什么事？

===Original===
Drummle
*To everybody.* You know George Orreyed?
Misquith
Yes.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（对众人。）*你们认识乔治·奥雷耶德（George Orreyed）吧？
米斯基斯
认识。

===Original===
Jayne
I’ve met him.
Drummle
Well, he’s a thing of the past.

===Chinese===
杰恩
见过。
德鲁姆尔
唉，他已经是个过去式了。

===Original===
Aubrey
Not dead!
Drummle
Certainly, in the worst sense. He’s married Mabel Hervey.

===Chinese===
奥布里
不是去世了吧！
德鲁姆尔
千真万确，而且是最糟意义上的。他娶了梅布尔·赫维（Mabel Hervey）。

===Original===
Misquith
What!
Drummle
It’s true﻿—this morning. The poor mother showed me his letter﻿—a dozen curt words, and some of those ill-spelt.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
什么！
德鲁姆尔
是真的——就在今天早上。可怜的母亲把他那封信拿给我看——寥寥十来个字，还有几个拼错的。

===Original===
Misquith
*Walking up to the fireplace.* I’m very sorry.
Jayne
Pardon my ignorance﻿—who *was* Mabel Hervey?

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
*（走到壁炉前。）*我很难过。
杰恩
恕我孤陋寡闻——梅布尔·赫维*究竟*是什么人？

===Original===
Drummle
You don’t﻿—? Oh, of course not. Miss Hervey﻿—Lady Orreyed, as she now is﻿—was a lady who would have been, perhaps has been, described in the reports of the Police or the Divorce Court as an actress. Had she belonged to a lower stratum of our advanced civilisation she would, in the event of judicial inquiry, have defined her calling with equal justification as that of a dressmaker. To do her justice, she is a type of a class which is immortal. Physically, by the strange caprice of creation, curiously beautiful; mentally, she lacks even the strength of deliberate viciousness. Paint her portrait, it would symbolise a creature perfectly patrician; lance a vein of her superbly-modelled arm, you would get the poorest *vin ordinaire*! Her affections, emotions, impulses, her very existence﻿—a burlesque! Flaxen, five-and-twenty, and feebly frolicsome; anybody’s, in less gentle society I should say everybody’s, property! That, doctor, was Miss Hervey who is the new Lady Orreyed. Dost thou like the picture?
Misquith
Very good, Cayley! Bravo!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
你们不——？哦，当然不知道。赫维小姐——如今是奥雷耶德夫人了——是这样一位女士：倘若出现在警局或离婚法庭的报道里，多半会被、或许曾经被描述为“女演员”。倘若她出身于我们这个先进文明的更低阶层，一旦司法调查起来，她同样有理由把自己的行当说成是裁缝。说句公道话，她是那类不朽阶层中的一个典型。论外貌，造化出于奇诡的任性，竟出落得异乎寻常地美；论心智，她连蓄意为恶的力气都没有。给她画一幅肖像，那会象征一个十足的贵妇；在她那线条绝美的手臂上划开一条血管，流出来的却是最劣等的*家常餐酒*！她的情感、情绪、冲动，乃至她的整个存在——一场滑稽戏！一头金发，二十五岁，有气无力地轻佻快活；在温文些的人群里是人人可得之物，在粗野些的人群里——恕我直言——是人人已得之物！医生，这位就是已成为新任奥雷耶德夫人的赫维小姐。您喜欢这幅画像吗？
米斯基斯
精彩，凯利！好极了！

===Original===
Aubrey
*Laying his hand on **Drummle’s** shoulder.* You’d scarcely believe it, Jayne, but none of us really know anything about this lady, our gay young friend here, I suspect, least of all.
Drummle
Aubrey, I applaud your chivalry.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（把手搭在**德鲁姆尔**肩上。）*你几乎不会相信，杰恩，可我们当中谁也并不真正了解这位女士，我猜，我们这位快活的年轻朋友了解得最少。
德鲁姆尔
奥布里，我赞赏你的骑士精神。

===Original===
Aubrey
And perhaps you’ll let me finish a couple of letters which Frank and Jayne have given me leave to write. *Returning to the writing-table.* Ring for what you want, like a good fellow!
***Aubrey** resumes his writing.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
也许你还能容我把几封信写完，弗兰克和杰恩已经准了我这个假。*（回到写字台前。）*想要什么就自己摇铃，好伙计！
***奥布里**继续写信。*

===Original===
Misquith
*To **Drummle**.* Still, the fish and cutlet remain unexplained.
Drummle
Oh, the poor old woman was so weak that I insisted upon her taking some food, and felt there was nothing for it but to sit down opposite her. The fool! the blackguard!

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
*（对**德鲁姆尔**。）*话说回来，鱼和肉排还没解释呢。
德鲁姆尔
哦，可怜的老太太身子太虚弱，我执意让她吃点东西，觉得没法子，只好在她对面坐下来陪她吃。那个蠢货！那个恶棍！

===Original===
Misquith
Poor Orreyed! Well, he’s gone under for a time.
Drummle
For a time! My dear Frank, I tell you he has absolutely ceased to be. ***Aubrey**, who has been writing busily, turns his head towards the speakers and listens. His lips are set, and there is a frown upon his face.* For all practical purposes you may regard him as the late George Orreyed. Tomorrow the very characteristics of his speech, as we remember them, will have become obsolete.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
可怜的奥雷耶德！唉，他算是沉下去一阵子了。
德鲁姆尔
一阵子！亲爱的弗兰克，我告诉你，他是彻彻底底地不复存在了。***奥布里**一直忙着写信，此时转过头来听他们说话。他双唇紧闭，眉头皱起。*就一切实际意义而言，你们可以把他当作已故的乔治·奥雷耶德。到了明天，他言谈中那些我们记忆里的独有特征，就将作废了。

===Original===
Jayne
But surely, in the course of years, he and his wife will outlive﻿—
Drummle
No, no, doctor, don’t try to upset one of my settled beliefs. You may dive into many waters, but there is *one* social Dead Sea﻿—!

===Chinese===
杰恩
可是年深日久，他和他太太总会挺过来的——
德鲁姆尔
不，不，医生，别妄想推翻我一条定见。你可以潜入许多水域，可是有一条社交上的死海——！

===Original===
Jayne
Perhaps you’re right.
Drummle
Right! Good God! I wish you could prove me otherwise! Why, for years I’ve been sitting, and watching and waiting.

===Chinese===
杰恩
也许你是对的。
德鲁姆尔
对！老天爷！我倒希望你能证明我错了！咳，多少年来，我就一直坐着，观望，等待。

===Original===
Misquith
You’re in form tonight, Cayley. May we ask where you’ve been in the habit of squandering your useful leisure?
Drummle
Where? On the shore of that same sea.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
你今晚状态好啊，凯利。能否请问，你一向惯于在什么地方挥霍你那有益的闲暇？
德鲁姆尔
在哪儿？就在那同一片海的海岸上。

===Original===
Misquith
And, pray, what have you been waiting for?
Drummle
For some of my best friends *to come up*. ***Aubrey** utters a half-stifled exclamation of impatience; then he hurriedly gathers up his papers from the writing-table. The three men turn to him.* Eh?

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
那么，请问，你一直在等什么？
德鲁姆尔
等我的一些最好的朋友*浮上来*。***奥布里**发出一声半压住的不耐烦的惊呼；随即匆匆把纸张从写字台上收起。三个男人都转向他。*嗯？

===Original===
Aubrey
Oh, I﻿—I’ll finish my letters in the other room if you’ll excuse me for five minutes. Tell Cayley the news.
*He goes out.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
哦，我——你们若容我离开五分钟，我到另一间房把信写完。把消息告诉凯利吧。
*（他下。）*

===Original===
Drummle
*Hurrying to the door.* My dear fellow, my jabbering has disturbed you! I’ll never talk again as long as I live!
Misquith
Close the door, Cayley.
***Drummle** shuts the door.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（赶到门边。）*亲爱的伙计，我的胡扯搅了你了！我只要活着就再也不说话了！
米斯基斯
把门关上，凯利。
***德鲁姆尔**关上门。*

===Original===
Jayne
Cayley﻿—
Drummle
*Advancing to the dinner table.* A smoke, a smoke, or I perish!
*Selects a cigar from the little cabinet.*

===Chinese===
杰恩
凯利——
德鲁姆尔
*（走向餐桌。）*抽口烟，抽口烟，不然我要死了！
*（从小雪茄匣里挑了一支雪茄。）*

===Original===
Jayne
Cayley, marriages are in the air.
Drummle
Are they? Discover the bacillus, doctor, and destroy it.

===Chinese===
杰恩
凯利，如今满城都在办婚事。
德鲁姆尔
是吗？医生，找出那病菌来，灭了它。

===Original===
Jayne
I mean, among our friends.
Drummle
Oh, Nugent Warrinder’s engagement to Lady Alice Tring. I’ve heard of that. They’re not to be married till the spring.

===Chinese===
杰恩
我是说，在我们的朋友中间。
德鲁姆尔
哦，纽金特·沃林德（Nugent Warrinder）和爱丽丝·特林小姐（Lady Alice Tring）订婚的事。我听说了。他们要到春天才结婚。

===Original===
Jayne
Another marriage that concerns us a little takes place tomorrow.
Drummle
Whose marriage?

===Chinese===
杰恩
还有一桩跟我们略有点关系的婚事，就在明天。
德鲁姆尔
谁的婚事？

===Original===
Jayne
Aubrey’s.
Drummle
Aub﻿—! *Looking towards **Misquith**.* Is it a joke?

===Chinese===
杰恩
奥布里的。
德鲁姆尔
奥——！*（朝**米斯基斯**望去。）*这是玩笑吧？

===Original===
Misquith
No.
Drummle
*Looking from **Misquith** to **Jayne**.* To whom?

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
不是。
德鲁姆尔
*（目光从**米斯基斯**移向**杰恩**。）*娶的是谁？

===Original===
Misquith
He doesn’t tell us.
Jayne
We three were asked here tonight to receive the announcement. Aubrey has some theory that marriage is likely to alienate a man from his friends, and it seems to me he has taken the precaution to wish us goodbye.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
他不肯说。
杰恩
我们三个今晚被请到这里，就是为了听这个宣布。奥布里有个理论，说婚姻很可能使一个男人疏远朋友；依我看，他是预先提防，来向我们道别的。

===Original===
Misquith
No, no.
Jayne
Practically, surely.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
不，不。
杰恩
可实际上分明就是。

===Original===
Drummle
*Thoughtfully.* Marriage in general, does he mean, or this marriage?
Jayne
That’s the point. Frank says﻿—

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（沉思地。）*他指的是泛泛的婚姻，还是这一桩？
杰恩
要害正在这里。弗兰克说——

===Original===
Misquith
No, no, no; I feared it suggested﻿—
Jayne
Well, well. *To **Drummle**.* What do you think of it?

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
不，不，不；我怕这话意味着——
杰恩
得了，得了。*（对**德鲁姆尔**。）*你怎么看？

===Original===
Drummle
*After a slight pause.* Is there a light there? *Lighting his cigar.* He﻿—wraps the lady﻿—in mystery﻿—you say?
Misquith
Most modestly.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（略停片刻。）*那儿有火吗？*（点雪茄。）*你是说——他把那位女士——裹在——迷雾里？
米斯基斯
裹得严严实实。

===Original===
Drummle
Aubrey’s﻿—not﻿—a very﻿—young man.
Jayne
Forty-three.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
奥布里——不算——是个很——年轻的人了。
杰恩
四十三了。

===Original===
Drummle
Ah! *L’age critique!*
Misquith
A dangerous age﻿—yes, yes.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
啊！*正是危急之龄！*
米斯基斯
危险的年纪——是啊，是啊。

===Original===
Drummle
When you two fellows go home, do you mind leaving me behind here?
Misquith
Not at all.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
你们两位回家的时候，把我一个人留在这儿，介意吗？
米斯基斯
一点也不。

===Original===
Jayne
By all means.
Drummle
All right. *Anxiously.* Deuce take it, the man’s second marriage mustn’t be another mistake!
*With his head bent he walks up to the fireplace.*

===Chinese===
杰恩
完全可以。
德鲁姆尔
好吧。*（焦虑地。）*见鬼，这个人的第二次婚姻，可千万别再错一遭！
*（他低着头走向壁炉。）*

===Original===
Jayne
You knew him in his short married life, Cayley. Terribly unsatisfactory, wasn’t it?
Drummle
Well﻿—*Looking at the door.* I quite closed that door?

===Chinese===
杰恩
他那段短暂的婚姻生活你是了解的，凯利。糟糕得很，是吧？
德鲁姆尔
呃——*（望着门。）*我把那扇门关严实了吗？

===Original===
Misquith
Yes.
*Settles himself on the sofa; **Jayne** is seated in an armchair.*

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
关了。
*（在沙发上安顿下来；**杰恩**坐在扶手椅里。）*

===Original===
Drummle
*Smoking, with his back to the fire.* He married a Miss Herriott; that was in the year eighteen﻿—confound dates﻿—twenty years ago. She was a lovely creature﻿—by Jove, she was; by religion a Roman Catholic. She was one of your cold sort, you know﻿—all marble arms and black velvet. I remember her with painful distinctness as the only woman who ever made me nervous.
Misquith
Ha, ha!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（抽着烟，背朝炉火。）*他娶的是一位赫里奥特小姐（Miss Herriott）；那是在十八——去他的年份——二十年前。她是个美人——天哪，确实是；宗教上是罗马天主教徒。她是那种冷冰冰的人物，你们知道——满身大理石般的手臂和黑丝绒。我记得她记得扎扎实实、想起来都不舒服——她是唯一一个让我紧张过的女人。
米斯基斯
哈，哈！

===Original===
Drummle
He loved her﻿—to distraction, as they say. Jupiter, how fervently that poor devil courted her! But I don’t believe she allowed him even to squeeze her fingers. She *was* an iceberg! As for kissing, the mere contact would have given him chapped lips. However, he married her and took her away, the latter greatly to my relief.
Jayne
Abroad, you mean?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
他爱她——爱得神魂颠倒，如人们所说。天神在上，那可怜虫追求她追求得多热烈啊！可我相信，她连让他捏一捏手指都不曾允许。她*就是*一座冰山！至于亲吻，单是碰一下就够他嘴唇皲裂的。不过他还是娶了她，把她带走了，后一桩事让我大大松了口气。
杰恩
你是说，去了国外？

===Original===
Drummle
Eh? Yes. I imagine he gratified her by renting a villa in Lapland, but I don’t know. After a while they returned, and then I saw how woefully Aubrey had miscalculated results.
Jayne
Miscalculated﻿—?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
嗯？是的。我猜想他在拉普兰（Lapland）租了幢别墅，算是遂了她的愿，不过我不清楚。过了一阵他们回来了，那时我才看出，奥布里把结果估量得多么可悲地离谱。
杰恩
估量错了——？

===Original===
Drummle
He had reckoned, poor wretch, that in the early days of marriage she would thaw. But she didn’t. I used to picture him closing his doors and making up the fire in the hope of seeing her features relax. Bless her, the thaw never set in! I believe she kept a thermometer in her stays and always registered ten degrees below zero. However, in time a child came﻿—a daughter.
Jayne
Didn’t that﻿—?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
这个可怜虫原先盘算着，结了婚，过不了多久她就会消融。可她没有。我常常想象他关紧门窗、把炉火拨旺，指望看见她眉目舒展。老天保佑她，解冻始终没有到来！我相信她在束胸里藏着温度计，永远指着零下十度。不过，后来有了一个孩子——一个女儿。
杰恩
那也没能——？

===Original===
Drummle
Not a bit of it; it made matters worse. Frightened at her failure to stir up in him some sympathetic religious belief, she determined upon strong measures with regard to the child. He opposed her for a miserable year or so, but she wore him down, and the insensible little brat was placed in a convent, first in France, then in Ireland. Not long afterwards the mother died, strangely enough, of fever, the only warmth, I believe, that ever came to that woman’s body.
Misquith
Don’t, Cayley!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
一点也没有；反倒更糟。她未能在他心里唤起些许共鸣的宗教信仰，惊惶之下，便决意对孩子采取强硬措施。他苦苦抵抗了一年上下，还是被她磨垮了，那个没心没肺的小丫头被送进了修道院，先在法国，后在爱尔兰。不久之后，做母亲的去世了，说来奇怪，死于热病——我相信，那是那个女人身体里仅有的一次发热。
米斯基斯
别说了，凯利！

===Original===
Jayne
The child is living, we know.
Drummle
Yes, if you choose to call it living. Miss Tanqueray﻿—a young woman of nineteen now﻿—is in the Loretto convent at Armagh. She professes to have found her true vocation in a religious life, and within a month or two will take final vows.

===Chinese===
杰恩
我们知道，那孩子还活着。
德鲁姆尔
是的，如果你们愿意把这叫作活着的话。坦奎雷小姐——如今是个十九岁的年轻姑娘了——在阿马（Armagh）的洛雷托修道院里。她自称已在修道生活中找到了真正的人生使命，一两个月内就要发终身愿了。

===Original===
Misquith
He ought to have removed his daughter from the convent when the mother died.
Drummle
Yes, yes, but absolutely at the end there was reconciliation between husband and wife, and she won his promise that the child should complete her conventual education. He reaped his reward. When he attempted to gain his girl’s confidence and affection he was too late; he found he was dealing with the spirit of the mother. You remember his visit to Ireland last month?

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
母亲一死，他就该把女儿从修道院里接出来。
德鲁姆尔
是，是，可到最后关头，夫妻俩和解了，她逼着他许下诺言，让孩子修完修道院的教育。他自食其果。等他试图赢得女儿的信任和爱时，已经太迟；他发现自己面对的是她母亲的魂灵。你记得他上个月的爱尔兰之行吧？

===Original===
Jayne
Yes.
Drummle
That was to wish his girl goodbye.

===Chinese===
杰恩
记得。
德鲁姆尔
那就是去跟女儿道别的。

===Original===
Misquith
Poor fellow?
Drummle
He sent for me when he came back. I think he must have had a lingering hope that the girl would relent﻿—would come to life, as it were﻿—at the last moment, for, for an hour or so, in this room, he was terribly shaken. I’m sure he’d clung to that hope from the persistent way in which he kept breaking off in his talk to repeat one dismal word, as if he couldn’t realise his position without dinning this damned word into his head.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
可怜的家伙？
德鲁姆尔
他一回来就把我叫了去。我想他心里一定还存着一线希望，指望那姑娘会在最后一刻回心转意——可以说，缓过一口气来；因为有个把钟头，就在这个房间里，他抖得厉害。我敢肯定他一直抱着那个希望，因为他说着说着总要停下来，反复念叨一个凄惨的词，仿佛不把这个该死的词敲进脑袋里，他就意识不到自己的处境。

===Original===
Jayne
What word was that?
Drummle
Alone﻿—alone.
***Aubrey** enters.*

===Chinese===
杰恩
是哪个词？
德鲁姆尔
孤独——孤独。
***奥布里**上。*

===Original===
Aubrey
A thousand apologies!
Drummle
*Gaily.* We are talking about you, my dear Aubrey.
*During the telling of the story, **Misquith** has risen and gone to the fire, and **Drummle** has thrown himself full-length on the sofa. **Aubrey** now joins **Misquith** and **Jayne**.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
万分抱歉！
德鲁姆尔
*（快活地。）*我们正说你呢，亲爱的奥布里。
*（讲故事期间，**米斯基斯**起身走向炉火，**德鲁姆尔**则整个人摊在沙发上。此时**奥布里**走到**米斯基斯**和**杰恩**近旁。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Well, Cayley, are you surprised?
Drummle
Surp﻿—! I haven’t been surprised for twenty years.

===Chinese===
奥布里
怎么样，凯利，你吃惊吗？
德鲁姆尔
吃——！我已经二十年没吃惊过了。

===Original===
Aubrey
And you’re not angry with me?
Drummle
Angry! *Rising.* Because you considerately withhold the name of a lady with whom it is now the object of my life to become acquainted? My dear fellow, you pique my curiosity, you give zest to my existence! And as for a wedding, who on earth wants to attend that familiar and probably draughty function? Ugh! My cigar’s out.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你也不生我的气？
德鲁姆尔
生气！*（起身。）*就因为你体贴地不肯说出一位女士的姓名，而结识这位女士如今已是我毕生之目标？亲爱的伙计，你勾起了我的好奇，给我的存在添了滋味！至于婚礼嘛，那种老一套、多半还灌风的仪式，天底下谁要去参加？呸！我的雪茄灭了。

===Original===
Aubrey
Let’s talk about something else.
Misquith
*Looking at his watch.* Not tonight, Aubrey.

===Chinese===
奥布里
咱们说点别的吧。
米斯基斯
*（看表。）*今晚不行了，奥布里。

===Original===
Aubrey
My dear Frank!
Misquith
I go up to Scotland tomorrow, and there are some little matters﻿—

===Chinese===
奥布里
亲爱的弗兰克！
米斯基斯
我明天要去苏格兰，还有几件小事——

===Original===
Jayne
I am off too.
Aubrey
No, no.

===Chinese===
杰恩
我也得走了。
奥布里
别，别。

===Original===
Jayne
I must: I have to give a look to a case in Clifford Street on my way home.
Aubrey
*Going to the door.* Well! ***Misquith** and **Jayne** exchange looks with **Drummle**. Opening the door and calling.* Morse, hats and coats! I shall write to you all next week from Genoa or Florence. Now, doctor, Frank, remember, my love to Mrs. Misquith and to Mrs. Jayne!
***Morse** enters with hats and coats.*

===Chinese===
杰恩
必须走了：回家路上我还得去克利福德街（Clifford Street）看一个病人。
奥布里
*（向门口走去。）*好吧！***米斯基斯**和**杰恩**与**德鲁姆尔**交换眼色。**奥布里**开门喊道。*莫尔斯，拿帽子和外套！下周我会从热那亚（Genoa）或佛罗伦萨（Florence）给你们大家写信。记着，医生，弗兰克，代我问米斯基斯夫人和杰恩夫人好！
***莫尔斯**拿着帽子和外套上。*

===Original===
**Misquith** and **Jayne**
Yes, yes﻿—yes, yes.
Aubrey
And your young people!
*As **Misquith** and **Jayne** put on their coats there is the clatter of careless talk.*

===Chinese===
**米斯基斯**与**杰恩**
好的，好的——好的，好的。
奥布里
还有你们的孩子们！
*（**米斯基斯**和**杰恩**穿衣时，众人闲谈声杂然。）*

===Original===
Jayne
Cayley, I meet you at dinner on Sunday.
Drummle
At the Stratfields’. That’s very pleasant.

===Chinese===
杰恩
凯利，星期天的晚宴我们见。
德鲁姆尔
在斯特拉特菲尔德家（the Stratfields’）。那太好了。

===Original===
Misquith
*Putting on his coat with **Aubrey’s** aid.* Ah-h!
Aubrey
What’s wrong?

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
*（在**奥布里**帮助下穿衣。）*啊——！
奥布里
怎么了？

===Original===
Misquith
A twinge. Why didn’t I go to Aix in August?
Jayne
*Shaking hands with **Drummle**.* Good night, Cayley.

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
抽了一下痛。八月里我怎么没去埃克斯（Aix）呢？
杰恩
*（与**德鲁姆尔**握手。）*晚安，凯利。

===Original===
Drummle
Good night, my dear doctor!
Misquith
*Shaking hands with **Drummle**.* Cayley, are you in town for long?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
晚安，亲爱的医生！
米斯基斯
*（与**德鲁姆尔**握手。）*凯利，你要在城里久待吗？

===Original===
Drummle
Dear friend, I’m nowhere for long. Good night.
Misquith
Good night.
***Aubrey**, **Jayne**, and **Misquith** go out, followed by **Morse**; the hum of talk is continued outside.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的朋友，我在哪儿都不久待。晚安。
米斯基斯
晚安。
***奥布里**、**杰恩**和**米斯基斯**下，**莫尔斯**随下；谈话的嗡嗡声在门外继续。*

===Original===
Aubrey
A cigar, Frank?
Misquith
No, thank you.

===Chinese===
奥布里
来支雪茄吗，弗兰克？
米斯基斯
不了，谢谢。

===Original===
Aubrey
Going to walk, doctor?
Jayne
If Frank will.

===Chinese===
奥布里
走回去吗，医生？
杰恩
只要弗兰克肯走。

===Original===
Misquith
By all means.
Aubrey
It’s a cold night.
*The door is closed. **Drummle** remains standing with his coat on his arm and his hat in his hand.*

===Chinese===
米斯基斯
完全没问题。
奥布里
今晚很冷。
*（门关上了。**德鲁姆尔**仍站在原地，臂上搭着外套，手里拿着帽子。）*

===Original===
Drummle
*To himself, thoughtfully.* Now then! What the devil﻿—!
***Aubrey** returns.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（自语，若有所思。）*这下好了！这算什么鬼——！
***奥布里**回。*

===Original===
Aubrey
*Eyeing **Drummle** a little awkwardly.* Well, Cayley?
Drummle
Well, Aubrey?
***Aubrey** walks up to the fire and stands looking into it.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（略显尴尬地打量**德鲁姆尔**。）*怎么样，凯利？
德鲁姆尔
怎么样，奥布里？
***奥布里**走到炉火前，站着凝望火中。*

===Original===
Aubrey
You’re not going, old chap?
Drummle
*Sitting.* No.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你不走了，老伙计？
德鲁姆尔
*（坐下。）*不走了。

===Original===
Aubrey
*After a slight pause, with a forced laugh.* Hah! Cayley, I never thought I should feel﻿—shy﻿—with you.
Drummle
Why do you?

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（略停，强笑。）*哈！凯利，我从没想过我会在你面前觉得——局促。
德鲁姆尔
为什么会呢？

===Original===
Aubrey
Never mind.
Drummle
Now, I can quite understand a man wishing to be married in the dark, as it were.

===Chinese===
奥布里
别管这个。
德鲁姆尔
呃，一个男人想悄悄地结婚，可以说瞒着人结婚，这我完全能理解。

===Original===
Aubrey
You can?
Drummle
In your place I should very likely adopt the same course.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你能？
德鲁姆尔
我要是处你的位置，多半也会照此办理。

===Original===
Aubrey
You think so?
Drummle
And if I intended marrying a lady not prominently in Society, as I presume you do﻿—as I presume you do﻿—

===Chinese===
奥布里
你这么想？
德鲁姆尔
再者，倘若我打算娶的女士并不显赫于上流社会——我料想你的情形便是如此——我料想你的情形便是如此——

===Original===
Aubrey
Well?
Drummle
As I presume you do, I’m not sure that *I* should tender her for preliminary dissection at afternoon tea-tables.

===Chinese===
奥布里
嗯？
德鲁姆尔
——我料想你的情形便是如此，那么我不敢说，*我*就一定会在午后的茶桌上把她交出去任人预先解剖。

===Original===
Aubrey
No?
Drummle
In fact, there is probably only one person﻿—were I in your position tonight﻿—with whom I should care to chat the matter over.

===Chinese===
奥布里
是吗？
德鲁姆尔
其实，倘若我今晚处在你的位置，大概只有一个人，是我愿意跟他就此事好好聊聊的。

===Original===
Aubrey
Who’s that?
Drummle
Yourself, of course. *Going to **Aubrey** and standing beside him.* Of course, yourself, old friend.

===Chinese===
奥布里
是谁？
德鲁姆尔
当然是你自己。*（走到**奥布里**身旁站定。）*当然是你自己，老朋友。

===Original===
Aubrey
*After a pause.* I must seem a brute to you, Cayley. But there are some acts which are hard to explain, hard to defend﻿—
Drummle
To defend﻿—?

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（停顿片刻。）*在你看来我一定像个浑蛋，凯利。可有些行为实在难以解释，难以辩护——
德鲁姆尔
难以辩护——？

===Original===
Aubrey
Some acts which one must trust to time to put right.
***Drummle** watches him for a moment, then takes up his hat and coat.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
有些行为，只能交给时间去摆正。
***德鲁姆尔**注视他片刻，随后拿起帽子和外套。*

===Original===
Drummle
Well, I’ll be moving.
Aubrey
Cayley! Confound you and your old friendship! Do you think I forget it? Put your coat down! Why did you stay behind here? Cayley, the lady I am going to marry is the lady﻿—who is known as﻿—Mrs. Jarman.
*There is a pause.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
好吧，我该走了。
奥布里
凯利！你和你的老交情真见鬼！你以为我忘了吗？把外套放下！你为什么要留下来？凯利，我要娶的那位女士，就是那位——人们称作——贾曼夫人（Mrs. Jarman）的女士。
*（一阵沉默。）*

===Original===
Drummle
*In a low voice.* Mrs. Jarman! are you serious?
*He walks up to the fireplace, where he leans upon the mantelpiece uttering something like a groan.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（低声。）*贾曼夫人！你是当真的？
*（他走到壁炉前，俯身倚着壁炉架，发出一声近乎呻吟的声音。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
As you’ve got this out of me I give you leave to say all you care to say. Come, we’ll be plain with each other. You know Mrs. Jarman?
Drummle
I first met her at﻿—what does it matter?

===Chinese===
奥布里
话既然从我这儿掏出来了，你想说什么就尽管说吧。来，咱们彼此打开天窗说亮话。你认识贾曼夫人？
德鲁姆尔
我初次遇见她是在——这有什么要紧？

===Original===
Aubrey
Yes, yes, everything! Come!
Drummle
I met her at Homburg, two﻿—three seasons ago.

===Chinese===
奥布里
要紧，要紧，一切都要紧！说吧！
德鲁姆尔
我是在巴特洪堡（Homburg）遇见她的，两——三个社交季之前。

===Original===
Aubrey
Not as Mrs. Jarman?
Drummle
No.

===Chinese===
奥布里
那时她不叫贾曼夫人？
德鲁姆尔
不。

===Original===
Aubrey
She was then﻿—?
Drummle
Mrs. Dartry.

===Chinese===
奥布里
她那时是——？
德鲁姆尔
达特里夫人（Mrs. Dartry）。

===Original===
Aubrey
Yes. She has also seen you in London, she says.
Drummle
Certainly.

===Chinese===
奥布里
是了。她说，她在伦敦也见过你。
德鲁姆尔
确实。

===Original===
Aubrey
In Aldford Street. Go on.
Drummle
Please!

===Chinese===
奥布里
在奥尔德福德街（Aldford Street）。说下去。
德鲁姆尔
别逼我了！

===Original===
Aubrey
I insist.
Drummle
*With a slight shrug of the shoulders.* Some time last year I was asked by a man to sup at his house, one night after the theatre.

===Chinese===
奥布里
我偏要你说。
德鲁姆尔
*（微微耸肩。）*去年某个时候，有位先生请我去他家用夜宵，一天夜里，看完戏之后。

===Original===
Aubrey
Mr. Selwyn Ethurst﻿—a bachelor.
Drummle
Yes.

===Chinese===
奥布里
塞尔温·埃瑟斯特先生（Selwyn Ethurst）——一位单身汉。
德鲁姆尔
正是。

===Original===
Aubrey
You were surprised therefore to find Mr. Ethurst aided in his cursed hospitality by a lady.
Drummle
I was unprepared.

===Chinese===
奥布里
于是你发现埃瑟斯特先生那可诅咒的殷勤里竟有一位女士帮忙，不免吃了一惊。
德鲁姆尔
我没有防备。

===Original===
Aubrey
The lady you had known as Mrs. Dartry? ***Drummle** inclines his head silently.* There is something of a yachting cruise in the Mediterranean too, is there not?
Drummle
I joined Peter Jarman’s yacht at Marseilles, in the Spring, a month before he died.

===Chinese===
奥布里
那位女士，就是你认识的达特里夫人？***德鲁姆尔**默默点头。*还有一次地中海的游艇巡航，是也不是？
德鲁姆尔
春天，我在马赛（Marseilles）上了彼得·贾曼（Peter Jarman）的游艇，那是他死前一个月。

===Original===
Aubrey
Mrs. Jarman was on board?
Drummle
She was a kind hostess.

===Chinese===
奥布里
贾曼夫人在船上？
德鲁姆尔
她是位和善的女主人。

===Original===
Aubrey
And an old acquaintance?
Drummle
Yes.

===Chinese===
奥布里
也是旧相识？
德鲁姆尔
是的。

===Original===
Aubrey
You have told your story.
Drummle
With your assistance.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你的故事讲完了。
德鲁姆尔
多亏了你的协助。

===Original===
Aubrey
I have put you to the pain of telling it to show you that this is not the case of a blind man entrapped by an artful woman. Let me add that Mrs. Jarman has no legal right to that name, that she is simply Miss Ray﻿—Miss Paula Ray.
Drummle
*After a pause.* I should like to express my regret, Aubrey, for the way in which I spoke of George Orreyed’s marriage.

===Chinese===
奥布里
我逼你忍痛把它讲出来，是要让你明白，这不是一个盲汉被狡诈女人诱入彀中的案子。再让我补一句：贾曼夫人对那个姓氏并没有合法权利，她其实就是雷小姐（Miss Ray）——宝拉·雷小姐（Miss Paula Ray）。
德鲁姆尔
*（停顿片刻。）*我想表示歉意，奥布里，为我说乔治·奥雷耶德婚事时的那些口吻。

===Original===
Aubrey
You mean you compare Lady Orreyed with Miss Ray? ***Drummle** is silent.* Oh, of course! To you, Cayley, all women who have been roughly treated, and who dare to survive by borrowing a little of our philosophy, are alike. You see in the crowd of the ill-used only one pattern; you can’t detect the shades of goodness, intelligence, even nobility there. Well, how should you? The crowd is dimly lighted! And, besides, yours is the way of the world.
Drummle
My dear Aubrey, I *live* in the world.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你的意思是，你把奥雷耶德夫人和雷小姐相提并论？***德鲁姆尔**沉默。*哦，当然了！在你眼里，凯利，凡是受过粗暴对待、又胆敢借我们男人的处世哲学活下去的女人，全都是一个样。在那群被亏待的人里，你只看见一种花样；你分辨不出其中各各不同的善良、才智，乃至高贵。唉，你又怎么能分辨呢？那群人光照昏暗！再说，你奉行的本就是世道的法则。
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的奥布里，我可是*活*在这个世道里的。

===Original===
Aubrey
The name we give our little parish of St. James’s.
Drummle
*Laying a hand on **Aubrey’s** shoulder.* And you are quite prepared, my friend, to forfeit the esteem of your little parish?

===Chinese===
奥布里
那不过是我们给圣詹姆斯（St. James’s）这个小教区起的名字罢了。
德鲁姆尔
*（把一只手放在**奥布里**肩上。）*那么，我的朋友，你完全准备好牺牲你那小教区的敬重了？

===Original===
Aubrey
I avoid mortification by shifting from one parish to another. I give up Pall Mall for the Surrey hills; leave off varnishing my boots and double the thickness of the soles.
Drummle
And your skin﻿—do you double the thickness of that also?

===Chinese===
奥布里
我靠从一个教区挪到另一个教区来避免受辱。我舍弃波迈街（Pall Mall）换到萨里山间；不再给靴子上蜡，把鞋底加厚一倍。
德鲁姆尔
那你的脸皮呢——也加厚一倍吗？

===Original===
Aubrey
I know you think me a fool, Cayley﻿—you needn’t infer that I’m a coward into the bargain. No! I know what I’m doing, and I do it deliberately, defiantly. I’m alone; I injure no living soul by the step I’m going to take; and so you can’t urge the one argument which might restrain me. Of course, I don’t expect you to think compassionately, fairly even, of the woman whom I﻿—whom I am drawn to﻿—
Drummle
My dear Aubrey, I assure you I consider Mrs.﻿—Miss Jarman﻿—Mrs. Ray﻿—Miss Ray﻿—delightful. But I confess there is a form of chivalry which I gravely distrust, especially in a man of﻿—our age.

===Chinese===
奥布里
我知道你把我当傻子，凯利——但你不必再推论我顺便还是个懦夫。不！我清楚自己在做什么，而且我是存心的、公然反抗着去做的。我是孤身一人；我要走的这一步伤害不了任何活人；所以你无法拿出那条也许能拦住我的理由。当然，我不指望你宽厚地、甚至公道地看待这个我——这个我心之所系的——女人——
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的奥布里，我向你保证，我认为贾曼夫——贾曼小姐——雷夫——雷小姐——可爱极了。不过我承认，有一种骑士精神是我深深怀疑的，尤其是在一个——我们这把年纪的男人身上。

===Original===
Aubrey
Thanks. I’ve heard you say that from forty till fifty a man is at heart either a stoic or a satyr.
Drummle
*Protestingly.* Ah! now﻿—

===Chinese===
奥布里
多谢。我听你说过，人从四十到五十，心里不是个禁欲者，便是个色情狂。
德鲁姆尔
*（抗议地。）*啊！这可——

===Original===
Aubrey
I am neither. I have a temperate, honourable affection for Mrs. Jarman. She has never met a man who has treated her well﻿—I intend to treat her well. That’s all. And in a few years, Cayley, if you’ve not quite forsaken me, I’ll prove to you that it’s possible to rear a life of happiness, of good repute, on a﻿—miserable foundation.
Drummle
*Offering his hand.* Do prove it!

===Chinese===
奥布里
我两样都不是。我对贾曼夫人怀着一种有节制、光明正大的感情。她从未遇见过一个善待她的男人——我打算善待她。仅此而已。过几年，凯利，倘若你没有彻底跟我断绝往来，我会向你证明：在一块——可怜的地基上，也盖得起幸福而清白的生活。
德鲁姆尔
*（伸出手。）*一定要证明给我看！

===Original===
Aubrey
*Taking his hand.* We have spoken too freely of﻿—of Mrs. Jarman. I was excited﻿—angry. Please forget it!
Drummle
My dear Aubrey, when we next meet I shall remember nothing but my respect for the lady who bears your name.
***Morse** enters, closing the door behind him carefully.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（握住他的手。）*我们把贾曼——把贾曼夫人讲得太无所顾忌了。我刚才激动了——动了气。请忘掉吧！
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的奥布里，下次见面时，我记住的将只有我对那位冠你之姓的女士的敬意。
***莫尔斯**上，随手小心地带上门。*

===Original===
Aubrey
What is it?
Morse
*Hesitatingly.* May I speak to you, Sir? *In an undertone.* Mrs. Jarman, sir.

===Chinese===
奥布里
什么事？
莫尔斯
*（迟疑地。）*能跟您说句话吗，先生？*（压低声音。）*是贾曼夫人，先生。

===Original===
Aubrey
*Softly to **Morse**.* Mrs. Jarman! Do you mean she is at the lodge in her carriage?
Morse
No, sir﻿—here. ***Aubrey** looks towards **Drummle**, perplexed.* There’s a nice fire in your﻿—in that room, sir. *Glancing in the direction of the door leading to the bedroom.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（轻声对**莫尔斯**。）*贾曼夫人！你是说她坐着马车在门房那儿？
莫尔斯
不是，先生——在这儿。***奥布里**困惑地朝**德鲁姆尔**望了一眼。*您那间——那间房里炉火正旺，先生。*（朝通往卧室的那扇门瞥了一眼。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*Between his teeth, angrily.* Very well.
***Morse** retires.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（从牙缝里，愠怒地。）*很好。
***莫尔斯**退下。*

===Original===
Drummle
*Looking at his watch.* A quarter to eleven﻿—horrible! *Taking up his hat and coat.* Must get to bed﻿—up late every night this week. ***Aubrey** assists **Drummle** with his coat.* Thank you. Well, good night, Aubrey. I feel I’ve been dooced serious, quite out of keeping with myself; pray overlook it.
Aubrey
*Kindly.* Ah, Cayley!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（看表。）*十一点差一刻——糟糕！*（拿起帽子和外套。）*必须睡了——这个礼拜天天熬夜。***奥布里**帮**德鲁姆尔**穿衣。*谢谢。那么，晚安，奥布里。我觉得自己今晚未免太一本正经了，全然不合我的本性；请多包涵。
奥布里
*（宽厚地。）*唉，凯利！

===Original===
Drummle
*Putting on a neck-handkerchief.* And remember that, after all, I’m merely a spectator in life; nothing more than a man at a play, in fact; only, like the old-fashioned playgoer, I love to see certain characters happy and comfortable at the finish. You understand?
Aubrey
I think I do.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（围上颈巾。）*还要记住，我终究只是人生的看客；说到底，不过是个坐在戏园子里看戏的人罢了；只是，像老派的看客一样，我爱看某些角色在终场时幸福安稳。你明白吗？
奥布里
我想我明白。

===Original===
Drummle
Then, for as long as you can, old friend, will you﻿—keep a stall for me?
Aubrey
Yes, Cayley.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
那么，老朋友，请你尽量——给我留个正厅的座位，可好？
奥布里
留的，凯利。

===Original===
Drummle
*Gaily.* Ah, ha! Good night! *Bustling to the door.* Don’t bother! I’ll let myself out! Good night! God bless yer!
*He goes out; **Aubrey** follows him. **Morse** enters by the other door, carrying some unopened letters which after a little consideration he places on the mantelpiece against the clock. **Aubrey** returns.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（快活地。）*啊，哈！晚安！*（匆匆向门口走去。）*别送了！我自己出去！晚安！上帝保佑你！
*（他下；**奥布里**送他。**莫尔斯**从另一扇门上，捧着几封未拆的信，略一斟酌后把它们靠着时钟立在壁炉架上。**奥布里**回。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Yes?
Morse
You hadn’t seen your letters that came by the nine o’clock post, sir; I’ve put ’em where they’ll catch your eye by-and-by.

===Chinese===
奥布里
什么事？
莫尔斯
九点钟那班邮差送来的信您还没看呢，先生；我把它们搁在您过一会儿一眼就能瞧见的地方了。

===Original===
Aubrey
Thank you.
Morse
*Hesitatingly.* Gunter’s cook and waiter have gone, sir. Would you prefer me to go to bed?

===Chinese===
奥布里
谢谢。
莫尔斯
*（迟疑地。）*冈特家（Gunter’s）的厨子和侍者都走了，先生。您要不要我也去睡？

===Original===
Aubrey
*Frowning.* Certainly not.
Morse
Very well, sir.
*He goes out.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（皱眉。）*当然不要。
莫尔斯
是，先生。
*（他下。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*Opening the upper door.* Paula! Paula!
***Paula** enters and throws her arms round his neck. She is a young woman of about twenty-seven: beautiful, fresh, innocent-looking. She is in superb evening dress.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（打开上面那扇门。）*宝拉（Paula）！宝拉！
***宝拉**上，一把搂住他的脖子。她是个二十七岁上下的年轻女子：美丽、鲜妍、面带天真。她穿着一身华贵的晚礼服。*

===Original===
Paula
Dearest!
Aubrey
Why have you come here?

===Chinese===
宝拉
亲爱的！
奥布里
你怎么到这儿来了？

===Original===
Paula
Angry?
Aubrey
Yes﻿—no. But it’s eleven o’clock.

===Chinese===
宝拉
生气了？
奥布里
生气——不生气。可现在十一点了。

===Original===
Paula
*Laughing.* I know.
Aubrey
What on earth will Morse think?

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（笑。）*我知道。
奥布里
莫尔斯会怎么想？

===Original===
Paula
Do you trouble yourself about what servants *think*?
Aubrey
Of course.

===Chinese===
宝拉
你还为仆人*怎么想*操心吗？
奥布里
当然。

===Original===
Paula
Goose! They’re only machines made to wait upon people﻿—and to give evidence in the Divorce Court. *Looking round.* Oh, indeed! A snug little dinner!
Aubrey
Three men.

===Chinese===
宝拉
傻瓜！他们不过是造出来伺候人的机器——外加在离婚法庭上作证。*（环顾四周。）*哦，好嘛！一顿舒舒服服的小晚宴！
奥布里
三个男人。

===Original===
Paula
*Suspiciously.* Men?
Aubrey
Men.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（起疑地。）*男人？
奥布里
男人。

===Original===
Paula
*Penitently.* Ah! *Sitting at the table.* I’m so hungry.
Aubrey
Let me get you some game pie, or some﻿—

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（悔罪似的。）*啊！*（在桌边坐下。）*我好饿。
奥布里
我给你拿点野味馅饼，或者——

===Original===
Paula
No, no, hungry for this. What beautiful fruit! I love fruit when it’s expensive. *He clears a space on the table, places a plate before her, and helps her to fruit.* I haven’t dined, Aubrey dear.
Aubrey
My poor girl! Why?

===Chinese===
宝拉
不，不，我馋的是这个。多好看的水果！水果越贵我越爱。*（他清出桌上一块地方，在她面前摆上盘子，为她布水果。）*我还没吃晚饭呢，亲爱的奥布里。
奥布里
我可怜的姑娘！怎么不吃？

===Original===
Paula
In the first place, I forgot to order any dinner, and my cook, who has always loathed me, thought he’d pay me out before he departed.
Aubrey
The beast!

===Chinese===
宝拉
首先，我忘了点晚餐，而我的厨子——他一向恨我——想在走之前报复我一下。
奥布里
畜生！

===Original===
Paula
That’s precisely what I﻿—
Aubrey
No, Paula!

===Chinese===
宝拉
那正是我——
奥布里
别说了，宝拉！

===Original===
Paula
What I told my maid to call him. What next will you think of me?
Aubrey
Forgive me. You must be starved.

===Chinese===
宝拉
——让女仆怎么叫他的。接下来你该怎么看我了呢？
奥布里
原谅我。你一定饿坏了。

===Original===
Paula
*Eating fruit.* *I* didn’t care. As there was nothing to eat, I sat in my best frock, with my toes on the dining-room fender, and dreamt, oh, such a lovely dinner-party.
Aubrey
Dear lonely little woman!

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（吃着水果。）**我*倒不在乎。反正没吃的，我就穿着最漂亮的衣裙坐着，脚尖搭在餐室的炉围上，做起了梦，哦，一场多可爱的晚宴。
奥布里
可爱又孤单的小女人！

===Original===
Paula
It was perfect. I saw you at the end of a very long table, opposite me, and we exchanged sly glances now and again over the flowers. We were host and hostess, Aubrey, and had been married about five years.
Aubrey
*Kissing her hand.* Five years.

===Chinese===
宝拉
那梦完美极了。我看见你坐在一张很长很长的桌子尽头，正对着我，我们不时隔着花丛交换狡黠的眼波。我们是男主人同女主人，奥布里，而且已经结婚五年左右了。
奥布里
*（吻她的手。）*五年。

===Original===
Paula
And on each side of us was the nicest set imaginable﻿—you know, dearest, the sort of men and women that can’t be imitated.
Aubrey
Yes, yes. Eat some more fruit.

===Chinese===
宝拉
我们两边坐的是想象得到的最美妙的一群人——你知道的，亲爱的，那种没法仿造的男人和女人。
奥布里
是，是。再多吃点水果。

===Original===
Paula
But I haven’t told you the best part of my dream.
Aubrey
Tell me.

===Chinese===
宝拉
可我还没告诉你我梦里最妙的部分呢。
奥布里
告诉我。

===Original===
Paula
Well, although we had been married only such a few years, I seemed to know by the look on their faces that none of our guests had ever heard anything﻿—anything﻿—anything peculiar about the fascinating hostess.
Aubrey
That’s just how it will be, Paula. The world moves so quickly. That’s just how it will be.

===Chinese===
宝拉
唔，虽然我们结婚才这么几年，我却仿佛从他们脸上的神色看出，我们的客人谁也没有听说过这位迷人的女主人有什么——什么——什么特别的地方。
奥布里
事情就会是那样的，宝拉。这世道变得快得很。就会是那样的。

===Original===
Paula
*With a little grimace.* I wonder! *Glancing at the fire.* Ugh! do throw another log on.
Aubrey
*Mending the fire.* There. But you mustn’t be here long.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（做了个小鬼脸。）*是吗！*（瞥了一眼炉火。）*呀！快再添块柴。
奥布里
*（拨旺炉火。）*好了。可你不能在这儿久待。

===Original===
Paula
Hospitable wretch! I’ve something important to tell you. No, stay where you are. *Turning from him, her face averted.* Look here, that was my dream, Aubrey; but the fire went out while I was dozing, and I woke up with a regular fit of the shivers. And the result of it all was that I ran upstairs and scribbled you a letter.
Aubrey
Dear baby!

===Chinese===
宝拉
好客的坏蛋！我有要紧事要告诉你。不，你待在原地。*（转过身去，背着脸。）*你听好，那是我的梦，奥布里；可我打盹的时候炉火灭了，我醒来时冻得一阵阵发抖。这一切的结果，就是我跑上楼给你潦草写了封信。
奥布里
亲爱的乖宝宝！

===Original===
Paula
Remain where you are. *Taking a letter from her pocket.* This is it. I’ve given you an account of myself, furnished you with a list of my adventures since I﻿—you know. *Weighing the letter in her hand.* I wonder if it would go for a penny. Most of it you’re acquainted with; *I’ve* told you a good deal, haven’t I?
Aubrey
Oh, Paula!

===Chinese===
宝拉
待在那儿别动。*（从口袋里取出一封信。）*就是这个。我给你写了一份自述，开列了我自——你知道的那事——以来的种种经历。*（在手里掂了掂信。）*不知它够不够一便士的邮资。大部分你是知道的；*我*跟你讲过不少了，对吧？
奥布里
唉，宝拉！

===Original===
Paula
What I haven’t told you I daresay you’ve heard from others. But in case they’ve omitted anything﻿—the dears﻿—it’s all here.
Aubrey
In Heaven’s name, why must you talk like this tonight?

===Chinese===
宝拉
我没告诉你的，你八成也从别人那里听说了。不过万一他们漏掉了什么——那些亲爱的们——都在这儿了。
奥布里
看在老天的份上，你今晚为什么非要这样说话？

===Original===
Paula
It may save discussion by-and-by, don’t you think? *Holding out the letter.* There you are.
Aubrey
No, dear, no.

===Chinese===
宝拉
省得以后再解释，不好吗？*（递出信。）*拿着。
奥布里
不，亲爱的，不。

===Original===
Paula
Take it. *He takes the letter.* Read it through after I’ve gone, and then﻿—read it again, and turn the matter over in your mind finally. And if, even at the very last moment, you feel you﻿—oughtn’t to go to church with me, send a messenger to Pont Street, any time before eleven tomorrow, telling me that you’re afraid, and I﻿—I’ll take the blow.
Aubrey
Why, what﻿—what do you think I am?

===Chinese===
宝拉
拿着。*（他接过信。）*我走之后把它通读一遍，然后——再读一遍，把这件事在你心里翻来覆去想个透。倘若到了最后一刻，你觉得——不该陪我上教堂，明天十一点之前随时派人送信到庞特街（Pont Street），告诉我你怕了，那我——我就受下这一击。
奥布里
咦，你——你把我当成什么人了？

===Original===
Paula
That’s it. It’s because I know you’re such a dear good fellow that I want to save you the chance of ever feeling sorry you married me. I really love you so much, Aubrey, that to save you that I’d rather you treated me as﻿—as the others have done.
Aubrey
*Turning from her with a cry.* Oh!

===Chinese===
宝拉
就是这话。正因为我深知你是个这么可爱的好人，我才要让你永无后悔娶我的一天。我是那么真心地爱你，奥布里，为了让你免受那种悔恨，我宁肯你像——像旁人那样对待我。
奥布里
*（叫了一声，转过身去。）*哦！

===Original===
Paula
*After a slight pause.* I suppose I’ve shocked you. I can’t help it if I have.
*She sits, with assumed languor and indifference. He turns to her, advances, and kneels by her.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（略停。）*我想我是吓着你了。吓着了也没法子。
*（她坐下，装出一副慵懒冷淡的神气。他转向她，走上前，跪在她身旁。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
My dearest, you don’t understand me. I﻿—I can’t bear to hear you always talking about﻿—what’s done with. I tell you I’ll never remember it; Paula, can’t you dismiss it? Try. Darling, if we promise each other to forget, to forget, we’re bound to be happy. After all, it’s a mechanical matter; the moment a wretched thought enters your head, you quickly think of something bright﻿—it depends on one’s will. Shall I burn this, dear? *Referring to the letter he holds in his hand.* Let me, let me!
Paula
*With a shrug of the shoulders.* I don’t suppose there’s much that’s new to you in it﻿—just as you like.
*He goes to the fire and burns the letter.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
我最亲爱的，你不理解我。我——我受不了听你老是谈那些——已经了结的事。我告诉你，我永远不会记着它；宝拉，你就不能把它抛开吗？试试看。亲爱的，只要我们彼此许诺忘掉，忘掉，我们一定会幸福。说到底，这不过是件机械的事；一个倒霉的念头刚钻进你脑子，你就赶快想点明亮的事——全看人的意志。让我把这封信烧了吧，亲爱的？*（指了指手里拿着的信。）*让我烧了它，让我烧了它！
宝拉
*（耸耸肩。）*我想里面没什么对你来说是新鲜的——随你的便。
*（他走到炉火前，把信烧了。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
There’s an end of it. *Returning to her.* What’s the matter?
Paula
*Rising, coldly.* Oh, nothing! I’ll go and put my cloak on.

===Chinese===
奥布里
这下完了结了。*（回到她身边。）*怎么啦？
宝拉
*（起身，冷冷的。）*哦，没什么！我去把斗篷披上。

===Original===
Aubrey
*Detaining her.* What *is* the matter?
Paula
Well, I think you might have said, “You’re very generous, Paula,” or at least, “Thank you, dear,” when I offered to set you free.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（拉住她。）*到底*怎么*啦？
宝拉
唔，我主动放你自由的时候，你总该说一句“你真宽宏大量，宝拉”，或者至少说声“谢谢你，亲爱的”吧。

===Original===
Aubrey
*Catching her in his arms.* Ah!
Paula
Ah! ah! Ha, ha! It’s all very well, but you don’t know what it cost me to make such an offer. I do so want to be married.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（把她搂进怀里。）*啊！
宝拉
啊！啊！哈，哈！说得好听，可你不知道我提出这话付出了多大代价。我多么想结这个婚啊。

===Original===
Aubrey
But you never imagined﻿—?
Paula
Perhaps not. And yet I *did* think of what I’d do at the end of our acquaintance if you had preferred to behave like the rest.
*Taking a flower from her bodice.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
可你从没想过——？
宝拉
也许没有。不过我倒*确实*想过，假如你宁肯像其他人那样行事，我们这段情缘了结时我会怎么做。
*（从胸前衣襟取下一朵花。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Hush!
Paula
Oh, I forgot!

===Chinese===
奥布里
嘘！
宝拉
哦，我忘了！

===Original===
Aubrey
What would you have done when we parted?
Paula
Why, killed myself.

===Chinese===
奥布里
我们分手的话，你会怎么做？
宝拉
还用问，自杀呗。

===Original===
Aubrey
Paula, dear!
Paula
It’s true. *Putting the flower in his buttonhole.* Do you know I feel certain I should make away with myself if anything serious happened to me.

===Chinese===
奥布里
宝拉，亲爱的！
宝拉
是真的。*（把花插进他的钮孔。）*你知道吗，我确信，万一我出了什么大事，我一定会把自己了结的。

===Original===
Aubrey
Anything serious! What, has nothing ever been serious to you, Paula?
Paula
Not lately; not since a long while ago. I made up my mind then to have done with taking things seriously. If I hadn’t, I﻿—However, we won’t talk about that.

===Chinese===
奥布里
出大事！怎么，宝拉，难道从来没有一件事对你是要紧的吗？
宝拉
近来没有了；很久以前就不了。打那时起我就拿定主意，再不把任何事当真。不然的话，我——算了，我们不谈这个。

===Original===
Aubrey
But now, now, life will be different to you, won’t it﻿—quite different? Eh, dear?
Paula
Oh yes, now. Only, Aubrey, mind you keep me always happy.

===Chinese===
奥布里
可是今后，今后，日子对你就会不一样了，对不对——全然不同了？嗯，亲爱的？
宝拉
哦是，今后。只是，奥布里，你可要一直让我快活。

===Original===
Aubrey
I will try to.
Paula
I know I couldn’t swallow a second big dose of misery. I know that if ever I felt wretched again﻿—truly wretched﻿—I should take a leaf out of Connie Tirlemont’s book. You remember? They found her﻿—*With a look of horror.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
我尽力。
宝拉
我知道，第二剂大苦水我再也咽不下了。我知道，假如我再觉得痛苦——真正痛苦——我就要学康妮·蒂勒蒙（Connie Tirlemont）的样子。你记得吗？人们发现她的时候——*（面露惊恐。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
For God’s sake, don’t let your thoughts run on such things!
Paula
*Laughing.* Ha, ha, how scared you look! There, think of the time! Dearest, what will my coachman say! My cloak!
*She runs off, gaily, by the upper door. **Aubrey** looks after her for a moment, then he walks up to the fire and stands warming his feet at the bars. As he does so he raises his head and observes the letters upon the mantelpiece. He takes one down quickly.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
看在上帝的份上，别让你的念头往这种事上跑！
宝拉
*（笑。）*哈，哈，瞧你吓得那样！得了，看看都几点了！亲爱的，我的车夫会怎么说呀！我的斗篷！
*（她欢欢喜喜地从上面那扇门跑下。**奥布里**目送她片刻，随后走向炉火，站在炉栏前烤脚。烤着烤着他抬起头，看见了壁炉架上的那几封信。他赶紧取下一封。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Ah! Ellean! *Opening the letter and reading.* “My dear father﻿—A great change has come over me. I believe my mother in Heaven has spoken to me, and counselled me to turn to you in your loneliness. At any rate, your words have reached my heart, and I no longer feel fitted for this solemn life. I am ready to take my place by you. Dear father, will you receive me?﻿—Ellean.”
***Paula** reenters, dressed in a handsome cloak. He stares at her as if he hardly realised her presence.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
啊！埃莉安（Ellean）！*（拆信读道。）*“亲爱的父亲——我起了极大的变化。我相信是天堂里的母亲对我开了口，劝我在您孤寂的时候回到您身边。至少，您的话已说进了我心里，我不再觉得自己适合这种庄严的生活。我准备回到您身旁尽我的本分。亲爱的父亲，您肯接纳我吗？——埃莉安。”
***宝拉**重新上，已披上一件华丽的斗篷。他直勾勾地望着她，仿佛几乎没意识到她的存在。*

===Original===
Paula
What are you staring at? Don’t you admire my cloak?
Aubrey
Yes.

===Chinese===
宝拉
你盯着看什么？不夸夸我的斗篷吗？
奥布里
夸。

===Original===
Paula
Couldn’t you wait till I’d gone before reading your letters?
Aubrey
*Putting the letter away.* I beg your pardon.

===Chinese===
宝拉
就不能等我走了再看你那些信吗？
奥布里
*（把信收起。）*请原谅。

===Original===
Paula
Take me downstairs to the carriage. *Slipping her arm through his.* How I tease you! Tomorrow! I’m so happy!
*They go out.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
送我下楼去马车那儿吧。*（挽住他的手臂。）*我多会折腾你！明天！我多幸福！
*（二人同下。）*

## Act II / 第二幕

===Original===
A morning-room in **Aubrey Tanqueray’s** house, “Highercoombe,” near Willowmere, Surrey﻿—a bright and prettily furnished apartment of irregular shape, with double doors opening into a small hall at the back, another door on the left, and a large recessed window through which is obtained a view of extensive grounds. Everything about the room is charming and graceful. The fire is burning in the grate, and a small table is tastefully laid for breakfast. It is a morning in early Spring, and the sun is streaming in through the window.
**Aubrey** and **Paula** are seated at breakfast, and **Aubrey** is silently reading his letters. Two servants, a man and a woman, hand dishes and then retire. After a little while **Aubrey** puts his letters aside and looks across to the window.
Aubrey
Sunshine! Spring!

===Chinese===
萨里郡威洛米尔附近，**奥布里·坦奎雷**的家宅“海厄库姆（Highercoombe）”中的一间晨间起居室——明亮而陈设精美的房间，形状不规则：双重门开向后头的小厅，左侧另有一扇门，一扇内凹的大窗，由此可望见广阔的庭园。房间里的一切都迷人而优美。炉栅里炉火正旺，一张小桌上雅致地摆着早餐。这是早春的一个早晨，阳光正从窗口倾泻进来。
**奥布里**与**宝拉**坐在早餐桌旁，**奥布里**正默读他的信件。一男一女两名仆人上过菜便退下。过了一会儿，**奥布里**把信放到一边，朝窗口望去。
奥布里
阳光！春天！

===Original===
Paula
*Glancing at the clock.* Exactly six minutes.
Aubrey
Six minutes?

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（瞟了一眼时钟。）*整整六分钟。
奥布里
六分钟？

===Original===
Paula
Six minutes, Aubrey dear, since you made your last remark.
Aubrey
I beg your pardon; I was reading my letters. Have you seen Ellean this morning?

===Chinese===
宝拉
亲爱的奥布里，距你上一回开口，已经整整六分钟了。
奥布里
请原谅；我在看信。你今天早上见过埃莉安吗？

===Original===
Paula
*Coldly.* Your last observation but one was about Ellean.
Aubrey
Dearest, what shall I talk about?

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（冷淡地。）*你倒数第二句话说的就是埃莉安。
奥布里
亲爱的，那要我谈什么呢？

===Original===
Paula
Ellean breakfasted two hours ago, Morgan tells me, and then went out walking with her dog.
Aubrey
She wraps up warmly, I hope; this sunshine is deceptive.

===Chinese===
宝拉
摩根（Morgan）告诉我，埃莉安两小时前就用了早餐，然后带着她的狗出去散步了。
奥布里
我希望她穿得够暖；这阳光靠不住。

===Original===
Paula
I ran about the lawn last night, after dinner, in satin shoes. Were you anxious about me?
Aubrey
Certainly.

===Chinese===
宝拉
昨晚饭后，我穿着缎子鞋在草地上跑来跑去。你当时为我担心了吗？
奥布里
当然。

===Original===
Paula
*Melting.* Really?
Aubrey
You make me wretchedly anxious; you delight in doing incautious things. You are incurable.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（心软了。）*真的吗？
奥布里
你让我担心得要命；你就爱做些不小心的事。你真是无可救药。

===Original===
Paula
Ah, what a beast I am! *Going to him and kissing him, then glancing at the letters by his side.* A letter from Cayley?
Aubrey
He is staying very near here, with Mrs.﻿—Very near here.

===Chinese===
宝拉
啊，我多浑啊！*（走到他身边吻他，随后瞟见他手边的信。）*凯利来的信？
奥布里
他就住在离这儿很近的地方，住在——就住在很近的地方。

===Original===
Paula
With the lady whose chimneys we have the honour of contemplating from our windows?
Aubrey
With Mrs. Cortelyon﻿—yes.

===Chinese===
宝拉
住在那位我们有幸从自家窗口瞻望烟囱的女士家？
奥布里
住在科特利恩夫人（Mrs. Cortelyon）家——是的。

===Original===
Paula
Mrs. Cortelyon! The woman who might have set the example of calling on me when we first threw out roots in this deadly-lively soil! Deuce take Mrs. Cortelyon!
Aubrey
Hush! my dear girl!

===Chinese===
宝拉
科特利恩夫人！我们刚在这块死气沉沉又装点热闹的土地上扎根时，本该由她带头来拜访我的！让科特利恩夫人见鬼去吧！
奥布里
嘘！我亲爱的姑娘！

===Original===
Paula
*Returning to her seat.* Oh, I know she’s an old acquaintance of yours﻿—and of the first Mrs. Tanqueray. And she joins the rest of ’em in slapping the second Mrs. Tanqueray in the face. However, I have my revenge﻿—she’s six-and-forty, and I wish nothing worse to happen to any woman.
Aubrey
Well, she’s going to town, Cayley says here, and his visit’s at an end. He’s coming over this morning to call on you. Shall we ask him to transfer himself to us? Do say yes.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（回到座位上。）*哦，我知道她是你的旧相识——也是坦奎雷前夫人的旧相识。她跟其余那些人一样，对着第二任坦奎雷夫人打耳光。不过我有我的报复——她四十六了，我不希望任何女人遭更大的罪。
奥布里
呃，凯利信上说，她要进城了，他这趟做客也到头了。他今天上午要过来看你。咱们请他搬来咱们这儿住好不好？千万说好。

===Original===
Paula
Yes.
Aubrey
*Gladly.* Ah, ha! old Cayley!

===Chinese===
宝拉
好。
奥布里
*（高兴地。）*啊，哈！老凯利！

===Original===
Paula
*Coldly.* He’ll amuse *you*.
Aubrey
And you too.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（冷淡地。）*他会给*你*解闷。
奥布里
也给你解闷。

===Original===
Paula
Because you find a companion, shall I be boisterously hilarious?
Aubrey
Come, come! He talks London, and you know you like that.

===Chinese===
宝拉
因为你找到了伴儿，我就得兴高采烈吗？
奥布里
得了，得了！他满口伦敦的见闻，你知道你喜欢听那些。

===Original===
Paula
London! London or Heaven! which is farther from me!
Aubrey
Paula!

===Chinese===
宝拉
伦敦！伦敦也好，天堂也好！哪个离我更远！
奥布里
宝拉！

===Original===
Paula
Oh! Oh, I am so bored, Aubrey!
Aubrey
*Gathering up his letters and going to her, leaning over her shoulder.* Baby, what can I do for you?

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦！哦，我闷死了，奥布里！
奥布里
*（收起信，走到她跟前，俯身在她肩上。）*宝宝，我能为你做点什么？

===Original===
Paula
I suppose, nothing. You have done all you can for me.
Aubrey
What do you mean?

===Chinese===
宝拉
我想，没什么了。你能为我做的都做了。
奥布里
这话什么意思？

===Original===
Paula
You have married me.
*He walks away from her thoughtfully, to the writing-table. As he places his letters on the table he sees an addressed letter, stamped for the post, lying on the blotting-book; he picks it up.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
你娶了我呀。
*（他若有所思地离开她，走向写字台。他把信放到桌上时，看见吸墨纸板上躺着一封已写好地址、贴好邮票待寄的信；他把它拿了起来。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*In an altered tone.* You’ve been writing this morning before breakfast?
Paula
*Looking at him quickly, then away again.* Er﻿—that letter.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（换了个声调。）*你今天早上早饭前就写信了？
宝拉
*（很快地看了他一眼，又移开视线。）*呃——那封信啊。

===Original===
Aubrey
*With the letter in his hand.* To Lady Orreyed. Why?
Paula
Why not? Mabel’s an old friend of mine.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（手里拿着信。）*写给奥雷耶德夫人的。为什么？
宝拉
为什么不？梅布尔是我的老朋友。

===Original===
Aubrey
Are you﻿—corresponding?
Paula
I heard from her yesterday. They’ve just returned from the Riviera. She seems happy.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你们——一直有通信？
宝拉
我昨天收到她的信。他们刚从里维埃拉（the Riviera）回来。她看起来很幸福。

===Original===
Aubrey
*Sarcastically.* That’s good news.
Paula
Why are you always so cutting about Mabel? She’s a kindhearted girl. Everything’s altered; she even thinks of letting her hair go back to brown. She’s Lady Orreyed. She’s married to George. What’s the matter with her?

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（讥诮地。）*那可是好消息。
宝拉
你为什么总对梅布尔这么刻薄？她是个心地善良的姑娘。一切都变了；她甚至想让头发重新变回棕色。她是奥雷耶德夫人。她嫁给了乔治。她有什么不好？

===Original===
Aubrey
*Turning away.* Oh!
Paula
You drive me mad sometimes with the tone you take about things! Great goodness, if you come to that, George Orreyed’s wife isn’t a bit worse than yours! *He faces her suddenly.* I suppose I needn’t have made that observation.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（转过身去。）*哦！
宝拉
你谈论事情的那副口吻，有时真要把我逼疯！天哪，真要论起来，乔治·奥雷耶德的太太一点也不比你的差！*（他猛地面对她。）*我想这话我原不必说的。

===Original===
Aubrey
No, there was scarcely a necessity.
*He throws the letter on to the table, and takes up the newspaper.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
是啊，实在没有必要。
*（他把信扔到桌上，拿起报纸。）*

===Original===
Paula
I am very sorry.
Aubrey
All right, dear.

===Chinese===
宝拉
我很抱歉。
奥布里
没什么，亲爱的。

===Original===
Paula
*Trifling with the letter.* I﻿—I’d better tell you what I’ve written. I meant to do so, of course. I﻿—I’ve asked the Orreyeds to come and stay with us. *He looks at her and lets the paper fall to the ground in a helpless way.* George was a great friend of Cayley’s; I’m sure *he* would be delighted to meet them here.
Aubrey
*Laughing mirthlessly.* Ha, ha, ha! They say Orreyed has taken to tippling at dinner. Heavens above!

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（摆弄着那封信。）*我——我还是告诉你我写了什么吧。我本来就打算说的。我——我请奥雷耶德夫妇来咱们家住些日子。*（他望着她，无可奈何地任报纸落到地上。）*乔治是凯利的好友；我肯定*他*一定乐意在这儿见到他们。
奥布里
*（笑得无欢。）*哈，哈，哈！听说奥雷耶德正餐时都灌上酒了。老天爷！

===Original===
Paula
Oh! I’ve no patience with you! You’ll kill me with this life! *She selects some flowers from a vase on the table, cuts and arranges them, and fastens them in her bodice.* What is my existence, Sunday to Saturday? In the morning, a drive down to the village, with the groom, to give my orders to the tradespeople. At lunch, you and Ellean. In the afternoon, a novel, the newspapers; if fine, another drive﻿—*if* fine! Tea﻿—you and Ellean. Then two hours of dusk; then dinner﻿—you and Ellean. Then a game of Bésique, you and I, while Ellean reads a religious book in a dull corner. Then a yawn from me, another from you, a sigh from Ellean; three figures suddenly rise﻿—“Good night, good night, good night!” *Imitating a kiss.* “God bless you!” Ah!
Aubrey
Yes, yes, Paula﻿—yes, dearest﻿—that’s what it is *now*. But, by-and-by, if people begin to come round us﻿—

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦！我对你真没耐心！这种日子会把我憋死的！*（她从桌上的花瓶里挑出几朵花，修剪插好，别在胸前衣襟上。）*我过的算什么日子，从星期天到星期六？早上，由马夫陪着坐车下山，到村里向商户们吩咐事宜。午饭时，是你和埃莉安。下午，一本小说，几份报纸；天气好就再坐一趟车——*要是*天气好！茶点——你和埃莉安。然后两个钟头的暮色；然后是晚饭——你和埃莉安。然后你我玩一局比齐克牌（Bésique），埃莉安在阴沉的角落里读一本宗教书。然后我打个哈欠，你再打一个，埃莉安叹口气；三条人影蓦地立起来——“晚安，晚安，晚安！”*（学了一个亲吻的声音。）*“上帝保佑你！”啊！
奥布里
是，是，宝拉——是的，亲爱的——*眼下*是这样。可是往后，要是人们开始到我们周围来——

===Original===
Paula
Hah! That’s where we’ve made the mistake, my friend Aubrey! *Pointing to the window.* Do you believe these people will *ever* come round us? Your former crony, Mrs. Cortelyon? Or the grim old vicar, or that wife of his whose huge nose is positively indecent? Or the Ullathornes, or the Gollans, or Lady William Petres? I know better! And when the young ones gradually take the place of the old, there will still remain the sacred tradition that the dreadful person who lives at the top of the hill is never, under any circumstances, to be called upon! And so we shall go on here, year in and year out, until the sap is run out of our lives, and we’re stale and dry and withered from sheer, solitary respectability. Upon my word, I wonder we didn’t see that we should have been far happier if we’d gone in for the devil-may-care, café-living sort of life in town! After all, *I* have a set and you might have joined it. It’s true I did want, dearly, dearly, to be a married woman, but where’s the pride in being a married woman among married women who are﻿—married! If﻿—*Seeing that **Aubrey’s** head has sunk into his hands.* Aubrey! My dear boy! You’re not﻿—crying?
*He looks up, with a flushed face. **Ellean** enters, dressed very simply for walking. She is a low voiced, grave girl of about nineteen, with a face somewhat resembling a Madonna. Towards **Paula** her manner is cold and distant.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
哈！错就错在这儿，我的朋友奥布里！*（指着窗。）*你相信这些人*会*到我们周围来吗？你从前那位密友科特利恩夫人？还是那位面目阴沉的老牧师，或是他那鼻子大得实在有伤风化的太太？还是尤拉索恩一家、戈兰一家、威廉·皮特里斯夫人（Lady William Petres）？我心里有数！等年轻一辈渐渐接了老一辈的位置，那神圣的传统依然会留着：住在山顶上的那个可怕人物，无论如何都不可以去拜访！于是我们就这么一年又一年过下去，直到生命的汁液流干，我们凭着这纯粹、孤高的体面，变得陈腐、干枯、萎谢。说真的，我纳闷咱们当初怎么没看出来：要是咱们在城里过那种无所顾忌、以咖啡馆为家的日子，反倒会快活得多！说到底，*我*原是有自己一圈人的，你满可以加入进来。不错，我确实一心一意、一心一意想做个有夫之妇，可在一群——正正经经结了婚的太太们中间做个已婚女人，体面又在哪儿呢！要是——*（看见**奥布里**的头已埋进双手。）*奥布里！我亲爱的！你不会是——在哭吧？
*（他抬起头，满脸涨红。**埃莉安**上，穿着十分朴素的散步装。她是个约莫十九岁的姑娘，说话低声、神色庄重，面貌有几分像圣母像。对**宝拉**，她的态度冷淡疏远。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*In an undertone.* Ellean!
Ellean
Good morning, papa. Good morning, Paula.
***Paula** puts her arms round **Ellean** and kisses her. **Ellean** makes little response.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（低声。）*埃莉安！
埃莉安
早上好，爸爸。早上好，宝拉。
*（**宝拉**搂住**埃莉安**吻她。**埃莉安**几乎没有回应。）*

===Original===
Paula
Good morning. *Brightly.* We’ve been breakfasting this side of the house, to get the sun.
*She sits at the piano and rattles at a gay melody. Seeing that **Paula’s** back is turned to them, **Ellean** goes to **Aubrey** and kisses him; he returns the kiss almost furtively. As they separate, the servants reenter, and proceed to carry out the breakfast-table.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
早上好。*（轻快地。）*我们一直在房子这一侧用早餐，为的是晒着太阳。
*（她坐到钢琴前，叮叮咚咚弹起一支欢快的曲子。趁**宝拉**背对着他们，**埃莉安**走到**奥布里**跟前吻他；他近乎悄悄地回了她一吻。二人分开时，仆人们又进来，动手把早餐桌撤出去。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*To **Ellean**.* I guess where you’ve been: there’s some gorse clinging to your frock.
Ellean
*Removing a sprig of gorse from her skirt.* Rover and I walked nearly as far as Black Moor. The poor fellow has a thorn in his pad; I am going upstairs for my tweezers.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（对**埃莉安**。）*我猜到你去了哪儿：你裙子上还挂着荆豆呢。
埃莉安
*（从裙上摘下一小枝荆豆。）*罗弗（Rover）和我差不多走到了黑荒原（Black Moor）。这可怜的家伙脚垫里扎了根刺；我上楼去拿镊子。

===Original===
Aubrey
Ellean! *She returns to him.* Paula is a little depressed﻿—out of sorts. She complains that she has no companion.
Ellean
I am with Paula nearly all the day, papa.

===Chinese===
奥布里
埃莉安！*（她回到他身边。）*宝拉有点情绪低落——不大舒坦。她抱怨身边没有伴儿。
埃莉安
我几乎整天都陪着宝拉呀，爸爸。

===Original===
Aubrey
Ah, but you’re such a little mouse. Paula likes cheerful people about her.
Ellean
I’m afraid I am naturally rather silent; and it’s so difficult to seem to be what one is not.

===Chinese===
奥布里
啊，可你太安静了，像只小老鼠。宝拉喜欢身边围着快活的人。
埃莉安
恐怕我天生就不爱说话；要装出另一个样子，实在很难。

===Original===
Aubrey
I don’t wish that, Ellean.
Ellean
I will offer to go down to the village with Paula this morning﻿—shall I?

===Chinese===
奥布里
我并不要你装，埃莉安。
埃莉安
今天上午我主动提出陪宝拉坐车去村里——好吗？

===Original===
Aubrey
*Touching her hand gently.* Thank you﻿—do.
Ellean
When I’ve looked after Rover, I’ll come back to her.
*She goes out; **Paula** ceases playing, and turns on the music-stool looking at **Aubrey**.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（轻轻碰了碰她的手。）*谢谢你——去吧。
埃莉安
等我照看好罗弗，我就回到她那儿去。
*（她下；**宝拉**停止弹奏，在琴凳上转过身来望着**奥布里**。）*

===Original===
Paula
Well, have you and Ellean had your little confidence?
Aubrey
Confidence?

===Chinese===
宝拉
怎么样，你跟埃莉安说完体己话了？
奥布里
体己话？

===Original===
Paula
Do you think I couldn’t feel it, like a pain between my shoulders?
Aubrey
Ellean is coming back in a few minutes to be with you. *Bending over her.* Paula, Paula dear, is this how you keep your promise?

===Chinese===
宝拉
你以为我感觉不出来吗，像两片肩胛之间的一阵隐痛？
奥布里
埃莉安几分钟后就回来陪你。*（俯身向她。）*宝拉，亲爱的宝拉，你就是这么守约的吗？

===Original===
Paula
Oh! *Rising impatiently and crossing swiftly to the settee, where she sits, moving restlessly.* I *can’t* keep my promise; I *am* jealous; it won’t be smothered. I see you looking at her, watching her; your voice drops when you speak to her. I know how fond you are of that girl, Aubrey.
Aubrey
What would you have? I’ve no other home for her. She is my daughter.

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦！*（不耐烦地起身，快步走到长椅那儿坐下，烦躁不安地动来动去。）*我*没法*守约；我*就是*在妒忌；这压不下去。我看见你望着她、留神她；你对她说话时嗓音都会放低。我知道你多疼那个姑娘，奥布里。
奥布里
那你要我怎么样？我别无地方安顿她。她是我女儿。

===Original===
Paula
She is your saint. Saint Ellean!
Aubrey
You have often told me how good and sweet you think her.

===Chinese===
宝拉
她是你的圣人。圣埃莉安！
奥布里
你不是常说，你觉得她多么善良、多么可爱吗。

===Original===
Paula
Good!﻿—yes! Do you imagine *that* makes me less jealous? *Going to him and clinging to his arm.* Aubrey, there are two sorts of affection﻿—the love for a woman you respect, and the love for a woman you﻿—love. She gets the first from you: I never can.
Aubrey
Hush, hush! you don’t realise what you say.

===Chinese===
宝拉
善良！——是啊！你以为*这样*我就不那么妒忌了？*（走到他身边，攀住他的手臂。）*奥布里，爱有两种——对一位你所敬重的女人的爱，和对一位你所——爱的女人的爱。她从你那里得到的是第一种：我却永远得不到。
奥布里
嘘，嘘！你自己不知道自己在说什么。

===Original===
Paula
If Ellean cared for me only a little, it would be different. I shouldn’t be jealous then. Why doesn’t she care for me?
Aubrey
She﻿—she﻿—she will, in time.

===Chinese===
宝拉
只要埃莉安对我有一点点心意，就不一样了。那我也就不妒忌了。她为什么对我没有心意？
奥布里
她——她——她会的，慢慢来。

===Original===
Paula
You can’t say that without stuttering.
Aubrey
Her disposition seems a little unresponsive; she resembles her mother in many ways; I can see it every day.

===Chinese===
宝拉
你说这话都结巴了。
奥布里
她的性子似乎有点冷淡寡情；她在许多地方像她母亲；我每天都看得出。

===Original===
Paula
She’s marble. It’s a shame. There’s not the slightest excuse; for all she knows, I’m as much a saint as she﻿—only married. Dearest, help me to win her over!
Aubrey
Help you?

===Chinese===
宝拉
她是大理石。这不像话。完全没有一点理由；在她知道的范围内，我和她一样是圣人——只不过结了婚。亲爱的，帮我把她争取过来！
奥布里
帮你？

===Original===
Paula
You can. Teach her that it is her duty to love me; she hangs on to every word you speak. I’m sure, Aubrey, that the love of a nice woman who believed me to be like herself would do me a world of good. You’d get the benefit of it as well as I. It would soothe me; it would make me less horribly restless; it would take this﻿—this﻿—mischievous feeling from me. *Coaxingly.* Aubrey!
Aubrey
Have patience; everything will come right.

===Chinese===
宝拉
你可以的。教她明白，爱我乃是她的本分；你说的每一个字她都记在心里。我确信，奥布里，一位善良女子的爱——当她相信我和她一样善良时——会给我莫大的好处。你我都会从中受益。它会抚慰我；会让我不再那么可怕地躁动不安；会把我这股——这股——恶念从我身上带走。*（哄诱地。）*奥布里！
奥布里
耐着性子；一切都会好起来的。

===Original===
Paula
Yes, if you help me.
Aubrey
In the meantime you will tear up your letter to Lady Orreyed, won’t you?

===Chinese===
宝拉
是啊，只要你帮我。
奥布里
那么，你会把写给奥雷耶德夫人的信撕掉，对吧？

===Original===
Paula
*Kissing his hand.* Of course I will﻿—anything!
Aubrey
Ah, thank you, dearest! *Laughing.* Why, good gracious!﻿—ha, ha!﻿—just imagine “Saint Ellean” and that woman side by side!

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（吻他的手。）*我当然会——什么都依你！
奥布里
啊，谢谢你，亲爱的！*（笑。）*哎呀，我的天！——哈，哈！——想想看，“圣埃莉安”跟那个女人肩并肩坐在一起！

===Original===
Paula
*Going back with a cry.* Ah!
Aubrey
What?

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（惊叫着退开。）*啊！
奥布里
怎么了？

===Original===
Paula
*Passionately.* It’s Ellean you’re considering, not me? It’s all Ellean with you! Ellean! Ellean!
***Ellean** reenters.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（激愤地。）*你顾虑的是埃莉安，不是我，对不对？你心里全是埃莉安！埃莉安！埃莉安！
*（**埃莉安**重新上。）*

===Original===
Ellean
Did you call me, Paula? *Clenching his hands, **Aubrey** turns away and goes out.* Is papa angry?
Paula
I drive him distracted sometimes. There, I confess it!

===Chinese===
埃莉安
你叫我了吗，宝拉？*（**奥布里**攥紧双手，转身走出。）*爸爸生气了吗？
宝拉
我有时把他逼得心烦意乱。好吧，我认了！

===Original===
Ellean
Do you? Oh, why do you?
Paula
Because I﻿—because I’m jealous.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
是吗？哦，为什么要这样？
宝拉
因为我——因为我在妒忌。

===Original===
Ellean
Jealous?
Paula
Yes﻿—of you. ***Ellean** is silent.* Well, what do you think of that?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
妒忌？
宝拉
是的——妒忌你。*（**埃莉安**沉默。）*怎么样，你怎么想？

===Original===
Ellean
I knew it; I’ve seen it. It hurts me dreadfully. What do you wish me to do? Go away?
Paula
Leave us! *Beckoning her with a motion of the head.* Look here! ***Ellean** goes to **Paula** slowly and unresponsively.* You could cure me of my jealousy very easily. Why don’t you﻿—like me?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
我早知道；我看出来了。这让我难过极了。你要我怎么做？走开吗？
宝拉
走开倒不必！*（朝她偏了偏头，示意她过来。）*你过来！*（**埃莉安**慢慢走过去，神情淡漠。）*你本可以轻而易举地治好我的妒忌。你为什么——不喜欢我呢？

===Original===
Ellean
What do you mean by﻿—like you? I don’t understand.
Paula
Love me.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
您说的——喜欢您，是什么意思？我不明白。
宝拉
爱我。

===Original===
Ellean
Love is not a feeling that is under one’s control. I shall alter as time goes on, perhaps. I didn’t begin to love my father deeply till a few months ago, and then I obeyed my mother.
Paula
Ah, yes, you dream things, don’t you﻿—see them in your sleep? You fancy your mother speaks to you?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
爱不是一种可以由人支配的感情。也许随着时间推移，我会变的。就在几个月前，我才开始深深地爱我的父亲，而那是遵从了我母亲的意愿。
宝拉
啊，是啊，你会梦见一些事，对吧——睡梦里看见它们？你想象你母亲对你说话？

===Original===
Ellean
When you have lost your mother it is a comfort to believe that she is dead only to this life, that she still watches over her child. I do believe that of my mother.
Paula
Well, and so you haven’t been bidden to love *me*?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
失去了母亲的人，若能相信她只是告别了今生，相信她仍在护佑自己的孩子，是一种安慰。对我的母亲，我正是这样相信的。
宝拉
那么，没有人嘱咐你要爱*我*了？

===Original===
Ellean
*After a pause, almost inaudibly.* No.
Paula
Dreams are only a hash-up of one’s day-thoughts, I suppose you know. Think intently of anything, and it’s bound to come back to you at night. I don’t cultivate dreams myself.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（停顿片刻，声音低得几乎听不见。）*没有。
宝拉
梦不过是白天心思的杂烩，这个想必你知道。白天念念不忘什么，夜里它准会回来。我自己可不栽培梦。

===Original===
Ellean
Ah, I knew you would only sneer!
Paula
I’m not sneering; I’m speaking the truth. I say that if you cared for me in the daytime I should soon make friends with those nightmares of yours. Ellean, why don’t you try to look on me as your second mother? Of course there are not many years between us, but I’m ever so much older than you﻿—in experience. I shall have no children of my own, I know that; it would be a real comfort to me if you would make me feel we belonged to each other. Won’t you? Perhaps you think I’m odd﻿—not nice. Well, the fact is I’ve two sides to my nature, and I’ve let the one almost smother the other. A few years ago I went through some trouble, and since then I haven’t shed a tear. I believe if you put your arms round me just once I should run upstairs and have a good cry. There, I’ve talked to you as I’ve never talked to a woman in my life. Ellean, you seem to fear me. Don’t! Kiss me!
*With a cry, almost of despair, **Ellean** turns from **Paula** and sinks on to the settee, covering her face with her hands.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
啊，我就知道您只会讥笑！
宝拉
我没有讥笑；我说的是实话。我要说，只要你白天肯喜欢我，你那些噩梦很快就会跟我交上朋友。埃莉安，你为什么不试着把我当作你的第二个母亲？当然，我们年纪相差不大，可我比你想的大得多——论阅历。我自己不会再有孩子了，这一点我知道；倘若你肯让我觉得我们彼此相属，那对我真是实实在在的安慰。你肯吗？也许你觉得我古怪——不讨人喜欢。唉，实情是，我的天性有两面，我让其中一面几乎把另一面闷死了。几年前我经受过一场磨难，打那以后我再没掉过一滴眼泪。我相信，只要你搂抱我一次，我就会跑上楼去痛痛快快哭一场。瞧，我跟你说的话，是我这辈子从没对任何女人说过的。埃莉安，你好像怕我。别怕！吻我！
*（**埃莉安**叫了一声，近乎绝望地从**宝拉**身边转过身去，跌坐在长椅上，双手掩住脸。）*

===Original===
Paula
*Indignantly.* Oh! Why is it! How dare you treat me like this? What do you mean by it? What do you mean?
*A **Servant** enters.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（愤然。）*哦！这是为什么！你怎么敢这样对我？你这是什么意思？你到底是什么意思？
*（一**仆人**上。）*

===Original===
Servant
Mr. Drummle, ma’am.
***Cayley Drummle**, in riding dress, enters briskly.*
*The **Servant** retires.*

===Chinese===
仆人
德鲁姆尔先生到，夫人。
*（**凯利·德鲁姆尔**穿着骑装，轻快地上。）*
*（**仆人**退下。）*

===Original===
Paula
*Recovering herself.* Well, Cayley!
Drummle
*Shaking hands with her cordially.* How are you? *Shaking hands with **Ellean**, who rises.* I saw you in the distance an hour ago, in the gorse near Stapleton’s.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（定下神来。）*好啊，凯利！
德鲁姆尔
*（热诚地与她握手。）*你好吗？*（与起身的**埃莉安**握手。）*一个钟头前我远远看见你了，在斯台普顿家（Stapleton’s）附近的荆豆丛边。

===Original===
Ellean
I didn’t see you, Mr. Drummle.
Drummle
My dear Ellean, it is my experience that no charming young lady of nineteen ever does see a man of forty-five. *Laughing.* Ha, Ha!

===Chinese===
埃莉安
我没看见您，德鲁姆尔先生。
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的埃莉安，凭我的经验，凡是十九岁的动人少女，从来是看不见四十五岁的男人的。*（笑。）*哈，哈！

===Original===
Ellean
*Going to the door.* Paula, papa wishes me to drive down to the village with you this morning. Do you care to take me?
Paula
*Coldly.* Oh, by all means. Pray tell Watts to balance the cart for three.
***Ellean** goes out.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（向门口走去。）*宝拉，爸爸要我今天上午陪你坐车去村里。你愿意带我去吗？
宝拉
*（冷淡地。）*哦，当然愿意。请吩咐瓦茨（Watts）把马车套成三人座。
*（**埃莉安**下。）*

===Original===
Drummle
How’s Aubrey?
Paula
Very well﻿—when Ellean’s about the house.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
奥布里怎么样？
宝拉
很好——只要埃莉安在屋里。

===Original===
Drummle
And you? I needn’t ask.
Paula
*Walking away to the window.* Oh, a dog’s life, my dear Cayley, mine.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
你呢？这我倒不用问了。
宝拉
*（走开到窗边。）*哦，狗过的日子，我亲爱的凯利，我过的。

===Original===
Drummle
Eh?
Paula
Doesn’t that define a happy marriage? I’m sleek, well-kept, well-fed, never without a bone to gnaw and fresh straw to lie upon. *Gazing out of the window.* Oh, dear me!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
嗯？
宝拉
这难道不正是美满婚姻的定义？我毛色光亮，被照料得好好的，吃得饱饱的，从来不缺一根啃的骨头、一张卧的新草垫。*（凝望窗外。）*哎呀，天哪！

===Original===
Drummle
H’m! Well, I heartily congratulate you on your kennel. The view from the terrace here is superb.
Paula
Yes, I can see London.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
唔！那么，我衷心祝贺你有这么一所上好的狗窝。这儿露台上的景致真是壮丽。
宝拉
是啊，我连伦敦都看得见。

===Original===
Drummle
London! Not quite so far, surely?
Paula
*I* can. Also the Mediterranean, on a fine day. I wonder what Algiers looks like this morning from the sea! *Impulsively.* Oh, Cayley, do you remember those jolly times on board Peter Jarman’s yacht when we lay off﻿—? *Stopping suddenly, seeing **Drummle** staring at her.* Good gracious! What are we talking about!
***Aubrey** enters.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
伦敦！总不至于那么远吧？
宝拉
*我*就看得见。天气好时，还看得见地中海。不知今天早晨从海上望阿尔及尔（Algiers）是什么样子！*（冲动地。）*哦，凯利，你还记得在彼得·贾曼的游艇上那些快活日子吗，我们停在——*（突然打住，看见**德鲁姆尔**直瞪着她。）*天哪！咱们在说什么呢！
*（**奥布里**上。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*To **Drummle**.* Dear old chap! Has Paula asked you?
Paula
Not yet.

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（对**德鲁姆尔**。）*亲爱的老伙计！宝拉跟你说了吗？
宝拉
还没有。

===Original===
Aubrey
We want you to come to us, now that you’re leaving Mrs. Cortelyon﻿—at once, today. Stay a month, as long as you please﻿—eh, Paula?
Paula
As long as you can possibly endure it﻿—do, Cayley.

===Chinese===
奥布里
我们想请你搬到我们这儿来，既然你要离开科特利恩夫人家了——马上，今天就搬。住上一个月，爱住多久住多久——嗯，宝拉？
宝拉
只要你受得了，住多久都行——来吧，凯利。

===Original===
Drummle
*Looking at **Aubrey**.* Delighted. *To **Paula**.* Charming of you to have me.
Paula
My dear man, you’re a blessing. I must telegraph to London for more fish! A strange appetite to cater for! Something to do, to do, to do!
*She goes out in a mood of almost childish delight.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（望着**奥布里**。）*太乐意了。*（对**宝拉**。）*承蒙相邀，不胜荣幸。
宝拉
我亲爱的好人，你真是天赐的福分。我得打电报到伦敦再订些鱼来！要伺候的口味可真稀奇！总算有点事做，做点事，做点事！
*（她怀着近乎孩子气的欢喜下。）*

===Original===
Drummle
*Eyeing **Aubrey**.* Well?
Aubrey
*With a wearied, anxious look.* Well, Cayley?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（打量**奥布里**。）*怎么样？
奥布里
*（带着疲惫忧虑的神色。）*怎么样，凯利？

===Original===
Drummle
How are you getting on?
Aubrey
My position doesn’t grow less difficult. I told you, when I met you last week, of this feverish, jealous attachment of Paula’s for Ellean?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
日子过得如何？
奥布里
我的处境没有变轻松。上周见到你时，我跟你说过宝拉对埃莉安那种狂热的、妒忌的依恋吧？

===Original===
Drummle
Yes. I hardly know why, but I came to the conclusion that you don’t consider it an altogether fortunate attachment.
Aubrey
Ellean doesn’t respond to it.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
说过了。不知怎么，我得出的结论是：你并不认为这种依恋完全是件幸事。
奥布里
埃莉安对此并无回应。

===Original===
Drummle
These are early days. Ellean will warm towards your wife by-and-by.
Aubrey
Ah, but there’s the question, Cayley!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
日子还早呢。往后埃莉安会对你太太热络起来的。
奥布里
啊，可问题恰恰在这儿，凯利！

===Original===
