Drummle
What question?
Aubrey
The question which positively distracts me. Ellean is so different from﻿—most women; I don’t believe a purer creature exists out of heaven. And I﻿—I ask myself, am I doing right in exposing her to the influence of poor Paula’s light, careless nature?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
什么问题？
奥布里
那个简直把我折磨疯了的问题。埃莉安与大多数女人太不一样了；我相信天上之下再没有比她更纯洁的人。而我——我扪心自问：让她受着可怜的宝拉那轻浮、草率的天性的熏染，我做对了吗？

===Original===
Drummle
My dear Aubrey!
Aubrey
That shocks you! So it does me. I assure you I long to urge my girl to break down the reserve which keeps her apart from Paula, but somehow I can’t do it﻿—well, I don’t do it. How can I make you understand? But when you come to us you’ll understand quickly enough. Cayley, there’s hardly a subject you can broach on which poor Paula hasn’t some strange, out-of-the-way thought to give utterance to; some curious, warped notion. They are not mere worldly thoughts﻿—unless, good God! they belong to the little hellish world which our blackguardism has created: no, her ideas have too little calculation in them to be called worldly. But it makes it the more dreadful that such thoughts should be ready, spontaneous; that expressing them has become a perfectly natural process; that her words, acts even, have almost lost their proper significance for her, and seem beyond her control. Ah, and the pain of listening to it all from the woman one loves, the woman one hoped to make happy and contented, who is really and truly a good woman, as it were, maimed! Well, this is my burden, and I shouldn’t speak to you of it but for my anxiety about Ellean. Ellean! What is to be her future? It is in my hands; what am I to do? Cayley, when I remember how Ellean comes to me, from another world I always think, when I realise the charge that’s laid on me, I find myself wishing, in a sort of terror, that my child were safe under the ground!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的奥布里！
奥布里
这让你震惊！也让我自己震惊。我向你保证，我渴望催促我的女儿打破那份使她疏远宝拉的矜持，可不知怎么，我做不到——唉，就是没有做。怎样才能让你明白？等你住到我们家，你很快就会明白的。凯利，几乎随便提起什么话题，可怜的宝拉都有一套古怪的、偏门的想法要发表；一些乖僻的、扭曲的念头。那些也不只是世俗的念头——除非，天哪！它们属于我们这帮恶棍造出来的那个小小的地狱世界：不，她的想法里算计的成分太少，还称不上世俗。可正因如此才更可怕：这样的念头竟是张口就来、不假思索；说出它们已成了全然自然的过程；她的话语、甚至行为，对她自己几乎失去了本来的分量，仿佛不受她的控制。唉，听着这一切从你所爱的女人口中说出——你本指望让她幸福满足的女人，一个真正善良的女人，却像被生生残害了——这是何等的痛楚！好吧，这就是我的重负，若不是为埃莉安焦虑，我也不会对你讲这些。埃莉安！她的将来会怎样？它就攥在我手里；我该怎么办？凯利，每当我想起埃莉安怎样来到我身边——我总觉得她来自另一个世界——每当我意识到落在自己肩上的责任，我就会在一种恐惧中暗自希望，我的孩子还是安睡在地下才好！

===Original===
Drummle
My dear Aubrey, aren’t you making a mistake?
Aubrey
Very likely. What is it?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的奥布里，你莫不是弄错了？
奥布里
很可能。错在哪儿？

===Original===
Drummle
A mistake, not in regarding your Ellean as an angel, but in believing that, under any circumstances, it would be possible for her to go through life without getting her white robe﻿—shall we say, a little dusty at the hem? Don’t take me for a cynic. I am sure there are many women upon earth who are almost divinely innocent; but being on earth, they must send their robes to the laundry occasionally. Ah, and it’s right that they should have to do so, for what can they learn from the checking of their little washing-bills but lessons of charity? Now I see but two courses open to you for the disposal of your angel.
Aubrey
Yes?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
错不在把你的埃莉安看作天使，而在于相信她竟能在任何情形下走完一生，而那件白袍子——我们不妨说——裙裾上不沾一点尘土。别把我当愤世嫉俗的人。我确信世上有许多几乎圣洁无瑕的女子；可既在尘世，她们总免不了时不时把袍子送去洗涮。而她们非得如此不可，这也是对的，因为除了学会宽容，她们还能从核对那张小小的洗衣单里学到什么呢？依我看，处置你的天使，路子只有两条。
奥布里
哪两条？

===Original===
Drummle
You must either restrict her to a paradise which is, like every earthly paradise, necessarily somewhat imperfect, or treat her as an ordinary flesh-and-blood young woman, and give her the advantages of that society to which she properly belongs.
Aubrey
Advantages?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
你要么把她关在一座乐园里——那乐园同尘世一切乐园一样，必然是不甚完美的；要么就把她当作一个有血有肉的寻常少女，让她享有她本来所属的那个社交圈子的种种好处。
奥布里
好处？

===Original===
Drummle
My dear Aubrey, of all forms of innocence mere ignorance is the least admirable. Take my advice, let her walk and talk and suffer and be healed with the great crowd. Do it, and hope that she’ll some day meet a good, honest fellow who’ll make her life complete, happy, secure. Now you see what I’m driving at.
Aubrey
A sanguine programme, my dear Cayley! Oh, I’m not pooh-poohing it. Putting sentiment aside, of course I know that a fortunate marriage for Ellean would be the best﻿—perhaps the only﻿—solution of my difficulty. But you forget the danger of the course you suggest.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的奥布里，在一切形态的无辜之中，单单无知这一种最不足称道。听我一句劝：让她到大人群里去行走、交谈、受伤、再痊愈吧。这样做了，再指望她有朝一日遇到一个善良诚实的青年，让她的生命完整、幸福、安稳。现在你明白我的用意了。
奥布里
好一幅乐观的蓝图，我亲爱的凯利！哦，我并非嗤之以鼻。把感情放一边，我当然明白，埃莉安若能结下一门好亲事，是解决我难题的最好办法——也许是唯一的办法。可你忘了你所建议的这条路子里的危险。

===Original===
Drummle
Danger?
Aubrey
If Ellean goes among men and women, how can she escape from learning, sooner or later, the history of﻿—poor Paula’s﻿—old life?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
危险？
奥布里
埃莉安一旦进入男女社交之中，她怎能逃得开，或迟或早，总有人会让她知道——可怜的宝拉——那段旧日生活的历史？

===Original===
Drummle
H’m! You remember the episode of the Jeweller’s Son in the *Arabian Nights*? Of course you don’t. Well, if your daughter lives, she *can’t* escape﻿—what you’re afraid of. ***Aubrey** gives a half stifled exclamation of pain.* And when she does hear the story, surely it would be better that she should have some knowledge of the world to help her to understand it.
Aubrey
To understand!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
唔！你记得《一千零一夜》（*Arabian Nights*）里珠宝商之子的故事吗？你当然不记得了。唉，只要你的女儿活着，她就*逃不脱*——你所害怕的那件事。*（**奥布里**发出一声半压住的痛苦的惊呼。）*等她真听到那段往事时，她若对世事有些见识，能帮她理解它，岂不更好。
奥布里
理解它！

===Original===
Drummle
To understand, to﻿—to philosophise.
Aubrey
To philosophise?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
理解它，再——再对它作哲思。
奥布里
作哲思？

===Original===
Drummle
Philosophy is toleration, and it is only one step from toleration to forgiveness.
Aubrey
You’re right, Cayley; I believe you always are. Yes, yes. But, even if I had the courage to attempt to solve the problem of Ellean’s future in this way, I﻿—I’m helpless.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
哲思即是宽容，而从宽容到宽恕，不过一步之遥。
奥布里
你说得对，凯利；我相信你永远是对的。是，是。可纵然我有胆量这样去解决埃莉安的前途问题，我——我也无能为力。

===Original===
Drummle
How?
Aubrey
What means have I now of placing my daughter in the world I’ve left?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
怎么会？
奥布里
如今我还有什么办法，把我的女儿送进那个我已经离开了的世界？

===Original===
Drummle
Oh, some friend﻿—some woman friend.
Aubrey
I have none; they’re gone.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
哦，找个朋友——某位女性朋友。
奥布里
我一个也没有；她们都散了。

===Original===
Drummle
You’re wrong there; I know one﻿—
Aubrey
*Listening.* That’s Paula’s cart. Let’s discuss this again.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
那你可说错了；我认识一位——
奥布里
*（倾听。）*那是宝拉的马车。这事我们回头再谈。

===Original===
Drummle
*Going up to the window and looking out.* It isn’t the dogcart. *Turning to **Aubrey**.* I hope you’ll forgive me, old chap.
Aubrey
What for?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（走到窗前望出去。）*不是那辆轻便马车。*（转向**奥布里**。）*希望你原谅我，老伙计。
奥布里
原谅什么？

===Original===
Drummle
Whose wheels do you think have been cutting ruts in your immaculate drive?
*A **Servant** enters.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
你猜是谁的车轮，在你那一尘不染的车道上压出了辙印？
*（一**仆人**上。）*

===Original===
Servant
*To **Aubrey**.* Mrs. Cortelyon, sir.
Aubrey
Mrs. Cortelyon! *After a short pause.* Very well. *The **Servant** withdraws.* What on earth is the meaning of this?

===Chinese===
仆人
*（对**奥布里**。）*科特利恩夫人到，先生。
奥布里
科特利恩夫人！*（略停。）*好。*（**仆人**退下。）*这到底是怎么回事？

===Original===
Drummle
Ahem! While I’ve been our old friend’s guest, Aubrey, we have very naturally talked a good deal about you and yours.
Aubrey
Indeed, have you?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
咳！我住在咱们老朋友家做客的这些日子，奥布里，我们自然谈了不少关于你和你家人的事。
奥布里
是吗，谈了？

===Original===
Drummle
Yes, and Alice Cortelyon has arrived at the conclusion that it would have been far kinder had she called on Mrs. Tanqueray long ago. She’s going abroad for Easter before settling down in London for the season, and I believe she has come over this morning to ask for Ellean’s companionship.
Aubrey
Oh, I see! *Frowning.* Quite a friendly little conspiracy, my dear Cayley!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
谈了，而且爱丽丝·科特利恩（Alice Cortelyon）得出了结论：倘若她早些来拜访坦奎雷夫人，那才是厚道得多。她复活节要出国，之后再回伦敦安顿下来过社交季，我相信她今天上午过来，是想请埃莉安做伴。
奥布里
哦，我明白了！*（皱眉。）*好一场友善的小阴谋，我亲爱的凯利！

===Original===
Drummle
Conspiracy! Not at all, I assure you. *Laughing.* Ha, ha!
***Ellean** enters from the hall with **Mrs. Cortelyon**, a handsome, good humoured, spirited woman of about forty-five.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
阴谋！绝不是，我向你保证。*（笑。）*哈，哈！
*（**埃莉安**与**科特利恩夫人**从厅里上；她是位约四十五岁的女子，俊朗、和气、有精神。）*

===Original===
Ellean
Papa﻿—
Mrs. Cortelyon
*To **Aubrey**, shaking hands with him heartily.* Well, Aubrey, how are you? I’ve just been telling this great girl of yours that I knew her when she was a sad-faced, pale baby. How is Mrs. Tanqueray? I have been a bad neighbour, and I’m here to beg forgiveness. Is she indoors?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
爸爸——
科特利恩夫人
*（对**奥布里**，热诚地与他握手。）*你好啊，奥布里，身体怎么样？我刚才正跟你的大姑娘说，她还是个哭丧着脸的苍白小婴孩时我就认得她了。坦奎雷夫人好吗？我一直算不得个好街坊，今天就是来求恕罪的。她在家吗？

===Original===
Aubrey
She’s upstairs putting on a hat, I believe.
Mrs. Cortelyon
*Sitting comfortably.* Ah! *She looks round: **Drummle** and **Ellean** are talking together in the hall.* We used to be very frank with each other, Aubrey. I suppose the old footing is no longer possible, eh?

===Chinese===
奥布里
我记得她在楼上戴帽子。
科特利恩夫人
*（舒舒服服地坐下。）*啊！*（她环顾四周：**德鲁姆尔**和**埃莉安**正在厅里说话。）*我们从前彼此可是无话不谈的，奥布里。我猜从前的老交情是再难恢复了，嗯？

===Original===
Aubrey
If so, I’m not entirely to blame, Mrs. Cortelyon.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Mrs. Cortelyon? H’m! No, I admit it. But you must make some little allowance for me, *Mr. Tanqueray*. Your first wife and I, as girls, were like two cherries on one stalk, and then I was the confidential friend of your married life. That post, perhaps, wasn’t altogether a sinecure. And now﻿—well, when a woman gets to my age I suppose she’s a stupid, prejudiced, conventional creature. However, I’ve got over it and﻿—*Giving him her hand*﻿—I hope you’ll be enormously happy and let me be a friend once more.

===Chinese===
奥布里
若真如此，也不全怪我，科特利恩夫人。
科特利恩夫人
科特利恩夫人？哼！不，我承认。可你也得体谅我几分，*坦奎雷先生*。你的前妻和我，做姑娘时好得像一枝梗上的两颗樱桃；后来你婚姻生活中那些体己话，也都是说给我这个心腹朋友听的。那个位置，恐怕不算全然的闲差。如今呢——唉，女人到了我这个年纪，大概就成了个愚蠢、偏见、守旧的动物了。不过我总算想通了，于是——*（把手伸给他。）*——但愿你们大富大贵地幸福，也让我重新做个朋友。

===Original===
Aubrey
Thank you, Alice.
Mrs. Cortelyon
That’s right. I feel more cheerful than I’ve done for weeks. But I suppose it would serve me right if the second Mrs. Tanqueray showed me the door. Do you think she will?

===Chinese===
奥布里
谢谢你，爱丽丝。
科特利恩夫人
这就对了。我此刻的心气，比几个星期以来的都高。不过，倘若第二任坦奎雷夫人把我扫地出门，那也是我应得的报应。你觉得她会吗？

===Original===
Aubrey
*Listening.* Here is my wife. ***Mrs. Cortelyon** rises, and **Paula** enters, dressed for driving; she stops abruptly on seeing **Mrs. Cortelyon**.* Paula dear, Mrs. Cortelyon has called to see you.
***Paula** starts, looks at **Mrs. Cortelyon** irresolutely, then after a slight pause barely touches **Mrs. Cortelyon’s** extended hand.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（倾听。）*我太太来了。*（**科特利恩夫人**起身；**宝拉**上，一身驾车装束；一看见**科特利恩夫人**，她猛地站住。）*亲爱的宝拉，科特利恩夫人来看你了。
*（**宝拉**一怔，迟疑不决地望着**科特利恩夫人**，片刻之后才勉强碰了碰**科特利恩夫人**伸出的手。）*

===Original===
Paula
*Whose manner now alternates between deliberate insolence and assumed sweetness.* Mrs.﻿—? What name, Aubrey?
Aubrey
Mrs. Cortelyon.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（她的态度此刻在存心的无礼与假作的甜腻之间来回变换。）*什么夫人——？叫什么名字来着，奥布里？
奥布里
科特利恩夫人。

===Original===
Paula
Cortelyon? Oh, yes. Cortelyon.
Mrs. Cortelyon
*Carefully guarding herself throughout against any expression of resentment.* Aubrey ought to have told you that Alice Cortelyon and he are very old friends.

===Chinese===
宝拉
科特利恩？哦，对了。科特利恩。
科特利恩夫人
*（自始至终小心克制，不露一点愠色。）*奥布里本该告诉你，爱丽丝·科特利恩和他自己是极老的朋友。

===Original===
Paula
Oh, very likely he has mentioned the circumstance. I have quite a wretched memory.
Mrs. Cortelyon
You know we are neighbours, Mrs. Tanqueray.

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦，这情形他很可能提过。我的记性糟得很。
科特利恩夫人
您知道的，我们是街坊，坦奎雷夫人。

===Original===
Paula
Neighbours? Are we really? Won’t you sit down? *They both sit.* Neighbours! That’s most interesting!
Mrs. Cortelyon
Very near neighbours. You can see my roof from your windows.

===Chinese===
宝拉
街坊？真是吗？您请坐。*（两人落座。）*街坊！这可太有意思了！
科特利恩夫人
很近的街坊。从您家的窗口就望得见我家的屋顶。

===Original===
Paula
I fancy I *have* observed a roof. But you have been away from home; you have only just returned.
Mrs. Cortelyon
I? What makes you think that?

===Chinese===
宝拉
我好像*确实*望见过一个屋顶。不过您一直不在家；您是刚回来的吧。
科特利恩夫人
我？您怎么会这么想？

===Original===
Paula
Why, because it is two months since we came to Highercoombe, and I don’t remember your having called.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Your memory is now terribly accurate. No, I’ve not been away from home, and it is to explain my neglect that I am here, rather unceremoniously, this morning.

===Chinese===
宝拉
咦，我们搬到海厄库姆已经两个月了，我不记得您来拜访过呀。
科特利恩夫人
您的记性此刻又准得吓人了。不，我没离开过家；今天上午我不揣冒昧登门，正是为了解释我的怠慢。

===Original===
Paula
Oh, to explain﻿—quite so. *With mock solicitude.* Ah, you’ve been very ill; I ought to have seen that before.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Ill!

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦，为了解释——原来如此。*（带着假装的关切。）*啊，您一定大病了一场；我早该看出来的。
科特利恩夫人
大病！

===Original===
Paula
You look dreadfully pulled down. We poor women show illness so plainly in our faces, don’t we?
Aubrey
*Anxiously.* Paula dear, Mrs. Cortelyon is the picture of health.

===Chinese===
宝拉
您瞧着憔悴得厉害。我们可怜的女人，病容总是清清楚楚挂在脸上，对吧？
奥布里
*（着急地。）*亲爱的宝拉，科特利恩夫人康健得很。

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
*With some asperity.* I have never *felt* better in my life.
Paula
*Looking round innocently.* Have I said anything awkward? Aubrey, tell Mrs. Cortelyon how stupid and thoughtless I always am!

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
*（略带愠意。）*我这辈子从没觉得*这么*好过。
宝拉
*（天真地环顾。）*我说了什么不得体的话吗？奥布里，快告诉科特利恩夫人，我一向多么蠢、多么没心眼！

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
*To **Drummle** who is now standing close to her.* Really, Cayley﻿—! *He soothes her with a nod and smile and a motion of his finger to his lip.* Mrs. Tanqueray, I am afraid my explanation will not be quite so satisfactory as either of those you have just helped me to. You may have heard﻿—but, if you have heard, you have doubtless forgotten﻿—that twenty years ago, when your husband first lived here, I was a constant visitor at Highercoombe.
Paula
Twenty years ago﻿—fancy. I was a naughty little child then.

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
*（对这时站到她近旁的**德鲁姆尔**。）*真是的，凯利——！*（他点点头、微微一笑，把手指竖在唇上安抚她。）*坦奎雷夫人，只怕我的解释不如您方才替我编的那两条来得叫您满意。您也许听说过——不过就算听说过，想必也忘了——二十年前，您丈夫初住此地时，我是海厄库姆的常客。
宝拉
二十年前——真难以想象。那时我还是个淘气的小丫头呢。

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
Possibly. Well, at that time, and till the end of her life, my affections were centred upon the lady of this house.
Paula
Were they? That was very sweet of you.
***Ellean** approaches **Mrs. Cortelyon**, listening intently to her.*

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
也许吧。呃，从那时起，直到她去世，我的感情都系在这座宅子的女主人身上。
宝拉
是吗？您可真多情。
*（**埃莉安**走近**科特利恩夫人**，凝神听她说话。）*

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
I will say no more on that score, but I must add this: when, two months ago, you came here, I realised, perhaps for the first time, that I was a middle-aged woman, and that it had become impossible for me to accept without some effort a breaking-in upon many tender associations. There, Mrs. Tanqueray, that is my confession. Will you try to understand it and pardon me?
Paula
*Watching **Ellean**﻿—sneeringly.* Ellean dear, you appear to be very interested in Mrs. Cortelyon’s reminiscences; I don’t think I can do better than make you my mouthpiece﻿—there is such sympathy between us. What do you say﻿—can we bring ourselves to forgive Mrs. Cortelyon for neglecting us for two weary months?

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
这个话题我就不再多说了，但有句话必须补上：两个月前你们搬来时，我也许头一回意识到自己已是个中年妇人，要我在一瞬间接受对诸多温情怀念的侵扰，已经不可能了。就是这样，坦奎雷夫人，这是我的自白。您愿试着理解它、宽恕我吗？
宝拉
*（望着**埃莉安**——讥诮地。）*亲爱的埃莉安，你好像对科特利恩夫人的怀旧很感兴趣；我看不如就请你做我的代言人——咱俩之间多有默契。你说呢——咱们能不能原谅科特利恩夫人把我们干晾了两个难熬的月份？

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
*To **Ellean**, pleasantly.* Well, Ellean? *With a little cry of tenderness **Ellean** impulsively sits beside **Mrs. Cortelyon** and takes her hand.* My dear child!
Paula
*In an undertone to **Aubrey**.* Ellean isn’t so very slow in taking to Mrs. Cortelyon!

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
*（对**埃莉安**，和颜悦色地。）*怎么样，埃莉安？*（**埃莉安**柔声轻唤，情不自禁地坐到**科特利恩夫人**身旁，握住她的手。）*我亲爱的孩子！
宝拉
*（低声对**奥布里**。）*埃莉安跟科特利恩夫人热络起来，倒也不算慢啊！

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
*To **Paula** and **Aubrey**.* Come, this encourages me to broach my scheme. Mrs. Tanqueray, it strikes me that you two good people are just now excellent company for each other, while Ellean would perhaps be glad of a little peep into the world you are anxious to avoid. Now, I’m going to Paris tomorrow for a week or two before settling down in Chester Square, so﻿—don’t gasp, both of you!﻿—if this girl is willing, and you have made no other arrangements for her, will you let her come with me to Paris, and afterwards remain with me in town during the Season? ***Ellean** utters an exclamation of surprise. **Paula** is silent.* What do you say?
Aubrey
Paula﻿—Paula dear. *Hesitatingly.* My dear Mrs. Cortelyon, this is wonderfully kind of you; I am really at a loss to﻿—eh, Cayley?

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
*（对**宝拉**和**奥布里**。）*来，这给了我开口提我计划的勇气。坦奎雷夫人，我忽然觉得，眼下你们两位好人彼此正是极好的伴儿，而埃莉安呢，你们一心想避开的那个人们眼中的世界，她也许乐意窥探一眼。是这样：我明天要去巴黎（Paris）一两个星期，之后便回伦敦切斯特广场（Chester Square）安顿——你们俩先别倒抽气！——倘若这孩子愿意，你们又没为她另作安排，能不能让她随我去巴黎，随后社交季期间留在城里陪着我？*（**埃莉安**惊呼出声。**宝拉**沉默。）*你们意下如何？
奥布里
宝拉——亲爱的宝拉。*（迟疑地。）*亲爱的科特利恩夫人，您真是太好了；我实在不知该——嗯，凯利？

===Original===
Drummle
*Watching **Paula** apprehensively.* Kind! Now I must say I don’t think so! I begged Alice to take *me* to Paris, and she declined. I am thrown over for Ellean! Ha! ha!
Mrs. Cortelyon
*Laughing.* What nonsense you talk, Cayley!
*The laughter dies out. **Paula** remains quite still.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（忧虑地望着**宝拉**。）*好！这我可就不同意了！我求爱丽丝带*我*去巴黎，她还回绝了。我竟被埃莉安取而代之！哈！哈！
科特利恩夫人
*（笑。）*你胡说些什么呀，凯利！
*（笑声渐息。**宝拉**一动不动。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Paula dear.
Paula
*Slowly collecting herself.* One moment. I﻿—I don’t quite﻿—*To **Mrs. Cortelyon**.* You propose that Ellean leaves Highercoombe almost at once and remains with you some months?

===Chinese===
奥布里
亲爱的宝拉。
宝拉
*（缓缓定下神。）*等一下。我——我还不很——*（对**科特利恩夫人**。）*您的意思是，让埃莉安差不多立刻离开海厄库姆，在您身边一住数月？

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
It would be a mercy to me. You can afford to be generous to a desolate old widow. Come, Mrs. Tanqueray, won’t you spare her?
Paula
Won’t *I* spare her. *Suspiciously.* Have you mentioned your plan to Aubrey﻿—before I came in?

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
那对我是桩慈善。对一个孤零零的老寡妇，你们大可慷慨些。来吧，坦奎雷夫人，您就舍得放她走吗？
宝拉
我有什么舍不得。*（起疑地。）*在我进来之前，您把这个计划跟奥布里提过吗？

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
No, I had no opportunity.
Paula
Nor to Ellean?

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
没有，我一直没有机会。
宝拉
也没跟埃莉安提过？

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
Oh, no.
Paula
*Looking about her, in suppressed excitement.* This hasn’t been discussed at all, behind my back?

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
哦，没有。
宝拉
*（环顾四周，压着激动。）*这件事压根儿没被人背着我说起过？

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
My dear Mrs. Tanqueray!
Paula
Ellean, let us hear your voice in the matter!

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
我亲爱的坦奎雷夫人！
宝拉
埃莉安，这件事我们也听听你的声音！

===Original===
Ellean
I should like to go with Mrs. Cortelyon﻿—
Paula
Ah!

===Chinese===
埃莉安
我愿意跟科特利恩夫人去——
宝拉
啊！

===Original===
Ellean
That is, if﻿—if﻿—
Paula
If﻿—if what?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
就是说，如果——如果——
宝拉
如果——如果什么？

===Original===
Ellean
*Looking towards **Aubrey**, appealingly.* Papa!
Paula
*In a hard voice.* Oh, of course﻿—I forgot. *To **Aubrey**.* My dear Aubrey, it rests with you, naturally, whether I am﻿—to lose﻿—Ellean.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（望着**奥布里**，恳求地。）*爸爸！
宝拉
*（声音生硬。）*哦，当然——我倒忘了。*（对**奥布里**。）*亲爱的奥布里，我是不是——要失去——埃莉安，自然由你说了算。

===Original===
Aubrey
Lose Ellean! *Advancing to **Paula**.* There is no question of losing Ellean. You would see Ellean in town constantly when she returned from Paris; isn’t that so, Mrs. Cortelyon?
Mrs. Cortelyon
Certainly.

===Chinese===
奥布里
失去埃莉安！*（走向**宝拉**。）*根本谈不上失去埃莉安。她从巴黎回来后，你在城里会时时见到她；是这样吧，科特利恩夫人？
科特利恩夫人
当然。

===Original===
Paula
*Laughing softly.* Oh, I didn’t know I should be allowed that privilege.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Privilege, my dear Mrs. Tanqueray!

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（轻声笑。）*哦，我还不知道我竟配享有那份荣幸。
科特利恩夫人
荣幸，我亲爱的坦奎雷夫人！

===Original===
Paula
Ha, ha! that makes all the difference, doesn’t it?
Aubrey
*With assumed gaiety.* All the difference? I should think so! *To **Ellean**, laying his hand upon her head, tenderly.* And you are quite certain you wish to see what the world is like on the other side of Black Moor?

===Chinese===
宝拉
哈，哈！那可就大不一样了，是不是？
奥布里
*（强作欢容。）*大不一样？那还用说！*（对**埃莉安**，把手温柔地放在她头上。）*你拿定主意了，一定要去看看黑荒原那一头的世界是什么样子？

===Original===
Ellean
If you are willing, papa, I am quite certain.
Aubrey
*Looking at **Paula** irresolutely, then speaking with an effort.* Then I﻿—I am willing.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
只要您肯，爸爸，我就拿定了主意。
奥布里
*（迟疑地望了望**宝拉**，然后费力地开口。）*那么我——我肯。

===Original===
Paula
*Rising and striking the table lightly with her clenched hand.* That decides it! *There is a general movement. Excitedly to **Mrs. Cortelyon**, who advances towards her.* When do you want her?
Mrs. Cortelyon
We go to town this afternoon at five o’clock, and sleep tonight at Bayliss’s. There is barely time for her to make her preparations.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（起身，用攥紧的拳头轻敲桌面。）*这就定了！*（众人一阵骚动。激动地对走向她的**科特利恩夫人**。）*您什么时候要她走？
科特利恩夫人
我们今天下午五点进城，今晚在贝利斯旅馆（Bayliss’s）过夜。她收拾行装的时间都很紧了。

===Original===
Paula
I will undertake that she is ready.
Mrs. Cortelyon
I’ve a great deal to scramble through at home too, as you may guess. Goodbye!

===Chinese===
宝拉
我来担保她到时候一切停当。
科特利恩夫人
您可以想见，我在家里也有一大堆事要赶着办。再见！

===Original===
Paula
*Turning away.* Mrs. Cortelyon is going.
***Paula** stands looking out of the window, with her back to those in the room.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（转过身去。）*科特利恩夫人要走了。
*（**宝拉**站着望向窗外，背对屋里的人。）*

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
*To **Drummle**.* Cayley﻿—
Drummle
*To her.* Eh?

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
*（对**德鲁姆尔**。）*凯利——
德鲁姆尔
*（对她。）*嗯？

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
I’ve gone through it, for the sake of Aubrey and his child, but I﻿—I feel a hundred. Is that a madwoman?
Drummle
Of course; all jealous women are mad.
*He goes out with **Aubrey**.*

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
为了奥布里和他的孩子，我把这场戏撑下来了，可我——我觉得自己有一百岁了。那是个疯女人吗？
德鲁姆尔
当然是；凡是妒忌的女人都是疯子。
*（他与**奥布里**同下。）*

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
*Hesitatingly, to **Paula**.* Goodbye, Mrs. Tanqueray.
***Paula** inclines her head with the slightest possible movement, then resumes her former position. **Ellean** comes from the hall and takes **Mrs. Cortelyon** out of the room. After a brief silence, **Paula** turns with a fierce cry, and hurriedly takes off her coat and hat, and tosses them upon the settee.*

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
*（迟疑地，对**宝拉**。）*再见，坦奎雷夫人。
*（**宝拉**极轻微地点了一下头，随即恢复原先的姿势。**埃莉安**从厅里过来，送**科特利恩夫人**出去。短暂的沉默之后，**宝拉**猛地转身发出一声狂怒的喊叫，匆匆脱下外套和帽子，把它们摔在长椅上。）*

===Original===
Paula
Oh! Oh! Oh! *She drops into the chair as **Aubrey** returns; he stands looking at her.* Who’s that?
Aubrey
I. You have altered your mind about going out?

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦！哦！哦！*（**奥布里**回来时，她跌坐在椅子里；他站着望她。）*是谁？
奥布里
是我。你改变主意不出门了？

===Original===
Paula
Yes. Please to ring the bell.
Aubrey
*Touching the bell.* You are angry about Mrs. Cortelyon and Ellean. Let me try to explain my reasons﻿—

===Chinese===
宝拉
是。请摇铃。
奥布里
*（按铃。）*你在为科特利恩夫人和埃莉安的事生气。让我试着解释我的理由——

===Original===
Paula
Be careful what you say to me just now! I have never felt like this﻿—except once﻿—in my life. Be careful what you say to me!
*A **Servant** enters.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
这会儿你说话可得小心！我这辈子从没这样过——除了有一回。你说话可得小心！
*（一**仆人**上。）*

===Original===
Paula
*Rising.* Is Watts at the door with the cart?
Servant
Yes, ma’am.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（起身。）*瓦茨套好车在门口了吗？
仆人
是的，夫人。

===Original===
Paula
Tell him to drive down to the post-office directly, with this.
*Picking up the letter which has been lying upon the table.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
叫他马上赶车去邮局，把这个送去。
*（拿起一直放在桌上的那封信。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
With that?
Paula
Yes. My letter to Lady Orreyed.
*Giving the letter to the **Servant**, who goes out.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
送这个？
宝拉
对。我写给奥雷耶德夫人的信。
*（把信交给**仆人**，仆人下。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Surely you don’t wish me to countermand any order of yours to a servant? Call the man back﻿—take the letter from him!
Paula
I have not the slightest intention of doing so.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你总不至于要我去收回你给仆人的什么命令吧？把那人叫回来——把信从他手里拿回来！
宝拉
我丝毫没有这个意思。

===Original===
Aubrey
I must, then. *Going to the door. She snatches up her hat and coat and follows him.* What are you going to do?
Paula
If you stop that letter, walk out of the house.
*He hesitates, then leaves the door.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
那我就必须这么做了。*（走向门口。她一把抓起帽子和外套，跟上他。）*你要干什么？
宝拉
你要是拦下那封信，就走出这个家。
*（他迟疑片刻，离开了门口。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
I am right in believing that to be the letter inviting George Orreyed and his wife to stay here, am I not?
Paula
Oh yes﻿—quite right.

===Chinese===
奥布里
我没弄错的话，那就是请乔治·奥雷耶德夫妇来家里做客的信，对吧？
宝拉
哦，没错——完全正确。

===Original===
Aubrey
Let it go; I’ll write to him by-and-by.
Paula
*Facing him.* You dare!

===Chinese===
奥布里
让它寄出去吧；回头我再写信给他。
宝拉
*（面对着他。）*你敢！

===Original===
Aubrey
Hush, Paula!
Paula
Insult me again and, upon my word, I’ll go straight out of the house!

===Chinese===
奥布里
嘘，宝拉！
宝拉
再羞辱我一次，我对天发誓，我就直接走出这个家！

===Original===
Aubrey
Insult you?
Paula
Insult me! What else is it? My God! what else is it? What do you mean by taking Ellean from me?

===Chinese===
奥布里
羞辱你？
宝拉
就是羞辱我！不然是什么？我的天！不然还能是什么？你把埃莉安从我身边夺走，是什么意思？

===Original===
Aubrey
Listen﻿—!
Paula
Listen to *me*! And how do you take her? You pack her off in the care of a woman who has deliberately held aloof from me, who’s thrown mud at me! Yet this Cortelyon creature has only to put foot here once to be entrusted with the charge of the girl you know I dearly want to keep near me!

===Chinese===
奥布里
听我说——！
宝拉
听*我*说！你是怎么夺走她的？你把她打包送走，托付给一个存心疏远我、往我身上泼过泥的女人！而这个科特利恩之类的货色，只要把脚往这儿一踏，就被委以重任，看管起你明知我多想留在身边的姑娘！

===Original===
Aubrey
Paula dear! hear me﻿—!
Paula
Ah! of course, of course! I can’t be so useful to your daughter as such people as this; and so I’m to be given the go-by for any town friend of yours who turns up and chooses to patronise us! Hah! Very well, at any rate, as you take Ellean from me you justify my looking for companions where I can most readily find ’em.

===Chinese===
奥布里
亲爱的宝拉！听我说——！
宝拉
啊！当然了，当然了！对你女儿，我不如这类人物有用；于是随便你哪个城里朋友露个面、肯赏光照拂我们，我就得靠边站！哈！好得很，不管怎么说，既然你把埃莉安从我身边夺走，那我到最容易找到伴儿的地方去找伴儿，就是名正言顺的了。

===Original===
Aubrey
You wish me to fully appreciate your reason for sending that letter to Lady Orreyed?
Paula
Precisely﻿—I do.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你是要我完全领会你给奥雷耶德夫人寄那封信的用意了？
宝拉
正是——就是要你领会。

===Original===
Aubrey
And could you, after all, go back to associates of that order? It’s not possible!
Paula
*Mockingly.* What, not after the refining influence of these intensely respectable surroundings? *Going to the door.* We’ll see!

===Chinese===
奥布里
难道说到底，你还能回到那一路人中间去？这不可能！
宝拉
*（讥讽地。）*什么，经过这极度体面的环境的熏陶，就回不去了？*（走向门口。）*走着瞧！

===Original===
Aubrey
Paula!
Paula
*Violently.* We’ll see!
*She goes out. He stands still looking after her.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
宝拉！
宝拉
*（狂怒地。）*走着瞧！
*（她下。他伫立原地，望着她的背影。）*

## Act III / 第三幕

===Original===
The drawing-room at “Highercoombe.” Facing the spectator are two large French windows, sheltered by a verandah, leading into the garden; on the right is a door opening into a small hall. The fireplace, with a large mirror above it, is on the left-hand side of the room, and higher up in the same wall are double doors recessed. The room is richly furnished, and everything betokens taste and luxury. The windows are open, and there is moonlight in the garden.
**Lady Orreyed**, a pretty, affected doll of a woman with a mincing voice and flaxen hair, is sitting on the ottoman, her head resting against the drum, and her eyes closed. **Paula**, looking pale, worn, and thoroughly unhappy, is sitting at a table. Both are in sumptuous dinner-gowns.
Lady Orreyed
*Opening her eyes.* Well, I never! I dropped off! *Feeling her hair.* Just fancy! Where are the men?

===Chinese===
“海厄库姆”的客厅。正对观众是两扇高大的落地长窗，外有游廊遮护，通向花园；右侧一扇门通向小厅。壁炉上方带一面大镜子，在房间左侧，同一面墙上更高处嵌着双重门。房间陈设华丽，处处见出品位与奢华。窗户开着，花园里洒满月光。
**奥雷耶德夫人（Lady Orreyed）**坐在软榻上，头靠在软榻的圆背上，闭着眼睛；她是个漂亮而做作的玩偶般的女人，说话矫声矫气，一头亚麻色头发。**宝拉**坐在桌旁，面色苍白，憔悴不堪，全然不快。两人都穿着华贵的晚礼服。
奥雷耶德夫人
*（睁开眼睛。）*哎呀，真是的！我睡着了！*（摸摸头发。）*想想看！男人们呢？

===Original===
Paula
*Icily.* Outside, smoking.
*A **Servant** enters with coffee, which he hands to **Lady Orreyed**. **Sir George Orreyed** comes in by the window. He is a man of about thirty-five, with a low forehead, a receding chin, a vacuous expression, and an ominous redness about the nose.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（冷冰冰地。）*在外面，抽烟。
*（一**仆人**端咖啡上，递给**奥雷耶德夫人**。**乔治·奥雷耶德爵士（Sir George Orreyed）**从窗外进来。他三十五岁上下，额头低窄，下巴后缩，表情空虚，鼻子一带透着不祥的红色。）*

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
*Taking coffee.* Here’s Dodo.
Sir George
I say, the flies under the verandah make you swear. *The **Servant** hands coffee to **Paula**, who declines it, then to **Sir George**, who takes a cup.* Hi! wait a bit! *He looks at the tray searchingly, then puts back his cup.* Never mind. *Quietly to **Lady Orreyed**.* I say, they’re dooced sparin’ with their liqueur, ain’t they?
*The **Servant** goes out at window.*

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
*（接过咖啡。）*多多（Dodo）来了。
乔治爵士
我说，游廊底下的苍蝇真能叫人骂娘。*（**仆人**把咖啡递给**宝拉**，她辞谢了；再递给**乔治爵士**，他接了一杯。）*嗨！慢着！*（他细细察看托盘，又把杯子放回去。）*算了。*（悄声对**奥雷耶德夫人**。）*我说，他们家的餐后酒给得也太抠了，是不是？
*（**仆人**从窗下。）*

===Original===
Paula
*To **Sir George**.* Won’t you take coffee, George?
Sir George
No, thanks. It’s gettin’ near time for a whisky and potass. *Approaching **Paula**, regarding **Lady Orreyed** admiringly.* I say, Birdie looks rippin’ tonight, don’t she?

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（对**乔治爵士**。）*你不喝点咖啡吗，乔治？
乔治爵士
不喝了，谢谢。快到喝威士忌苏打的时候了。*（走近**宝拉**，爱慕地望着**奥雷耶德夫人**。）*我说，小鸟儿（Birdie）今晚俏得很，是不是？

===Original===
Paula
Your wife?
Sir George
Yaas﻿—Birdie.

===Chinese===
宝拉
你的太太？
乔治爵士
是啊——小鸟儿。

===Original===
Paula
Rippin’?
Sir George
Yaas.

===Chinese===
宝拉
俏得很？
乔治爵士
是啊。

===Original===
Paula
Quite﻿—quite rippin’.
*He moves round to the settee. **Paula** watches him with distaste, then rises and walks away. **Sir George** falls asleep on the settee.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
俏——俏得很哪。
*（他挪到长椅那儿。**宝拉**厌恶地看着他，起身走开。**乔治爵士**在长椅上睡着了。）*

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
Paula love, I fancied you and Aubrey were a little more friendly at dinner. You haven’t made it up, have you?
Paula
We? Oh, no. We speak before others, that’s all.

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
宝拉亲爱的，我怎么觉得晚饭时你和奥布里和气了些。你们还没和好，对吧？
宝拉
我们？哦，没有。我们当着旁人的面说话，仅此而已。

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
And how long do you intend to carry on this game, dear?
Paula
*Turning away impatiently.* I really can’t tell you.

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
那这场戏你打算唱多久呢，亲爱的？
宝拉
*（不耐烦地转过身去。）*我实在说不上来。

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
Sit down, old girl; don’t be so fidgety. ***Paula** sits on the upper seat of the ottoman with her back to **Lady Orreyed**.* Of course, it’s my duty, as an old friend, to give you a good talking-to﻿—***Paula** glares at her suddenly and fiercely*﻿—but really I’ve found one gets so many smacks in the face through interfering in matrimonial squabbles that I’ve determined to drop it.
Paula
I think you’re wise.

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
坐下吧，老妹子；别这么坐立不安的。*（**宝拉**在软榻高的一头坐下，背对着**奥雷耶德夫人**。）*当然，作为老朋友，我有责任好好数落你一顿——*（**宝拉**突然凶狠地瞪住她）*——不过我确实发现，插手人家夫妻拌嘴，挨的耳光太多，我决心不干这营生了。
宝拉
我看你很明智。

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
However, I must say that I do wish you’d look at marriage in a more solemn light﻿—just as I do, in fact. It is such a beautiful thing﻿—marriage, and if people in our position don’t respect it, and set a good example by living happily with their husbands, what can you expect from the middle classes? When did this sad state of affairs between you and Aubrey actually begin?
Paula
Actually, a fortnight and three days ago; I haven’t calculated the minutes.

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
不过，我必须说，我真希望你把婚姻看得更庄重些——就像我一样。婚姻是件多么美好的东西——我们这种身份的人若不尊重它，不同自己的丈夫和和美美地过日子、做个好榜样，还能指望中产阶级怎么样呢？你跟奥布里这桩伤心事，究竟是从什么时候开始的？
宝拉
说准了，是两周零三天前；分钟数我还没算。

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
A day or two before Dodo and I turned up﻿—arrived.
Paula
Yes. One always remembers one thing by another; we left off speaking to each other the morning I wrote asking you to visit us.

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
比我和多多露面——抵达，早一两天。
宝拉
是啊。人总是靠一件事记起另一件事；我们停止交谈的那个早晨，正是我写信请你们来做客的那天。

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
Lucky for you I was able to pop down, wasn’t it, dear?
Paula
*Glaring at her again.* Most fortunate.

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
幸亏我能一下子赶过来，是不是，亲爱的？
宝拉
*（又瞪了她一眼。）*三生有幸。

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
A serious split with your husband without a pal on the premises﻿—I should say, without a friend in the house﻿—would be most unpleasant.
Paula
*Turning to her abruptly.* This place must be horribly doleful for you and George just now. At least you ought to consider him before me. Why don’t you leave me to my difficulties?

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
同丈夫闹大了，宅子里却连个伙伴都没有——我该说，家里连个朋友都没有——那可就难受死了。
宝拉
*（猛地转向她。）*这地方眼下对你和乔治来说一定乏味透顶了。你至少也该先顾着他，再顾我。为什么不把我撇下，让我跟我的难处作伴呢？

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
Oh, we’re quite comfortable, dear, thank you﻿—both of us. George and me are so wrapped up in each other, it doesn’t matter where we are. I don’t want to crow over you, old girl, but I’ve got a perfect husband.
***Sir George** is now fast asleep, his head thrown back and his mouth open, looking hideous.*

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
哦，我们舒服得很，亲爱的，谢谢你——我们俩都是。乔治和我彼此黏得紧，在哪儿都无所谓。我不是要气你，老妹子，可我得了个十全十美的丈夫。
*（**乔治爵士**此时已沉沉睡去，头向后仰着，嘴张着，模样难看极了。）*

===Original===
Paula
*Glancing at **Sir George**.* So you’ve given me to understand.
Lady Orreyed
Not that we don’t have our little differences. Why, we fell out only this very morning. You remember the diamond and ruby tiara Charley Prestwick gave poor dear Connie Tirlemont years ago, don’t you?

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（瞥了一眼**乔治爵士**。）*这话你已经让我领会过了。
奥雷耶德夫人
倒也不是说我们没有小小的口角。咳，就在今天早上我们还闹了一场。你记得多年前查利·普雷斯特威克（Charley Prestwick）送给可怜的亲爱的康妮·蒂勒蒙的那顶钻石红宝石冠冕吧？

===Original===
Paula
No, I do not.
Lady Orreyed
No? Well, it’s in the market. Benjamin of Piccadilly has got it in his shopwindow, and I’ve set my heart on it.

===Chinese===
宝拉
不记得。
奥雷耶德夫人
不记得？唉，它如今上市了。皮卡迪利的本杰明（Benjamin）把它摆在了橱窗里，我一心想要它。

===Original===
Paula
You consider it quite necessary?
Lady Orreyed
Yes, because what I say to Dodo is this﻿—a lady of my station must smother herself with hair ornaments. It’s different with you, love﻿—people don’t look for so much blaze from you, but I’ve got rank to keep up; haven’t I?

===Chinese===
宝拉
你觉得非有不可？
奥雷耶德夫人
是，因为我跟多多是这么说的——我这样身份的女士，头上非得插满发饰不可。你就不同了，亲爱的——人家不指望你珠光宝气，可我有身价要撑；是不是？

===Original===
Paula
Yes.
Lady Orreyed
Well, that was the cause of the little set-to between I and Dodo this morning. He broke two chairs, he was in such a rage. I forgot, they’re your chairs; do you mind?

===Chinese===
宝拉
是。
奥雷耶德夫人
呃，今儿早上我跟多多那场小小的不痛快，起因就在这儿。他气得摔坏了两把椅子。哦对了，我忘了，那是你家的椅子；你不心疼吧？

===Original===
Paula
No.
Lady Orreyed
You know, poor Dodo can’t lose his temper without smashing something; if it isn’t a chair, it’s a mirror; if it isn’t that, it’s china﻿—a bit of Dresden for choice. Dear old pet! he loves a bit of Dresden when he’s furious. He doesn’t really throw things *at* me, dear; he simply lifts them up and drops them, like a gentleman. I expect our room upstairs will look rather wrecky before I get that tiara.

===Chinese===
宝拉
不心疼。
奥雷耶德夫人
你知道，可怜的多多发起脾气来不砸点什么不行；不是椅子，就是镜子；再不然就是瓷器——顶爱摔德累斯顿瓷。亲爱的老宝贝！他暴怒的时候就爱摔件德累斯顿瓷。其实他并不朝*我*身上扔东西，亲爱的；他只是把东西举起来，撒手任它掉，像个绅士。我瞧着等我拿到那顶冠冕之前，我们楼上的房间准得像遭了劫似的。

===Original===
Paula
Excuse the suggestion, perhaps your husband can’t afford it.
Lady Orreyed
Oh, how dreadfully changed you are, Paula! Dodo can always mortgage something, or borrow of his ma. What *is* coming to you!

===Chinese===
宝拉
恕我多句嘴，也许你丈夫买不起。
奥雷耶德夫人
哦，你可变得厉害呀，宝拉！多多总有东西可抵押，再不然还能向他妈借钱。你这是*怎么*了！

===Original===
Paula
Ah!
*She sits at the piano and touches the keys.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
啊！
*（她坐到钢琴前，按了按琴键。）*

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
Oh, yes, do play! That’s the one thing I envy you for.
Paula
What shall I play?

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
哦，对，弹吧！这是我唯一羡慕你的地方。
宝拉
弹什么呢？

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
What was that heavenly piece you gave us last night, dear?
Paula
A bit of Schubert. Would you like to hear it again?

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
昨晚你给我们弹的那支天堂似的曲子是什么来着，亲爱的？
宝拉
一段舒伯特（Schubert）。想再听一遍吗？

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
You don’t know any comic songs, do you?
Paula
I’m afraid not.

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
你不会滑稽歌儿吧，会吗？
宝拉
恐怕不会。

===Original===
Lady Orreyed
I leave it to you, then.
***Paula** plays. **Aubrey** and **Cayley Drummle** appear outside the window; they look into the room.*

===Chinese===
奥雷耶德夫人
那就听你安排了。
*（**宝拉**弹琴。**奥布里**与**凯利·德鲁姆尔**出现在窗外；他们朝屋里望。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*To **Drummle**.* You can see her face in that mirror. Poor girl, how ill and wretched she looks.
Drummle
When are the Orreyeds going?

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（对**德鲁姆尔**。）*你在那面镜子里看得见她的脸。可怜的姑娘，她瞧着又病又苦。
德鲁姆尔
奥雷耶德夫妇什么时候走？

===Original===
Aubrey
Heaven knows!
*Entering the room.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
天知道！
*（走进房间。）*

===Original===
Drummle
But *you’re* entertaining them; what’s it to do with Heaven?
*Following **Aubrey**.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
可款待他们的是*你*；跟天有什么相干？
*（随**奥布里**进屋。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Do you know, Cayley, that even the Orreyeds serve a useful purpose? My wife actually speaks to me before our guests﻿—think of that! I’ve come to rejoice at the presence of the Orreyeds!
Drummle
I daresay; we’re taught that beetles are sent for a benign end.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你知道吗，凯利，连奥雷耶德夫妇都派得上用场？我太太居然肯当着客人的面跟我说话了——想想这个！我现在是见到奥雷耶德夫妇就要欢天喜地了！
德鲁姆尔
未必不是；人家教导我们说，连甲虫都是本着善意的目的派来的。

===Original===
Aubrey
Cayley, talk to Paula again tonight.
Drummle
Certainly, if I get the chance.

===Chinese===
奥布里
凯利，今晚再跟宝拉谈谈。
德鲁姆尔
只要逮着机会，一定谈。

===Original===
Aubrey
Let’s contrive it. George is asleep; perhaps I can get that doll out of the way. *As they advance into the room, **Paula** abruptly ceases playing and finds interest in a volume of music. **Sir George** is now nodding and snoring apoplectically.* Lady Orreyed, whenever you feel inclined for a game of billiards I’m at your service.
Lady Orreyed
*Jumping up.* Charmed, I’m sure! I really thought you’d forgotten poor little me. Oh, look at Dodo!

===Chinese===
奥布里
咱们来安排一下。乔治睡着了；那个玩偶也许我能引开。*（他们往房间里走时，**宝拉**突然停止弹琴，专心翻起一本琴谱。**乔治爵士**此刻正打着瞌睡，鼾声如中风一般。）*奥雷耶德夫人，您什么时候想打一局台球，我随时奉陪。
奥雷耶德夫人
*（跳起来。）*荣幸之至！我还真以为你把可怜的小我给忘了呢。哦，瞧多多那样儿！

===Original===
Aubrey
No, no, don’t wake him; he’s tired.
Lady Orreyed
I must, he looks so plain. *Rousing **Sir George**.* Dodo! Dodo!

===Chinese===
奥布里
不，不，别吵醒他；他累了。
奥雷耶德夫人
非叫醒不可，他那样子太不体面了。*（唤醒**乔治爵士**。）*多多！多多！

===Original===
Sir George
*Stupidly.* ’Ullo!
Lady Orreyed
Dodo, dear, you were snoring.

===Chinese===
乔治爵士
*（迷迷糊糊地。）*喂——！
奥雷耶德夫人
多多，亲爱的，你打鼾了。

===Original===
Sir George
Oh, I say, you could ’a told me that by-and-by.
Aubrey
You want a cigar, George; come into the billiard room. *Giving his arm to **Lady Orreyed**.* Cayley, bring Paula.
***Aubrey** and **Lady Orreyed** go out.*

===Chinese===
乔治爵士
哦，我说，这事你本可以待会儿再告诉我的。
奥布里
你该抽支雪茄了，乔治；到台球室来。*（把手臂让给**奥雷耶德夫人**。）*凯利，带上宝拉。
*（**奥布里**与**奥雷耶德夫人**下。）*

===Original===
Sir George
*Rising.* Hey, what! billiard room! *Looking at his watch.* How goes the﻿—? Phew! ’Ullo, ’Ullo! Whisky and potass!
*He goes rapidly after **Aubrey** and **Lady Orreyed**. **Paula** resumes playing.*

===Chinese===
乔治爵士
*（起身。）*嗨，什么！台球室！*（看表。）*几点——？嚯！喂，喂！威士忌苏打！
*（他快步追**奥布里**和**奥雷耶德夫人**下。**宝拉**重新弹起琴来。）*

===Original===
Paula
*After a pause.* Don’t moon about after me, Cayley; follow the others.
Drummle
Thanks, by-and-by. *Sitting.* That’s pretty.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（停顿片刻。）*别在我跟前晃悠了，凯利；跟着他们去。
德鲁姆尔
谢谢，等会儿吧。*（坐下。）*真好听。

===Original===
Paula
*After another pause, still playing.* I wish you wouldn’t stare so.
Drummle
Was I staring? I’m sorry. *She plays a little longer, then stops suddenly, rises, and goes to the window, where she stands looking out. **Drummle** moves from the ottoman to the settee.* A lovely night.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（又停了停，仍在弹。）*请你别这么盯着看。
德鲁姆尔
我盯着看了吗？抱歉。*（她又弹了一会儿，忽然停住，起身走到窗前，站在那儿望向外头。**德鲁姆尔**从软榻挪到长椅上。）*夜色真好。

===Original===
Paula
*Startled.* Oh! *Without turning to him.* Why do you hop about like a monkey?
Drummle
Hot rooms play the deuce with the nerves. Now, it would have done you good to have walked in the garden with us after dinner and made merry. Why didn’t you?

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（一惊。）*哦！*（没有转向他。）*你为什么像猴子似的蹦来蹦去？
德鲁姆尔
燠热的屋子最跟神经过不去。喏，饭后要是你跟我们一起在花园里散散步、开开心，对你本有大好处。为什么不去？

===Original===
Paula
You know why.
Drummle
Ah, you’re thinking of the﻿—difference between you and Aubrey?

===Chinese===
宝拉
你知道为什么。
德鲁姆尔
啊，你在想那件——你跟奥布里之间的——别扭？

===Original===
Paula
Yes, I *am* thinking of it.
Drummle
Well, so am I. How long﻿—?

===Chinese===
宝拉
是的，我*正是在*想它。
德鲁姆尔
唉，我也在想。有多久了——？

===Original===
Paula
Getting on for three weeks.
Drummle
Bless me, it must be! And this would have been such a night to have healed it! Moonlight, the stars, the scent of flowers; and yet enough darkness to enable a kind woman to rest her hand for an instant on the arm of a good fellow who loves her. Ah, ha! it’s a wonderful power, dear Mrs. Aubrey, the power of an offended woman! Only realise it! Just that one touch﻿—the mere tips of her fingers﻿—and, for herself and another, she changes the colour of the whole world!

===Chinese===
宝拉
快三个星期了。
德鲁姆尔
天哪，可不是嘛！而这样的夜晚本该把它治好的！月光、星辰、花香；夜色又暗得恰到好处，足以让一位善良的女人把她的手在一个爱她的好男人的臂上轻轻搭那么一瞬。啊，哈！受了冒犯的女人的力量，亲爱的奥布里太太，那可是一种神奇的力量！只要意识到它！仅仅那一下轻触——只是指尖——为她自己，也为另一个人，她就能叫整个世界变个颜色！

===Original===
Paula
*Turning to him, calmly.* Cayley, my dear man, you talk exactly like a very romantic old lady.
*She leaves the window and sits playing with the knickknacks on the table.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（转向他，平静地。）*凯利，我亲爱的，你说话活像一位多情得不得了的老婆婆。
*（她离开窗边，坐下摆弄桌上的小摆设。）*

===Original===
Drummle
*To himself.* H’m, that hasn’t done it! Well﻿—ha, ha!﻿—I accept the suggestion. An old woman, eh?
Paula
Oh, I didn’t intend﻿—

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（自语。）*唔，这话没起作用！好吧——哈，哈！——这个建议我领了。就当个老婆子，嗯？
宝拉
哦，我并不是有意——

===Original===
Drummle
But why not? I’ve every qualification﻿—well, almost. And I confess it would have given this withered bosom a throb of grandmotherly satisfaction if I could have seen you and Aubrey at peace before I take my leave tomorrow.
Paula
Tomorrow, Cayley!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
可为什么不呢？我的资格样样齐全——嗯，差不多样样。而且我承认，倘若明天临行之前能看到你和奥布里言归于好，这颗干瘪的心胸准会涌起一股老祖母式的欢慰。
宝拉
明天，凯利！

===Original===
Drummle
I must.
Paula
Oh, this house is becoming unendurable.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
我必须走了。
宝拉
哦，这宅子越来越叫人忍不下去了。

===Original===
Drummle
You’re very kind. But you’ve got the Orreyeds.
Paula
*Fiercely.* The Orreyeds! I﻿—I hate the Orreyeds! I lie awake at night, hating them!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
你太客气了。可你还有奥雷耶德夫妇作伴呢。
宝拉
*（狠狠地。）*奥雷耶德夫妇！我——我恨奥雷耶德夫妇！我夜里躺着睡不着，恨他们！

===Original===
Drummle
Pardon me, I’ve understood that their visit is, in some degree, owing to﻿—hem!﻿—your suggestion.
Paula
Heavens! that doesn’t make me like them better. Somehow or another, I﻿—I’ve outgrown these people. This woman﻿—I used to think her “jolly!”﻿—sickens me. I can’t breathe when she’s near me: the whiff of her handkerchief turns me faint! And she patronises me by the hour, until I﻿—I feel my nails growing longer with every word she speaks!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
恕我冒昧，据我所知，他们这趟来做客，多少是——咳！——出于你的提议。
宝拉
老天！这并不能让我多喜欢他们半分。不知怎么的，我——我已经长过这些人一头了。这个女人——我从前还觉得她“够味”！——如今叫我恶心。她一凑近我就喘不上气：她那手帕上的气味一飘过来我就发晕！她一连几个钟头对我摆架子施恩惠，直弄得我——她说一个字，我就觉得指甲长长一分！

===Original===
Drummle
My dear lady, why on earth don’t you say all this to Aubrey?
Paula
Oh, I’ve been such an utter fool, Cayley!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
亲爱的夫人，这些话你到底为什么不对奥布里说？
宝拉
哦，我一直是个十足的傻瓜，凯利！

===Original===
Drummle
*Soothingly.* Well, well, mention it to Aubrey!
Paula
No, no, you don’t understand. What do you think I’ve done?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（劝慰地。）*好了好了，跟奥布里提一提吧！
宝拉
不，不，你不明白。你猜我干了什么？

===Original===
Drummle
Done! What, *since* you invited the Orreyeds?
Paula
Yes; I must tell you﻿—

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
干了什么！怎么，*打从*你请奥雷耶德夫妇来之后？
宝拉
是的；我必须告诉你——

===Original===
Drummle
Perhaps you’d better not.
Paula
Look here. I’ve intercepted some letters from Mrs. Cortelyon and Ellean to﻿—him. *Producing three unopened letters from the bodice of her dress.* There are the accursed things! From Paris﻿—two from the Cortelyon woman, the other from Ellean!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
也许你还是别说的好。
宝拉
你听好。我截下了科特利恩夫人和埃莉安写给——他的几封信。*（从裙子的衣襟里掏出三封未拆的信。）*这些该死的东西就在这儿！从巴黎寄来的——科特利恩那女人的两封，埃莉安的一封！

===Original===
Drummle
But why﻿—why?
Paula
I don’t know. Yes, I do! I saw letters coming from Ellean to her father; not a line to me﻿—not a line. And one morning it happened I was downstairs before he was, and I spied this one lying with his heap on the breakfast-table, and I slipped it into my pocket﻿—out of malice, Cayley, pure devilry! And a day or two afterwards I met Elwes the postman at the Lodge, and took the letters from him, and found these others amongst ’em. I felt simply fiendish when I saw them﻿—fiendish! *Returning the letters to her bodice.* And now I carry them about with me, and they’re scorching me like a mustard plaster!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
可为什么——为什么？
宝拉
我不知道。不，我知道！我看见埃莉安一封封来信给她父亲；给我却一个字也没有——一个字也没有。一天早上，碰巧我比他先下楼，我瞥见早餐桌上他那一堆信里躺着这样一封，我就把它滑进了口袋——纯粹是使坏，凯利，纯粹的恶念！过了一两天，我在门房那儿遇上邮差埃尔维斯（Elwes），把信从他手里接了过来，在其中发现了另外这几封。看见它们的时候，我觉得自己简直像恶魔——像恶魔！*（把信放回衣襟。）*如今我随身带着它们，它们像芥末膏药一样烤着我！

===Original===
Drummle
Oh, this accounts for Aubrey not hearing from Paris lately!
Paula
That’s an ingenious conclusion to arrive at! Of course it does! *With an hysterical laugh.* Ha, ha!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
哦，这就解释了奥布里近来为什么收不到巴黎的信了！
宝拉
好个机灵的结论！可不是嘛！*（歇斯底里地笑。）*哈，哈！

===Original===
Drummle
Well, well! *Laughing.* Ha, ha, ha!
Paula
*Turning upon him.* I suppose it *is* amusing!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
好了，好了！*（笑。）*哈，哈，哈！
宝拉
*（猛地转向他。）*我猜这*确实*好笑！

===Original===
Drummle
I beg pardon.
Paula
Heaven knows I’ve little enough to brag about! I’m a bad lot, but not in mean tricks of this sort. In all my life this is the most caddish thing I’ve done. How am I to get rid of these letters﻿—that’s what I want to know? How am I to get rid of them?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
请恕罪。
宝拉
老天知道，我本来就没什么可夸口的！我是个坏货，可坏也不坏在这种下三滥的伎俩上。我这一辈子，就数这件事最下作。这几封信我怎么才能打发掉——我想知道的就是这个？我怎么才能打发掉它们？

===Original===
Drummle
If I were you I should take Aubrey aside and put them into his hands as soon as possible.
Paula
What! and tell him to his face that I﻿—! No, thank you. I suppose *you* wouldn’t like to﻿—

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
我要是你，就把奥布里拉到一边，尽快把它们放到他手里。
宝拉
什么！当着他的面告诉他我——！不了，谢谢。我猜*你*也不愿意——

===Original===
Drummle
No, no; I won’t touch ’em!
Paula
And you call yourself my friend?

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
不，不；我绝不碰它们！
宝拉
你还自称是我的朋友？

===Original===
Drummle
*Good-humouredly.* No, I don’t!
Paula
Perhaps I’ll tie them together and give them to his man in the morning.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（和气地。）*我可没自称过！
宝拉
也许我会把它们捆在一起，明早交给他贴身的仆人。

===Original===
Drummle
That won’t avoid an explanation.
Paula
*Recklessly.* Oh, then he must miss them﻿—

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
那也免不了一番解释。
宝拉
*（豁出去地。）*哦，那他就只能丢了它们——

===Original===
Drummle
And trace them.
Paula
*Throwing herself upon the ottoman.* I don’t care!

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
然后追查它们。
宝拉
*（整个人扑倒在软榻上。）*我不管！

===Original===
Drummle
I know you don’t; but let me send him to you now, may I?
Paula
Now! What do you think a woman’s made of? I couldn’t stand it, Cayley. I haven’t slept for nights; and last night there was thunder, too! I believe I’ve got the horrors.

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
我知道你不管；不过让我现在就把他给你叫来，行吗？
宝拉
现在！你当女人是什么做的？我受不了的，凯利。我好几夜没合眼了；昨晚还打雷来着！我看我是得了恐怖症了。

===Original===
Drummle
*Taking the little hand-mirror from the table.* You’ll sleep well enough when you deliver those letters. Come, come, Mrs. Aubrey﻿—a good night’s rest! *Holding the mirror before her face.* It’s quite time.
*She looks at herself for a moment, then snatches the mirror from him.*

===Chinese===
德鲁姆尔
*（从桌上拿起那面小手镜。）*等这几封信交了出去，你自然睡得香。来吧，来吧，奥布里太太——安睡一夜！*（把镜子举到她脸前。）*正是时候了。
*（她照了自己片刻，一把从他手里夺过镜子。）*

===Original===
Paula
You brute, Cayley, to show me that!
Drummle
Then﻿—may I? Be guided by a fr﻿—a poor old woman! May I?

===Chinese===
宝拉
你这狠心的，凯利，竟拿这个给我看！
德鲁姆尔
那么——可以吗？听一个老太——一个可怜的老婆子的话！可以吗？

===Original===
Paula
You’ll kill me, amongst you!
Drummle
What do you say?

===Chinese===
宝拉
你们一个个的，要把我逼死了！
德鲁姆尔
你怎么说？

===Original===
Paula
*After a pause.* Very well. *He nods his head and goes out rapidly. She looks after him for a moment, and calls “Cayley! Cayley!” Then she again produces the letters, deliberately, one by one, fingering them with aversion. Suddenly she starts, turning her head towards the door.* Ah!
***Aubrey** enters quickly.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（停顿片刻。）*好吧。*（他点点头，快步出去。她目送他片刻，喊了两声“凯利！凯利！”随后她又把那几封信一封一封慢慢地掏出来，厌恶地用手指拨弄着。突然她一惊，猛回头朝门口望去。）*啊！
*（**奥布里**快步上。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Paula!
Paula
*Handing him the letters, her face averted.* There! *He examines the letters, puzzled, and looks at her inquiringly.* They are many days old. I stole them, I suppose to make you anxious and unhappy.
*He looks at the letters again, then lays them aside on the table.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
宝拉！
宝拉
*（把信递给他，脸偏到一边。）*拿去！*（他困惑地查看信件，询问地望着她。）*它们已经搁了好些天了。是我偷的，我想是要叫你焦灼、叫你不痛快。
*（他又看了看信，把它们放到桌上。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*Gently.* Paula, dear, it doesn’t matter.
Paula
*After a short pause.* Why﻿—why do you take it like this?

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（温和地。）*宝拉，亲爱的，不要紧。
宝拉
*（略停。）*为什么——为什么你这样不当回事？

===Original===
Aubrey
What did you expect?
Paula
Oh, but I suppose silent reproaches are really the severest. And then, naturally, you are itching to open your letters.
*She crosses the room as if to go.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
你原指望怎样？
宝拉
哦，不过我猜无声的责备才是最厉害的。再说，你自然急着要拆信了。
*（她穿过房间，像是要走开。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Paula! *She pauses.* Surely, surely it’s all over now?
Paula
All over! *Mockingly.* Has my stepdaughter returned then? When did she arrive? I haven’t heard of it!

===Chinese===
奥布里
宝拉！*（她停住。）*想必，想必现在总都过去了？
宝拉
都过去了！*（讥诮地。）*敢情我那位继女回来了？她几时到的？我怎么没听说！

===Original===
Aubrey
You can be very cruel.
Paula
That word’s always on a man’s lips; he uses it if his soup’s cold. *With another movement as if to go.* Need we﻿—

===Chinese===
奥布里
你有时真够残忍的。
宝拉
这个字眼男人随时挂在嘴上；汤凉了他也用它。*（又做出要走的样子。）*咱们还用不用——

===Original===
Aubrey
I know I’ve wounded you, Paula. But isn’t there any way out of this?
Paula
When does Ellean return? Tomorrow? Next week?

===Chinese===
奥布里
我知道我伤了你的心，宝拉。可这局面就真没有出路吗？
宝拉
埃莉安什么时候回来？明天？下星期？

===Original===
Aubrey
*Wearily.* Oh! Why should we grudge Ellean the little pleasure she is likely to find in Paris and in London.
Paula
I grudge her nothing, if that’s a hit at me. But with that woman﻿—!

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（疲惫地。）*唉！埃莉安在巴黎、在伦敦能寻到的那点快乐，咱们何必吝惜给她。
宝拉
要是这话是刺我，我可什么都没吝惜给她。可跟那个女人在一起——！

===Original===
Aubrey
It must be that woman or another. You know that at present we are unable to give Ellean the opportunity of﻿—of﻿—
Paula
Of mixing with respectable people.

===Chinese===
奥布里
不是这个女人就是别的女人。你知道，眼下我们还给不了埃莉安那样的机会，去——去——
宝拉
去结交体面人。

===Original===
Aubrey
The opportunity of gaining friends, experience, ordinary knowledge of the world. If you are interested in Ellean, can’t you see how useful Mrs. Cortelyon’s good offices are?
Paula
May I put one question? At the end of the London season, when Mrs. Cortelyon has done with Ellean, is it quite understood that the girl comes back to us? ***Aubrey** is silent.* Is it? Is it?

===Chinese===
奥布里
去赢得朋友、阅历、寻常的处世见识。倘若你真把埃莉安放在心上，难道看不出科特利恩夫人的从中引荐多么有用？
宝拉
我能问一个问题吗？等伦敦的社交季到头、科特利恩夫人用完了埃莉安，这姑娘要回到我们身边来，这一点说定了没有？*（**奥布里**沉默。）*说定了没有？说定了没有？

===Original===
Aubrey
Let us wait till the end of the season﻿—
Paula
Oh! I knew it. You’re only fooling me; you put me off with any trash. I believe you’ve sent Ellean away, not for the reasons you give, but because you don’t consider me a decent companion for her, because you’re afraid she might get a little of her innocence rubbed off in my company? Come, isn’t that the truth? Be honest! Isn’t that it?

===Chinese===
奥布里
且等到社交季结束——
宝拉
哦！我就知道。你不过是在哄我；随便拿什么破烂搪塞我。我相信你送走埃莉安，并不是为你说的那些缘故，而是因为你不把我当作配她的伴儿，因为你怕她跟我待久了，那份天真要被磨掉一点？说吧，难道不是这样？老实说！是不是这样？

===Original===
Aubrey
Yes.
*There is a moment’s silence on both sides.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
是的。
*（双方沉默了片刻。）*

===Original===
Paula
*With uplifted hands as if to strike him.* Oh!
Aubrey
*Taking her by the wrists.* Sit down. Sit down. *He puts her into a chair; she shakes herself free with a cry.* Now listen to me. Fond as you are, Paula, of harking back to your past, there’s one chapter of it you always let alone. I’ve never asked you to speak of it; you’ve never offered to speak of it. I mean the chapter that relates to the time when you were﻿—like Ellean. *She attempts to rise; he restrains her.* No, no.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（双手扬起，像要打他。）*哦！
奥布里
*（抓住她的手腕。）*坐下。坐下。*（他把她按进椅子里；她叫了一声，挣脱开来。）*现在听我说。宝拉，你虽爱翻你的过去，其中有一章你从来绕着不走。我从没要求你谈它；你也从没主动谈过它。我指的那一章，关系到你——像埃莉安这么大的时候。*（她试图起身；他按住她。）*不，不。

===Original===
Paula
I don’t choose to talk about that time. I won’t satisfy your curiosity.
Aubrey
My dear Paula, I have no curiosity﻿—I know what you were at Ellean’s age. I’ll tell you. You hadn’t a thought that wasn’t a wholesome one, you hadn’t an impulse that didn’t tend towards good, you never harboured a notion you couldn’t have gossiped about to a parcel of children. *She makes another effort to rise: he lays his hand lightly on her shoulder.* And this was a very few years back﻿—there are days now when you look like a schoolgirl﻿—but think of the difference between the two Paulas. You’ll have to think hard, because after a cruel life one’s perceptions grow a thick skin. But, for God’s sake, do think till you get these two images clearly in your mind, and then ask yourself what sort of a friend such a woman as you are today would have been for the girl of seven or eight years ago.

===Chinese===
宝拉
我不愿谈那时候的事。我不会满足你的好奇心。
奥布里
亲爱的宝拉，我没有好奇心——你像埃莉安这么大时是什么样子，我都知道。我来告诉你。那时候你没有一个念头不是健全的，没有一丝冲动不是向善的，你心里从没窝藏过一个不能讲给一群孩子听的念头。*（她又挣着要起来；他把手轻轻搭在她肩上。）*而那不过是短短几年之前——如今还有些日子，你看上去仍像个女学生——可想想两个宝拉之间的差别吧。你得狠狠地想，因为经过残酷的生活之后，人的感知会磨出一层厚茧。可看在上帝的份上，务必想着，直到这两个形象清清楚楚立在你心里，然后问问自己：你今天这样的一个女人，对七年前、八年前那个姑娘来说，会是个什么样的朋友。

===Original===
Paula
*Rising.* How dare you? I could be almost as good a friend to Ellean as her own mother would have been had she lived. I know what you mean. How dare you?
Aubrey
You say that; very likely you believe it. But you’re blind, Paula; you’re blind. You! Every belief that a young, pure-minded girl holds sacred﻿—that you once held sacred﻿—you now make a target for a jest, a sneer, a paltry cynicism. I tell you, you’re not mistress any longer of your thoughts or your tongue. Why, how often, sitting between you and Ellean, have I seen her cheeks turn scarlet as you’ve rattled off some tale that belongs by right to the club or the smoking-room! Have you noticed the blush? If you have, has the cause of it ever struck you? And this is the girl you say you love, I admit that you *do* love, whose love you expect in return! Oh, Paula, I make the best, the only, excuse for you when I tell you you’re blind!

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（起身。）*你怎么敢？我对埃莉安，几乎能像她亲妈活着时一样做个好朋友。我知道你什么意思。你怎么敢？
奥布里
你这么说；多半你也这么信。可你是瞎的，宝拉；你是瞎的。你！一个年轻、心地纯洁的姑娘奉为神圣的每一样信念——你从前也奉为神圣的每一样——如今都被你当成打趣、讥笑、浅薄玩世不恭的靶子。我告诉你，你早已做不了自己的念头和舌头的主了。唉，多少回，我坐在你和埃莉安中间，眼看你说起一段本该属于俱乐部或吸烟室的故事，她的双颊烧得通红！你可留意过她脸红？若是留意过，可曾想过缘由？而这正是那个你自称爱着的姑娘——我承认你*确实*爱她——你还指望她回过头来爱你！哦，宝拉，我对你的最好、也是唯一的开脱，就是说你瞎了眼！

===Original===
Paula
Ellean﻿—Ellean blushes easily.
Aubrey
You blushed as easily a few years ago.

===Chinese===
宝拉
埃莉安——埃莉安脸皮薄，容易红。
奥布里
几年前你也是这么脸皮薄，动不动就红。

===Original===
Paula
*After a short pause.* Well! Have you finished your sermon?
Aubrey
*With a gesture of despair.* Oh, Paula!
*Going up to the window and standing with his back to the room.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（略停。）*好了！你这通训道讲完了没有？
奥布里
*（做个绝望的手势。）*哦，宝拉！
*（走到窗前，背对房间站着。）*

===Original===
Paula
*To herself.* A few﻿—years ago! *She walks slowly towards the door, then suddenly drops upon the ottoman in a paroxysm of weeping.* O God! A few years ago!
Aubrey
*Going to her.* Paula!

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（自语。）*几——年前！*（她慢慢朝门口走去，忽然扑倒在软榻上，一阵撕心的痛哭。）*哦，上帝！几年前！
奥布里
*（走到她身边。）*宝拉！

===Original===
Paula
*Sobbing.* Oh, don’t touch me!
Aubrey
Paula!

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（抽泣。）*哦，别碰我！
奥布里
宝拉！

===Original===
Paula
Oh, go away from me! *He goes back a few steps, and after a little while she becomes calmer and rises unsteadily; then in an altered tone.* Look here﻿—! *He advances a step; she checks him with a quick gesture.* Look here! Get rid of these people﻿—Mabel and her husband﻿—as soon as possible! I﻿—I’ve done with them!
Aubrey
*In a whisper.* Paula!

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦，离我远点！*（他后退几步，过了一会儿她平静了些，摇摇晃晃地站起来；然后换了副声调。）*你听好——！*（他上前一步；她一个急促的手势止住他。）*你听好！把这些人——梅布尔和她丈夫——尽早打发走！我——我跟他们一刀两断了！
奥布里
*（耳语。）*宝拉！

===Original===
Paula
And then﻿—then﻿—when the time comes for Ellean to leave Mrs. Cortelyon, give me﻿—give me another chance! *He advances again, but she shrinks away.* No, no!
*She goes out by the door on the right. He sinks on to the settee, covering his eyes with his hands. There is a brief silence, then a **Servant** enters.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
还有——还有——等埃莉安该离开科特利恩夫人的时候，再给——再给我一次机会！*（他又上前，她却躲开了。）*不，不！
*（她从右边那扇门下。他跌坐在长椅上，双手掩住眼睛。短暂的沉默之后，一**仆人**上。）*

===Original===
Servant
Mrs. Cortelyon, sir, with Miss Ellean.
***Aubrey** rises to meet **Mrs. Cortelyon**, who enters, followed by **Ellean**, both being in travelling dresses. The **Servant** withdraws.*

===Chinese===
仆人
科特利恩夫人到，先生，同埃莉安小姐一起。
*（**奥布里**起身迎接**科特利恩夫人**，她上，**埃莉安**随在后面，两人都穿着旅行装。**仆人**退下。）*

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
*Shaking hands with **Aubrey**.* Oh, my dear Aubrey!
Aubrey
Mrs. Cortelyon! *Kissing **Ellean**.* Ellean dear!

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
*（与**奥布里**握手。）*哦，我亲爱的奥布里！
奥布里
科特利恩夫人！*（吻**埃莉安**。）*亲爱的埃莉安！

===Original===
Ellean
Papa, is all well at home?
Mrs. Cortelyon
We’re shockingly anxious.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
爸爸，家里一切都好吗？
科特利恩夫人
我们都快急死了。

===Original===
Aubrey
Yes, yes, all’s well. This is quite unexpected. *To **Mrs. Cortelyon**.* You’ve found Paris insufferably hot?
Mrs. Cortelyon
Insufferably hot! Paris is pleasant enough. We’ve had no letter from you!

===Chinese===
奥布里
好了，好了，一切都好。这可太意外了。*（对**科特利恩夫人**。）*你们觉得巴黎热得受不了吧？
科特利恩夫人
热得受不了！巴黎倒还挺宜人。可我们没收到你的信！

===Original===
Aubrey
I wrote to Ellean a week ago.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Without alluding to the subject I had written to you upon.

===Chinese===
奥布里
一星期前我给埃莉安写了信。
科特利恩夫人
却对我写信给你的那件事只字不提。

===Original===
Aubrey
*Thinking.* Ah, of course﻿—
Mrs. Cortelyon
And since then we’ve both written and you’ve been absolutely silent. Oh, it’s too bad!

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（思索。）*啊，对了——
科特利恩夫人
打那以后我们两人都写过信，你却音信全无。哦，太不像话了！

===Original===
Aubrey
*Picking up the letters from the table.* It isn’t altogether my fault. Here are the letters﻿—
Ellean
Papa!

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（从桌上拿起那几封信。）*这可不全怪我。信在这儿——
埃莉安
爸爸！

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
They’re unopened.
Aubrey
An accident delayed their reaching me till this evening. I’m afraid this has upset you very much.

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
都没拆过。
奥布里
一个意外使它们直到今晚才到我手里。只怕这叫你们非常不安了。

===Original===
Mrs. Cortelyon
Upset me!
Ellean
*In an undertone to **Mrs. Cortelyon**.* Never mind. Not now, dear﻿—not tonight.

===Chinese===
科特利恩夫人
何止不安！
埃莉安
*（低声对**科特利恩夫人**。）*别计较了。现在别说，亲爱的——今晚别说。

===Original===
Aubrey
Eh?
Mrs. Cortelyon
*To **Ellean** aloud.* Child, run away and take your things off. She doesn’t look as if she’d journeyed from Paris today.

===Chinese===
奥布里
嗯？
科特利恩夫人
*（大声对**埃莉安**。）*孩子，去把行装卸了吧。她看上去可不像今天刚从巴黎赶了路的人。

===Original===
Aubrey
I’ve never seen her with such a colour.
*Taking **Ellean’s** hands.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
我从没见她气色这么好过。
*（握住**埃莉安**的双手。）*

===Original===
Ellean
*To **Aubrey**, in a faint voice.* Papa, Mrs. Cortelyon has been so very, very kind to me, but I﻿—I have come home.
*She goes out.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（对**奥布里**，声音轻得几乎听不见。）*爸爸，科特利恩夫人待我太好、太好了，可我——我回家了。
*（她下。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
Come home! *To **Mrs. Cortelyon**.* Ellean returns to us, then?
Mrs. Cortelyon
That’s the very point I put to you in my letters, and you oblige me to travel from Paris to Willowmere on a warm day to settle it. I think perhaps it’s right that Ellean should be with you just now, although I﻿—My dear friend, circumstances are a little altered.

===Chinese===
奥布里
回家了！*（对**科特利恩夫人**。）*这么说，埃莉安要回到我们身边了？
科特利恩夫人
这正是我在信里向你提出的问题，而你逼得我在大热天从巴黎赶到威洛米尔来解决它。我想，眼下埃莉安回到你身边，也许是对的，尽管我——我亲爱的朋友，情形有点变化了。

===Original===
Aubrey
Alice, you’re in some trouble.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Well﻿—yes, I *am* in trouble. You remember pretty little Mrs. Brereton who was once Caroline Ardale?

===Chinese===
奥布里
爱丽丝，你遇到什么难处了。
科特利恩夫人
唉——是的，我*确实*遇到难处了。你记得那位当年叫卡罗琳·阿代尔（Caroline Ardale）的漂亮小巧的布里尔顿夫人（Mrs. Brereton）吗？

===Original===
Aubrey
Quite well.
Mrs. Cortelyon
She’s a widow now, poor thing. She has the entresol of the house where we’ve been lodging in the Avenue de Friedland. Caroline’s a dear chum of mine; she formed a great liking for Ellean.

===Chinese===
奥布里
记得很清楚。
科特利恩夫人
她如今守寡了，可怜的人。我们住在弗里德兰大道（Avenue de Friedland），她的套房就在我们寄住那幢房子的夹层。卡罗琳是我的密友；她一下子就喜欢上埃莉安了。

===Original===
Aubrey
I’m very glad.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Yes, it’s nice for her to meet her mother’s friends. Er﻿—that young Hugh Ardale the papers were full of some time ago﻿—he’s Caroline Brereton’s brother, you know.

===Chinese===
奥布里
我很高兴。
科特利恩夫人
是啊，让她结识她母亲的朋友是件好事。呃——前一阵子报纸上登满了的那个年轻的休·阿代尔（Hugh Ardale）——他是卡罗琳·布里尔顿的弟弟，你知道吧。

===Original===
Aubrey
No, I didn’t know. What did he do? I forget.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Checked one of those horrid mutinies at some faraway station in India, marched down with a handful of his men and a few faithful natives, and held the place until he was relieved. They gave him his company and a V.C. for it.

===Chinese===
奥布里
不知道。他做了什么来着？我忘了。
科特利恩夫人
在印度某个遥远的驿站平定了那些可怕的兵变中的一场，带着他手下寥寥几个人和几个忠心的当地人一路进军，死守那地方直到援兵到来。他们为此给了他连级军衔，还有一枚维多利亚十字勋章（V.C.）。

===Original===
Aubrey
And he’s Mrs. Brereton’s brother?
Mrs. Cortelyon
Yes. He’s with his sister﻿—*was*, rather﻿—in Paris. He’s home﻿—invalided. Good gracious, Aubrey, why don’t you help me out? Can’t you guess what has occurred?

===Chinese===
奥布里
他就是布里尔顿夫人的弟弟？
科特利恩夫人
是。他跟他姐姐在一起——不如说*曾经*在一起——在巴黎。他回国了——是伤病退伍。天哪，奥布里，你怎么不帮我接下去？难道猜不出出了什么事？

===Original===
Aubrey
Alice!
Mrs. Cortelyon
Young Ardale﻿—Ellean!

===Chinese===
奥布里
爱丽丝！
科特利恩夫人
小阿代尔——埃莉安！

===Original===
Aubrey
An attachment?
Mrs. Cortelyon
Yes, Aubrey. *After a little pause.* Well, I suppose I’ve got myself into sad disgrace. But really I didn’t foresee anything of this kind. A serious, reserved child like Ellean, and a boyish, high-spirited soldier﻿—it never struck me as being likely. ***Aubrey** paces to and fro thoughtfully.* I did all I could directly Captain Ardale spoke﻿—wrote to you at once. Why on earth don’t you receive your letters promptly, and when you do get them why can’t you open them? I endured the anxiety till last night, and then made up my mind﻿—home! Of course, it has worried me terribly. My head’s bursting. Are there any salts about? ***Aubrey** fetches a bottle from the cabinet and hands it to her.* We’ve had one of those hateful smooth crossings that won’t let you be properly indisposed.

===Chinese===
奥布里
两人有了情意？
科特利恩夫人
是的，奥布里。*（略停。）*唉，我算是把自己弄进丢脸的境地了。可我实在没料到会出这种事。像埃莉安这样庄重、矜持的孩子，和一个孩子气、血气旺的军人——我压根没觉得有这个可能。*（**奥布里**若有所思地踱来踱去。）*阿代尔上尉一开口，我就竭力处置——立刻给你写了信。你究竟为什么不及时收信，收到了又为什么不拆？我熬到昨晚，实在熬不住了，拿定主意——回家！当然，这事把我急坏了。我的头都要裂了。这儿有嗅盐吗？*（**奥布里**从柜子里取来一个小瓶递给她。）*我们这一趟赶上的是那种可恨的风平浪静的渡海——连好好晕一场船都不让。

===Original===
Aubrey
My dear Alice, I assure you I’ve no thought of blaming you.
Mrs. Cortelyon
That statement always precedes a quarrel.

===Chinese===
奥布里
亲爱的爱丽丝，我向你保证，我丝毫没有怪你的意思。
科特利恩夫人
这种话后面跟着的准是一场争吵。

===Original===
Aubrey
I don’t know whether this is the worst or the best luck. How will my wife regard it? Is Captain Ardale a good fellow?
Mrs. Cortelyon
My dear Aubrey, you’d better read up the accounts of his wonderful heroism. Face to face with death for a whole week; always with a smile and a cheering word for the poor helpless souls depending on him! Of course, it’s that that has stirred the depths of your child’s nature. I’ve watched her while we’ve been dragging the story out of him, and if angels look different from Ellean at that moment, I don’t desire to meet any, that’s all!

===Chinese===
奥布里
我不知道这算最坏还是最好的运气。我太太会怎么看？阿代尔上尉是个正派青年吗？
科特利恩夫人
亲爱的奥布里，你最好去读读那些报道他非凡英勇事迹的文章。整整一个星期直面死亡；对那些仰赖他的可怜无助的人，脸上总带着笑，口里总有鼓劲的话！当然，正是这个，搅动了你孩子天性深处的东西。我们一点点把那故事从他嘴里掏出来的时候，我一直望着她；那一刻，若是天使的模样与埃莉安有所不同，那我宁愿一个天使也不见，就这么回事！

===Original===
Aubrey
If you were in my position﻿—? But you can’t judge.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Why, if I had a marriageable daughter of my own and Captain Ardale proposed for her, naturally I should cry my eyes out all night﻿—but I should thank Heaven in the morning.

===Chinese===
奥布里
倘若你处在我这个位置——？可你没法判断。
科特利恩夫人
咳，倘若我自己有个待嫁的女儿，阿代尔上尉来求婚，我自然要哭肿了眼睛哭一整夜——可第二天一早，我就要感谢老天爷了。

===Original===
Aubrey
You believe so thoroughly in him?
Mrs. Cortelyon
Do you think I should have only a headache at this minute if I didn’t! Look here, you’ve got to see me down the lane; that’s the least you can do, my friend. Come into my house for a moment and shake hands with Hugh.

===Chinese===
奥布里
你就这么信得过他？
科特利恩夫人
要是信不过，你以为我此刻只犯个头疼就完了！听我说，你得陪我走下那条小径；这是你起码能做的，我的朋友。到我宅子里坐一会儿，跟休握握手吧。

===Original===
Aubrey
What, is he here?
Mrs. Cortelyon
He came through with us, to present himself formally tomorrow. Where are my gloves? ***Aubrey** fetches them from the ottoman.* Make my apologies to Mrs. Tanqueray, please. She’s well, I hope? *Going towards the door.* I can’t feel sorry she hasn’t seen me in this condition.
***Ellean** enters.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
什么，他在这儿？
科特利恩夫人
他跟我们一路来的，明天要正式登门拜见。我的手套哪儿去了？*（**奥布里**从软榻上取来手套。）*请代我向坦奎雷夫人致歉。她身子好吧，但愿？*（向门口走去。）*她没见着我这副模样，我倒不觉得遗憾。
*（**埃莉安**上。）*

===Original===
Ellean
*To **Mrs. Cortelyon**.* I’ve been waiting to wish you good night. I was afraid I’d missed you.
Mrs. Cortelyon
Good night, Ellean.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（对**科特利恩夫人**。）*我一直等着向您道晚安呢。还怕错过了您。
科特利恩夫人
晚安，埃莉安。

===Original===
Ellean
*In a low voice, embracing **Mrs. Cortelyon**.* I can’t thank you. Dear Mrs. Cortelyon!
Mrs. Cortelyon
*Her arms round **Ellean**, in a whisper to **Aubrey**.* Speak a word to her.
***Mrs. Cortelyon** goes out.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（低声，拥住**科特利恩夫人**。）*我怎么谢您也谢不尽。亲爱的科特利恩夫人！
科特利恩夫人
*（搂着**埃莉安**，悄声对**奥布里**。）*你去跟她说句话。
*（**科特利恩夫人**下。）*

===Original===
Aubrey
*To **Ellean**.* Ellean, I’m going to see Mrs. Cortelyon home. Tell Paula where I am; explain, dear.
*Going to the door.*

===Chinese===
奥布里
*（对**埃莉安**。）*埃莉安，我要送科特利恩夫人回去。告诉宝拉我在哪儿；跟她解释一下，亲爱的。
*（向门口走去。）*

===Original===
Ellean
*Her head drooping.* Yes. *Quickly.* Father! You are angry with me﻿—disappointed?
Aubrey
Angry?﻿—no.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（低垂着头。）*好的。*（急切地。）*爸爸！您生我的气了——失望了？
奥布里
生气？——没有。

===Original===
Ellean
Disappointed?
Aubrey
*Smiling and going to her and taking her hand.* If so, it’s only because you’ve shaken my belief in my discernment. I thought you took after your poor mother a little, Ellean; but there’s a look on your face tonight, dear, that I never saw on hers﻿—never, never.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
失望了？
奥布里
*（微笑着走到她跟前，握住她的手。）*若说有，那也只因你动摇了我对自己眼力的信心。我原以为你有一点像你可怜的母亲，埃莉安；可今晚你脸上有种神色，亲爱的，我在她脸上从没见过——从没有过，从没有过。

===Original===
Ellean
*Leaning her head on his shoulder.* Perhaps I ought not to have gone away?
Aubrey
Hush! You’re quite happy?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（把头靠在他肩上。）*也许我本不该离开家？
奥布里
嘘！你现在很幸福？

===Original===
Ellean
Yes.
Aubrey
That’s right. Then, as you are quite happy there is something I particularly want you to do for me Ellean.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
是的。
奥布里
那就好。既然你很幸福，那我有件事特别要请你为我做，埃莉安。

===Original===
Ellean
What is that?
Aubrey
Be very gentle with Paula. Will you?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
什么事？
奥布里
温柔地待宝拉。好不好？

===Original===
Ellean
You think I have been unkind.
Aubrey
*Kissing her upon the forehead.* Be very gentle with Paula.
*He goes out and she stands looking after him, then, as she turns thoughtfully from the door, a rose is thrown through the window and falls at her feet. She picks up the flower wonderingly and goes to the window.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
您觉得我一直刻薄。
奥布里
*（吻她的额头。）*温柔地待宝拉。
*（他下。她站在原地望着他的背影；随后她若有所思地从门边转过身来，一朵玫瑰花从窗外抛进来，落在她脚边。她诧异地拾起花，走到窗前。）*

===Original===
Ellean
*Starting back.* Hugh!
***Hugh Ardale**, a handsome young man of about seven-and-twenty, with a boyish face and manner, appears outside the window.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（惊退。）*休！
*（**休·阿代尔**出现在窗外；他是个二十七岁上下英俊的青年，面貌和举止都带着孩子气。）*

===Original===
Hugh
Nelly! Nelly dear!
Ellean
What’s the matter?

===Chinese===
休
奈莉（Nelly）！亲爱的奈莉！
埃莉安
怎么了？

===Original===
Hugh
Hush! Nothing. It’s only fun. *Laughing.* Ha, ha, ha! I’ve found out that Mrs. Cortelyon’s meadow runs up to your father’s plantation; I’ve come through a gap in the hedge.
Ellean
Why, Hugh?

===Chinese===
休
嘘！没什么。闹着玩罢了。*（笑。）*哈，哈，哈！我发现科特利恩夫人的草场一直连到你父亲的林子；我是从树篱的一个豁口钻过来的。
埃莉安
为什么，休？

===Original===
Hugh
I’m miserable at The Warren; it’s so different from the Avenue de Friedland. Don’t look like that! Upon my word I meant just to peep at your home and go back, but I saw figures moving about here, and came nearer, hoping to get a glimpse of you. Was that your father?
*Entering the room.*

===Chinese===
休
我在沃伦宅（The Warren）里闷得慌；那儿跟弗里德兰大道太不一样了。别那样看我！我以名誉担保，本来只想望一眼你的家就回去的，可我看见这儿有人影走动，就凑近了些，盼能瞧你一眼。刚才是你父亲吗？
*（走进房间。）*

===Original===
Ellean
Yes.
Hugh
Isn’t this fun! A rabbit ran across my foot while I was hiding behind that old yew.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
是的。
休
这多有意思！我躲在那棵老紫杉后面时，一只兔子从我脚上蹿过去。

===Original===
Ellean
You must go away; it’s not right for you to be here like this.
Hugh
But it’s only fun, I tell you. You take everything so seriously. Do wish me good night.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
你必须走；你这样待在这儿不合适。
休
可我告诉你，这只是闹着玩。你把什么都看得那么重。就跟我说声晚安吧。

===Original===
Ellean
We have said good night.
Hugh
In the hall at The Warren before Mrs. Cortelyon and a manservant. Oh, it’s so different from the Avenue de Friedland!

===Chinese===
埃莉安
我们已经道过晚安了。
休
在沃伦宅的门厅里，当着科特利恩夫人和一个男仆的面。哦，这跟弗里德兰大道多不一样啊！

===Original===
Ellean
*Giving him her hand hastily.* Good night, Hugh.
Hugh
Is that all? We might be the merest acquaintances.
*He momentarily embraces her, but she releases herself.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
*（匆匆把手伸给他。）*晚安，休。
休
就这样？我们简直像最泛泛的点头之交。
*（他短暂地拥抱她，她却挣脱开了。）*

===Original===
Ellean
It’s when you’re like this that you make me feel utterly miserable. *Throwing the rose from her angrily.* Oh!
Hugh
I’ve offended you now, I suppose?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
就是你这个样子，叫我难受得要命。*（气恼地把那朵玫瑰扔掉。）*哦！
休
我这下惹恼你了，是吧？

===Original===
Ellean
Yes.
Hugh
Forgive me, Nelly. Come into the garden for five minutes; we’ll stroll down to the plantation.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
是的。
休
原谅我，奈莉。到花园里来五分钟；我们散步到林子那儿去。

===Original===
Ellean
No, no.
Hugh
For two minutes﻿—to tell me you forgive me.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
不，不。
休
就两分钟——告诉我你原谅我了。

===Original===
Ellean
I forgive you.
Hugh
Evidently. I shan’t sleep a wink tonight after this. What a fool I am! Come down to the plantation. Make it up with me.

===Chinese===
埃莉安
我原谅你。
休
听得出是真心的。经你这么一说，今晚我更一眨眼也睡不着了。我真是个傻瓜！到林子那边来吧。跟我讲和。

===Original===
Ellean
There is somebody coming into this room. Do you wish to be seen here?
Hugh
I shall wait for you behind that yew tree. You must speak to me. Nelly!
*He disappears. **Paula** enters.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
有人正朝这屋里来。你想被人看见在这儿吗？
休
我就在那棵紫杉后面等你。你一定要来跟我说句话。奈莉！
*（他隐去。**宝拉**上。）*

===Original===
Paula
Ellean!
Ellean
You﻿—you are very surprised to see me, Paula, of course.

===Chinese===
宝拉
埃莉安！
埃莉安
你——你见到我一定很意外，宝拉，当然啦。

===Original===
Paula
Why are you here? Why aren’t you with﻿—your friend?
Ellean
I’ve come home﻿—if you’ll have me. We left Paris this morning; Mrs. Cortelyon brought me back. She was here a minute or two ago; papa has just gone with her to The Warren. He asked me to tell you.

===Chinese===
宝拉
你怎么在这儿？怎么不陪着——你的朋友？
埃莉安
我回家了——只要你们肯收留我。我们今早离开巴黎；科特利恩夫人把我送回来的。一两分钟前她还在屋里；爸爸刚陪她去沃伦宅了。他要我告诉你一声。

===Original===
Paula
There are some people staying with us that I’d rather you didn’t meet. It was hardly worth your while to return for a few hours.
Ellean
A few hours?

===Chinese===
宝拉
家里正住着几位客人，我宁可你别碰上。为几个小时赶回来，未免不值得。
埃莉安
几个小时？

===Original===
Paula
Well, when do you go to London?
Ellean
I don’t think I go to London, after all.

===Chinese===
宝拉
那么，你几时去伦敦？
埃莉安
我看我不去伦敦了，说到底。

===Original===
Paula
*Eagerly.* You﻿—you’ve quarrelled with her?
Ellean
No, no, no, not that; but﻿—Paula! *In an altered tone.* Paula.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（急切地。）*你——你跟她闹翻了？
埃莉安
不，不，不，不是那样；只是——宝拉！*（换了副声调。）*宝拉。

===Original===
Paula
*Startled.* Eh? ***Ellean** goes deliberately to **Paula** and kisses her.* Ellean!
Ellean
Kiss me.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（一惊。）*嗯？*（**埃莉安**郑重地走到**宝拉**跟前吻她。）*埃莉安！
埃莉安
吻我。

===Original===
Paula
What﻿—what’s come to you?
Ellean
I want to behave differently to you in the future. Is it too late?

===Chinese===
宝拉
你——你是怎么了？
埃莉安
往后我想换一副样子待你。还来得及吗？

===Original===
Paula
Too﻿—late! *Impulsively kissing **Ellean** and crying.* No﻿—no﻿—no! No﻿—no!
Ellean
Paula, don’t cry.

===Chinese===
宝拉
来不——来得及！*（冲动地吻**埃莉安**，哭起来。）*不——不——不！不——不！
埃莉安
宝拉，别哭。

===Original===
Paula
*Wiping her eyes.* I’m a little shaky; I haven’t been sleeping. It’s all right﻿—talk to me.
Ellean
There is something I want to tell you﻿—

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（擦眼睛。）*我有点发虚；一直没睡好。没事了——跟我说话吧。
埃莉安
有件事我要告诉你——

===Original===
Paula
Is there﻿—is there?
*They sit together on the ottoman, **Paula** taking **Ellean’s** hand.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
是吗——是吗？
*（两人一同坐在软榻上，**宝拉**握着**埃莉安**的手。）*

===Original===
Ellean
Paula, in our house in the Avenue de Friedland, on the floor below us, there was a Mrs. Brereton. She used to be a friend of my mother’s. Mrs. Cortelyon and I spent a great deal of our time with her.
Paula
*Suspiciously.* Oh! *Letting **Ellean’s** hand fall.* Is this lady going to take you up in place of Mrs. Cortelyon?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
宝拉，在我们弗里德兰大道的住处，我们楼下那一层，住着一位布里尔顿夫人。她从前是我母亲的朋友。科特利恩夫人和我在她那儿度过了许多时光。
宝拉
*（起疑地。）*哦！*（放开**埃莉安**的手。）*这位女士是要接替科特利恩夫人，把你带在她身边了？

===Original===
Ellean
No, no. Her brother is staying with her﻿—*was* staying with her. Her brother﻿—
*Breaking off in confusion.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
不，不。她的弟弟住在她那儿——*先前*住在她那儿。她的弟弟——
*（慌乱地住了口。）*

===Original===
Paula
Well?
Ellean
*Almost inaudibly.* Paula﻿—
*She rises and walks away, **Paula** following her.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
怎么？
埃莉安
*（声音低得几乎听不见。）*宝拉——
*（她起身走开，**宝拉**跟上去。）*

===Original===
Paula
Ellean! *Taking hold of her.* You’re not in love!
***Ellean** looks at **Paula** appealingly.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
埃莉安！*（一把抓住她。）*你不会是恋爱了！
*（**埃莉安**恳求地望着**宝拉**。）*

===Original===
Paula
Oh! *You* in love! You! Oh, this is why you’ve come home! Of course, you can make friends with me now! You’ll leave us for good soon, I suppose; so it doesn’t much matter being civil to me for a little while!
Ellean
Oh, Paula!

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦！*你*恋爱了！你！哦，原来你为这个回家的！怪不得，如今你肯跟我交好了！你不久就要一去不回地离开我们了，我猜；那么眼下这几个钟头对我客气些，也就无关紧要了！
埃莉安
哦，宝拉！

===Original===
Paula
Why, how you have deceived us﻿—all of us! We’ve taken you for a cold-blooded little saint. The fools you’ve made of us! Saint Ellean! Saint Ellean!
Ellean
Ah, I might have known you’d only mock me!

===Chinese===
宝拉
咦，你把我们骗得好苦——我们全都叫你骗了！我们一直把你当成个冷血的小圣人。我们成了多大的傻瓜！圣埃莉安！圣埃莉安！
埃莉安
啊，我本该知道你只会讥笑我！

===Original===
Paula
*Her tone changing.* Eh?
Ellean
I﻿—I can’t talk to you. *Sitting on the settee.* You do nothing else but mock and sneer, nothing else.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（口气变了。）*嗯？
埃莉安
我——我没法跟你说话。*（坐到长椅上。）*你除了讥笑和嘲讽什么也不会，什么也不会。

===Original===
Paula
Ellean dear! Ellean! I didn’t mean it. I’m so horribly jealous, it’s a sort of curse on me. *Kneeling beside **Ellean** and embracing her.* My tongue runs away with me. I’m going to alter, I swear I am. I’ve made some good resolutions, and, as God’s above me, I’ll keep them! If you are in love, if you do ever marry, that’s no reason why we shouldn’t be fond of each other. Come, you’ve kissed me of your own accord﻿—you can’t take it back. Now we’re friends again, aren’t we? Ellean dear! I want to know everything, everything. Ellean dear, Ellean!
Ellean
Paula, Hugh has done something that makes me very angry. He came with us from Paris today, to see papa. He is staying with Mrs. Cortelyon and﻿—I ought to tell you﻿—

===Chinese===
宝拉
亲爱的埃莉安！埃莉安！我不是有意的。我这妒忌心太可怕了，像一道缠在我身上的咒。*（跪在**埃莉安**身边搂住她。）*我的舌头总背着我说胡话。我要改，我发誓一定改。我下过一些好好的决心，上帝在上，我一定守住！你若是恋爱了，将来真要出嫁，那也不是我们彼此不能相亲的理由。来，你已经主动吻过我了——收不回去了。这下我们又成朋友了，对不对？亲爱的埃莉安！我要知道一切，一切。亲爱的埃莉安，埃莉安！
埃莉安
宝拉，休做了件事，叫我很生气。他今天跟我们一道从巴黎来，要见爸爸。他住在科特利恩夫人家，还有——我应当告诉你——

===Original===
Paula
Yes, yes. What?
Ellean
He has found his way by The Warren meadow through the plantation up to this house. He is waiting to bid me good night. *Glancing towards the garden.* He is﻿—out there.

===Chinese===
宝拉
好的，好的。什么事？
埃莉安
他顺着沃伦宅的草场、穿过林子一路寻到这所房子来了。他正等着向我道晚安。*（朝花园望了一眼。）*他——就在外头。

===Original===
Paula
Oh!
Ellean
What shall I do?

===Chinese===
宝拉
哦！
埃莉安
我该怎么办？

===Original===
Paula
Bring him in to see me! Will you?
Ellean
No, no.

===Chinese===
宝拉
领他进来见见我！好不好？
埃莉安
不，不。

===Original===
Paula
But I’m dying to know him. Oh, yes, you must. I shall meet him before Aubrey does. *Excitedly running her hands over her hair.* I’m so glad. ***Ellean** goes out by the window.* The mirror﻿—mirror. What a fright I must look! *Not finding the hand-glass on the table, she jumps on to the settee, and surveys herself in the mirror over the mantelpiece, then sits quietly down and waits.* Ellean! Just fancy! Ellean!
*After a pause **Ellean** enters by the window with **Hugh**.*

===Chinese===
宝拉
可我渴望认识他呀。哦，一定得领来。我要赶在奥布里前头见着他。*（激动地抹了抹头发。）*我真高兴。*（**埃莉安**从窗下。）*镜子——镜子。我这模样一定吓人吧！*（桌上找不到手镜，她跳上长椅，对着壁炉架上方的镜子照了照，然后安静地坐下等候。）*埃莉安！想想看！埃莉安！
*（片刻之后，**埃莉安**与**休**从窗进屋。）*

===Original===
Ellean
Paula, this is Captain Ardale﻿—Mrs. Tanqueray.
***Paula** risen and turns, and she and **Hugh** stand staring blankly at each other for a moment or two; then **Paula** advances and gives him her hand.*

===Chinese===
埃莉安
宝拉，这位是阿代尔上尉——这位是坦奎雷夫人。
*（**宝拉**起身转过脸来；她和**休**面面相觑，怔了好一会儿；随后**宝拉**走上前，把手伸给他。）*

===Original===
Paula
*In a strange voice, but calmly.* How do you do?
Hugh
How do you do?

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（声音异样，却很平静。）*你好。
休
你好。

===Original===
Paula
*To **Ellean**.* Mr. Ardale and I have met in London, Ellean. Er﻿—Captain Ardale, now?
Hugh
Yes.

===Chinese===
宝拉
*（对**埃莉安**。）*阿代尔先生和我在伦敦见过，埃莉安。呃——如今是阿代尔上尉了？
休
是的。

===Original===
Ellean
In London?
Paula
They say the world’s very small, don’t they?

===Chinese===
埃莉安
在伦敦？
宝拉
人们都说世界很小，是不是？

===Original===
Hugh
Yes.
Paula
Ellean, dear, I want to have a little talk about you to Mr. Ardale﻿—Captain Ardale﻿—alone. *Putting her arms round **Ellean**, and leading her to the door.* Come back in a little while. ***Ellean** nods to **Paula** with a smile and goes out, while **Paula** stands watching her at the open door.* In a little while﻿—in a little﻿—*Closing the door and then taking a seat facing **Hugh**.* Be quick! Mr. Tanqueray has only gone down to The Warren with Mrs. Cortelyon. What is to be done?

===Chinese===
休
是的。
宝拉
亲爱的埃莉安，我想同阿代尔先生——阿代尔上尉——单独说几句关于你的话。*（搂着**埃莉安**，把她送到门口。）*过一小会儿再回来。*（**埃莉安**含笑对**宝拉**点点头，下；**宝拉**站在敞开的门口目送她。）*一小会儿——一小——*（关上门，然后在**休**对面坐下。）*快点！坦奎雷先生只是陪科特利恩夫人去沃伦宅了。现在该怎么办？

===Original===
Hugh
*Blankly.* Done?
Paula
Done﻿—done. Something must be done.

===Chinese===
休
*（茫然地。）*办什么？
宝拉
办——办。总得有个办法。

===Original===
Hugh
I understood that Mr. Tanqueray had married a Mrs.﻿—Mrs.﻿—
Paula
Jarman?

===Chinese===
休
我听说坦奎雷先生娶的是一位——一位——
宝拉
贾曼夫人？

===Original===
Hugh
Yes.
Paula
I’d been going by that name. You didn’t follow my doings after we separated.

===Chinese===
休
对。
宝拉
我先前用的是那个名字。我们分手以后，你并没有留意我的行踪。

===Original===
Hugh
No.
Paula
*Sneeringly.* No.

===Chinese===
休
没有。
宝拉
*（冷笑。）*是没有。

===Original===
Hugh
I went out to India.
Paula
What’s to be done?

===Chinese===
休
我去了印度。
宝拉
现在该怎么办？

===Original===
Hugh
Damn this chance!
Paula
Oh, my God!

===Chinese===
休
该死的巧遇！
宝拉
哦，我的上帝！

===Original===
Hugh
Your husband doesn’t know, does he?
Paula
That you and I﻿—?

===Chinese===
休
你丈夫不知道，对吧？
宝拉
你和我——？

===Original===
Hugh
Yes.
Paula
No. He knows about others.

===Chinese===
休
对。
宝拉
不知道。别人他知道。

===Original===
Hugh
Not about me. How long were we﻿—?
Paula
I don’t remember, exactly.

===Chinese===
休
却不知道我。我们那时候——有多久？
宝拉
记不清了，说不准。

===Original===
Hugh
Do you﻿—do you think it matters?
Paula
His﻿—his daughter. *With a muttered exclamation he turns away and sits with his head in his hands.* What’s to be done?

===Chinese===
休
你——你觉得要紧吗？
宝拉
他的——他的女儿。*（他低低咒骂了一声，转过身去，双手抱头坐下。）*现在该怎么办？

===Original===
Hugh
I wish I could think.
Paula
Oh! Oh! What happened to that flat of ours in Ethelbert Street?

===Chinese===
休
但愿我能想出个法子。
宝拉
哦！哦！咱们在埃塞尔伯特街（Ethelbert Street）的那套房间后来怎么样了？

===Original===
Hugh
I let it.
Paula
All that pretty furniture?

===Chinese===
休
我把它租出去了。
宝拉
那些漂亮的家具呢？

===Original===
Hugh
Sold it.
Paula
I came across the key of the escritoire the other day in an old purse! *Suddenly realising the horror and hopelessness of her position, and starting to her feet with an hysterical cry of rage.* What am I maundering about?

===Chinese===
休
卖掉了。
宝拉
前几天我在一只旧钱袋里翻到了那口书柜写字台的钥匙！*（猛然意识到自己处境的可怕与无望，狂怒地一声喊，霍地站起来。）*我在这儿絮叨些什么呀？

===Original===
Hugh
For God’s sake, be quiet! Do let me think.
Paula
This will send me mad! *Suddenly turning and standing over him.* You﻿—you beast, to crop up in my life again like this!

===Chinese===
休
看在上帝的份上，安静点！让我想想。
宝拉
这要把我逼疯了！*（猛地转身，居高临下站在他面前。）*你——你这畜生，竟这样又冒回我的生活里来！

===Original===
Hugh
I always treated you fairly.
Paula
*Weakly.* Oh! I beg your pardon﻿—I know you did﻿—I﻿—
*She sinks on to the settee, crying hysterically.*

===Chinese===
休
我一向待你不薄。
